  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (rule 4):** `N` {5,10,15,20,25,30} x `GROSS` 17 rungs 0.20..1.00

  incumbent's construction: H = 126 min hold, weekly, t+1, 10 bps, above-200d + vol20 < 0.60,

  (OOS 14.95% / 1.1833).

  **(A) THE ANSWER.** The gross that maximises J = min(DD margin, CAGR margin) **moves with N**
  in sample on all three panels: U56 **0.40 / 0.50 / 0.60 / 0.65 / 0.65 / 0.65** at N = 5..30
  (spread 0.25, Spearman **+0.941**), B136 0.35 -> 0.65 (**+0.986**), SMALL 0.30 -> 0.45
  (**+0.971**). The shape rule was fixed before the run (RIDGE if spread <= 0.05; DIAGONAL if
  spread >= 0.15 and |rho| >= 0.80); all three IS windows classify DIAGONAL. So idea 1290's
  g* = 0.65 is a fact about **N = 20**, not about the family.

  **(B) WHAT A TRANSPLANTED GROSS COSTS.** Freezing gross at g*(20) across the other five
  breadths costs a mean **-1.98 pp** of J_IS (worst -8.66 pp); freezing it at the certified 0.65
  costs **-5.10 pp** (worst -15.77 pp, on SMALL, which is 7 rungs away from its own argmax).
  4b passes **50 of 306** cells (U56 25, B136 25, **SMALL 0**); **4a passes 0 of 306**; among the
  256 failures MaxDD is the sole binder on 70 and the CAGR floor on 67 — the two legs gross
  trades against each other, exactly as 1292 found.

  **(C) RULE 8 REACHES A BETTER-CUSHIONED BOOK IN THE SAME FAMILY.** Both dials chosen on
  warm-up..2016-12-31 only (IS 4b-analogue legs, then highest IS J, ties to lower gross then lower
  N), 2017-2026 read ONCE. U56 picks **N = 15 / g = 0.60**: full **13.67% / 1.1712 / -16.38%**,
  halves 1.2596 / 1.1154, OOS **15.14% / 1.1952 / -16.38%** against SPY 15.28% / 0.8745 / -33.72%

  from 1292 and not re-tuned) runs it through the 15-point phase x delay ensemble: **15 of 15**,

  **(A) THE ANSWER.** H moves the leg that binds this whole family by **more than the timing noise
  the record already accepts**. Per cell the H ladder's dd_margin range is scored against that
  cell's OWN 15-point ensemble range at H=126 (a conservative yardstick: 15 points against 8 rungs):
  ratios **5.80 / 5.84 / 2.54 / 2.51 / 2.20 / 2.20**, i.e. DD-MATERIAL in **6 of 6** cells, median
  range **6.46 pp** against **2.36 pp**. Stress-robust-4b status changes across the ladder in all
  six cells. So the pre-declared (A) fires, and (B) "turnover only" is refuted on its own terms.

  **(B) THE TWO AXES DO NOT BEHAVE ALIKE.** Turnover is *perfectly* monotone in H (rho **-1.000**
  in 6 of 6, range 8.09 turns/yr = 74x-129x the ensemble's 0.09). Drawdown is **not** (rho median
  **-0.595**): margins run +4.37 / +4.12 / +2.56 / +0.22 / **-2.20** / +3.50 / +1.17 / +1.07 pp
  over H = 0..252 at U56 N=20 g=0.65. H=63 is the trough at every cell and is stress-robust at
  **0 of 6**; longer holding buys DD margin only up to a point and then loses it again.

  — **ANSWERED = NO ON THE LITERAL QUESTION, (B) CAP-SENSITIVE ON THE VERDICT. THE BOOK IS

  Verdict KILL (capital): no book promoted, no RULES change, no PROTOCOL edit (rule 6);

  two ideas filed under this sprint's documented fallback (see the 1287 entry below), claimed
  as the LAST of the three. Runtime 10.9s, offline, deterministic.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (rule 4):** `GROUP CAP` {2, 3, 5, 8, 20} x `N` {10, 20} = 10 cells
  per panel, **30 published**. c = 20 is no cap and reproduces the incumbent bit for bit. The
  GROUPING is construction, not a dial, and it is POINT-IN-TIME: each name is assigned to
  whichever of nine sector ETFs (XLK/XLF/XLV/XLE/XLI/XLY/XLP/XLU/XLB) its returns correlate with
  most over THE FIRST 252 TRADING DAYS OF ITS OWN HISTORY, and is cap-exempt until then, so no
  assignment uses data from after the day it first applies. XLRE and XLC are excluded for having
  no history at the start of the tape — declared, not chosen on a result. universe.json's own
  four labels are re-run on U56 N=20 as a declared cross-check (4b passes 2 of 5 caps there).
  FROZEN: H=126, gross 0.75, weekly, 10 bps, t+1, above-200d + vol20 < 0.60, 260-row warm-up.

  **(A) THE CENSUS — THE LITERAL QUESTION.** Over the uncapped incumbent's **87,969 held
  name-days**, the largest group is **XLK at 0.382** and the Herfindahl over ten groups is
  **0.2147** against 0.1000 for an even split. B136: XLK 0.328, HHI 0.1838. SMALL: XLY 0.255,
  HHI 0.1534. So the book is roughly twice as concentrated as an even split and tilted to
  technology — but it is **not** a one-group bet, and the answer to the queue's literal question
  is NO.

  **(B) THE FINDING THAT MATTERS, AND IT CORROBORATES 1287 DOWN AN UNRELATED AXIS.** Capping
  sector exposure **flips the 4b verdict**: U56 N=20 clears 4b uncapped and at c=8 and **FAILS at
  c=2, 3 and 5**. At every one of those failures `b_h1`, `b_h2`, `b_oos` and `b_cagr` are **Y** and
  the binding leg is the **MaxDD cap ALONE**. DD margin against the -20.23% bar, by cap:
  **-1.06 / -1.34 / -1.53 / +0.67 / +1.10 pp**. Most sharply: **c = 5 is Sharpe-DOMINANT over the
  incumbent** — 1.2115 vs 1.1529 full, 1.2258 vs 1.1837 OOS, CAGR 15.91% vs 15.79% — **and 4b
  rejects it on drawdown alone.** Idea 1287 found the same knife-edge by perturbing execution lag;
  this run reaches it by perturbing sector concentration. Two unrelated axes, one binding leg.

  **(C) ONE PARKED BOOK, STATED WITH WHAT DISQUALIFIES IT.** `U56 / N=10 / H=126 / gross 0.75 /
  weekly / SECTOR CAP 3` reads **17.35% / 1.2109 / -18.26%**, halves **1.2930 / 1.1412**, OOS
  **18.47% / 1.2446** — better than the standing incumbent on **every** 4b leg, and 4b PASSES.
  It is **PARK, not KEEP.** With N held fixed the IS cap chooser does land on c=3 (IS 1.1682, OOS
  1.2446, +0.1486 over the uncapped anchor, 4b Y) — but **N is a dial too**, and the STRICT joint
  (N, cap) IS chooser lands on **N=20 / c=5**, which fails 4b. Rule 8 does not certify a cell you
  can only reach by fixing a dial after the fact.

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS.** Both readings published. N-fixed: beats the uncapped anchor OOS
  in **4 of 6** (panel, N) pairs, mean delta **+0.0560**, clears 4b in **1 of 6** against the
  anchor's 2. STRICT joint: U56 -> N=20/c=5 (OOS 1.2258, +0.0421), B136 -> N=10/c=3 (1.0172,
  -0.0073), SMALL -> N=20/c=2 (0.5212, +0.0670); beats the anchor in 2 of 3 and **clears 4b in
  0 of 3**. Grid-wide **4a 0 of 30, 4b 7 of 30** (U56 5/10, B136 2/10, SMALL 0/10).
  **NOTHING ENACTED.**

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56, B136 and SMALL (663 investable, 52 dropped for max_1d_move
  >= 1.0 in data/small_meta.csv) are all CURRENT-constituent lists. This flatters the UNCAPPED
  book most, because the corner the screen concentrates into is the corner whose survivors are
  known in advance — which is a reason to read (C)'s capped book charitably and the incumbent's
  uncapped 4b pass sceptically, not the other way round.

  price-only stress tests of the standing KEEP-4b book, and the first (1287) claimed. Runtime
  9.9s, offline, deterministic.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (rule 4):** `LAG` {1, 2, 3, 5, 10} trading days x `CADENCE` {W, M}
  = 10 cells per panel, **30 published**, none dropped. LAG = d means rank on the close of row
  (t-d) and hold from the close of row t; **d = 1 IS PROTOCOL rule 2** and reproduces the
  committed incumbent to 1e-4. NOT dials, reported at every value: PANEL {U56, B136, SMALL}
  (rule 9); the 4a and 4b legs; full sample, both halves, and the rule-8 OOS window. FROZEN at
  the incumbent's construction: N=20, H=126, gross 0.75, 3-leg composite (21/252, 0/126, 0/63),
  above-200d + vol20 < 0.60, 10 bps, 260-row warm-up.

  **(A) THE ANSWER.** The incumbent's own cell (U56, W, d=1) reads **15.79% / 1.1529 / -19.13%,
  halves 1.2132 / 1.1133, OOS 17.30% / 1.1837** against U56 SPY **15.13% / 0.8848 / -33.72%**
  (halves 0.9600/0.8236, OOS 15.28% / 0.8745) and clears 4b. Move the ranking snapshot back ONE
  trading day and it **FAILS 4b**; it fails again at d=3 and d=10; it clears again at d=5. So the
  pass is not a monotone decay — it is a **coin-flip across lags**.

  **(B) WHY, AND THIS IS THE LOAD-BEARING PART.** Every one of the three failures binds on the
  **MaxDD cap ALONE**. 4b's three Sharpe legs (H1, H2, OOS) and its CAGR floor pass at **5 of 5**
  lags. Net Sharpe moves only **0.0181 (SD)** across the five rungs; the median Sharpe slope per
  day of delay over the 6 (panel, cadence) pairs is **+0.00131/day** (range -0.00584 to +0.01292)
  and **3 of 6 cells IMPROVE at d=2**, so the book is NOT harvesting one-day stale prices and its
  edge is delay-insensitive out to two trading weeks. **The 4b verdict is what is one day deep.**

  **(C) THE KNIFE-EDGE, PRICED.** U56 SPY MaxDD -33.72%, so 4b's cap is **-20.23%**. MaxDD by lag:
  **-19.13 / -21.57 / -20.50 / -19.77 / -20.91%**. The lag-1 margin is **+1.10 pp** against a
  **0.95 pp** SD of MaxDD across the five lags — the standing candidate sits **1.16 lag-SDs**
  inside a HARD cap, on the single noisiest statistic in the rule, and a perturbation that leaves
  Sharpe flat flips its verdict. Nothing here refutes the incumbent's 2026-09-04 reading; it says
  the reading is not resolved at the precision 4b's DD leg is being quoted to.

- 2026-09-18 (lane C, idea 1222 is-the-WIDEST-DIAL-HABIT-RESOLVABLE-on-ANY-ATTAINABLE-FOLD-COUNT)
  — **ANSWERED = YES ON THE RECORD'S OWN FOUR-LADDER SET, AND THE ENTIRE YES IS THE DEGENERATE GROSS
  LADDER.** Verdict KILL (capital). No RULES change, no book promoted, no PROTOCOL edit (rule 6);
  RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, engine.py, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched. SELECTION: this lane
  takes the SECOND open idea; 1222 was second in '## Open' (1283 first, lane A's) and is not EDGAR /
  Form 4 / 8-K / options / live-data. Price leg of 66 real rung books on three panels, 96 rule-8
  decisions and 96 stitched deployable curves, so it carries this run's mandatory walk-forward and
  both KEEP paths. 10 of 10 gates pass; runtime 14.1s, offline, deterministic.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (rule 4, and the queue names both):** `FOLD LENGTH L` {63, 126, 252,
  504} x `LADDER PAIR SET` {ALL4, NG} = **8 cells, EVERY ONE PUBLISHED**, over 108 (panel, pair, L)
  rows. alpha is NOT a third dial: 0.05 is the queue's and 0.10 is reported beside it everywhere.
  NOT dials, reported at every value: PANEL {U56, B136, SMALL} (rule 9); the record's four ladders
  N (6 rungs) / H (4) / GROSS (10) / CADENCE (2); all 6 unordered ALL4 pairs and all 3 NG pairs; the
  4a and 4b legs; IS and OOS. 10 bps, t+1, weekly, 260-row warm-up, anchor N=20 / H=126 / g=0.75 / W.

  **(0) THE ONE CONSTRUCTION CHANGE, DECLARED NOT BURIED.** 1214's fourteen folds are NESTED
  expanding windows, so its fourteen readings of a pair are fourteen readings of mostly the SAME data
  and cannot be averaged to buy precision. A required-precision calculation needs INDEPENDENT folds,
  so this run tiles the tape with DISJOINT consecutive folds of length L and makes L a dial. 1214's
  252-pair table is NOT reproduced by this script and is not claimed to be. The statistic is a
  difference of logs so fold readings ADD, and the band on their mean shrinks as 1/sqrt(F) — gated
  to 0.0100 at F = 1/4/16/64 (G1). **E[D] under H0 is NOT zero** (the log of an unbiased estimator is
  biased low): mu0 runs **-0.0699 to +0.3731** over the six pairs and is measured by Monte Carlo at
  1,000,000 draws, not assumed. Orientation is fixed ONCE on the full sample and held across every
  fold and cell (per-fold agreement with it: median 0.9143), so no conditioning correction is
  smuggled in.

  **(A) THE ANSWER, AND THE FACT THAT EMPTIES IT.** Headline cell (ALL4, L = 252, alpha = 0.05):
  **9 of 18 (panel, pair) cells resolve** at the F_max = 17 folds the tape supplies — exactly the
  0.50 the pre-declared outcome (A) required. Over all 8 cells **52 of 108** resolve. But **the 36
  GROSS pairs resolve at 36 of 36, at every panel and every fold length, |t| 6.11 to 41.34**: IS
  Sharpe is flat in gross (1189, reproduced), so that ladder's spread is ~0 at every fold and the log
  ratio against it is huge and stable. Strip it and the habit resolves at **8 of 36**, at **0 of 9**
  at the headline fold length, and the median non-GROSS pair needs **27.5 to 72 years** of tape.
  Best fold length per (panel, pair): **14 of 18** need no more tape than the 15.6-17.6 years in
  hand, **but only 5 of 9 once GROSS is dropped**, and those five need 6.5-13.2 of the years in hand.
  **1223's open complaint is now priced on the resolution axis too: the record's own comparison set
  manufactures its own resolvability.**

  **(B) TWO REQUIRED-PRECISION FACTS THE RECORD DID NOT HAVE.** (i) **SHORTER FOLDS WIN**: 13 cells
  resolve at L = 63 against 9 at L = 252, i.e. the sqrt(F) gain dominates the dispersion a short
  window adds, so a dial read off yearly folds leaves resolution on the table. (ii) **THE IID BAND IS
  TOO NARROW BY A MEASURED AMOUNT**: the empirical per-fold sd runs at a median **1.3879x** the
  iid-normal null's (range 0.4784-4.0366), so a band assumed rather than measured understates the
  required fold count by about **1.9x**. Every F* here is quoted on the tape's own dispersion; the
  iid F* sits beside it in `.foldcount.csv`.

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS — NOTHING MOVES, AND WHAT PASSES IS THE INCUMBENT.** Benchmarks:
  **U56 SPY 15.13% / 0.8849 / -33.72% (halves 0.9600/0.8236), OOS 15.28% / 0.8747; U56 RULES v2
  (live) @10 bps 8.62% / 1.2018 / -12.05%, OOS 1.2781; B136 SPY 15.16% / 0.8862, OOS 0.8769; B136
  LIVE 7.98% / 1.0994, OOS 1.1061; SMALL SPY 14.06% / 0.8582, OOS 0.8769; SMALL LIVE 4.64% / 0.7130,
  OOS 0.6518.** Chosen on warm-up..2016 ONLY, 2017-2026 read ONCE. **66 rung books: 4a 0; 4b full 17;
  4b OOS 16; BOTH 15. 96 rule-8 decisions: 0 clear 4a; 16 clear 4b and every one is ONE distinct
  book — the U56 anchor N=20 / H=126 / g=0.75 / W, 15.78% / 1.1522 / -19.13%, halves 1.2127/1.1128,
  OOS 17.28% / 1.1832 / -19.13%** — the standing 2026-09-04 incumbent, so **CONFIRMATORY, NOT
  GENERATIVE. NO NEW CANDIDATE, NO MEMO, NOTHING ENACTED.** Made deployable, the resolution test is
  the only chooser that does not lose — mean OOS Sharpe **CH_RES 0.9226 > CH_ANCHOR 0.8869 > CH_RAW =
  CH_POINT 0.8479**, CH_RES vs anchor +0.0357 with 4 wins / 0 losses / 20 ties — but under 1211's
  decision-row correction that collapses to **ONE distinct move** (SMALL, H = 252, +0.2143 of OOS
  Sharpe) counted four times across the (ladder set x L) cells, inflation factor 4, and the same
  deltas run t = +0.40 to +0.84 in the rolling walk. **THE LADDER-SET DIAL IS INERT ON PRICE:** ALL4
  and NG give bit-identical picks at all 48 rule-8 rows and all 672 rolling pick-cells, because the
  point-widest ladder and its runner-up are never GROSS.

  **CAVEATS.** Survivorship: universe.json / universe_broad.json are CURRENT constituents; the small
  panel is the current output of a sub-$2B screen — 715 rows at this commit, 51 drop for
  `max_1d_move >= 1.0`, 664 tradable remain (the label SMALL439 does not denote this pool, 1074 still
  open). F* is a point estimate built on an observed effect itself measured with error, so F* = 18
  against F_max = 17 is not meaningfully different from resolvable; the honest content is the ORDER
  of magnitude, and for the non-GROSS pairs that order is decades. The disjoint tiling drops the
  tape's remainder (up to L-1 rows) and treats folds as independent, which the 1.39x sd ratio shows
  they are not exactly. Benchmarks read ~0.07pp above 1214's committed figures throughout because the
  price caches were refreshed between the two runs, not because the construction changed.

- 2026-09-18 (lane B, idea 1115 does-the-PER-CELL-CALIBRATION-BAR-change-any-committed-DECISIVENESS-claim)
  — **ANSWERED: THE PER-CELL BAR IS STRICTLY STRICTER AND PERFECTLY ONE-SIDED, BUT AT THE RECORD'S OWN
  INCUMBENT BAR IT MOVES ALMOST NOTHING, AND IT BUYS NOTHING. KILL (capital) / PUBLISHING-ONLY
  RECOMMENDATION.**

  **WHY THIS IDEA.** 1115 was the LAST numbered item standing in QUEUE.md's '## Open' and was claimed
  without an eligibility skip: lane B's own 1265 correction applies (a census CAN carry a capital arm),
  and this run gives it one.

  **WHAT COULD AND COULD NOT BE DONE.** The committed decisiveness rows do not carry their return
  series in this repo, so they cannot be re-bootstrapped and NO committed verdict is re-adjudicated or
  claimed to flip. What is measured instead is the aggregate-vs-per-cell gap on the record's OWN four
  CORE ladders, rebuilt here as real books: 3 panels (U56 55 names, B136 135, SMALL 663) x 2 1101
  anchors x 4 ladders = 24 families, 162 books, 876 cells, circular block bootstrap L=63 x 1000 draws,
  10 bps, t+1 fill. Two dials only: CLAIM SET {CS_STEPS, CS_ENDS, CS_VSANCHOR, CS_VSBEST} x BAR q
  {0.50 ... 0.95}, all 24 cells published.

  **THE GAP IS REAL AND ONE-SIDED.** Over 576 verdict pairs: AGGREGATE decisive 325, PER-CELL 252,
  FLIPS 73 (0.1267) — and **73 of 73 flips run AGG-pass / PER-CELL-fail; the reverse direction is
  empty at every cell.** So the per-cell reading can only ever withdraw a committed decisive call,
  never add one. Flip rate by bar on CS_STEPS: 0.0417 / 0.4167 / 0.2917 / 0.1667 / 0.0417 / 0.0000 at
  q = 0.50 / 0.60 / 0.70 / 0.80 / 0.90 / 0.95.

  **BUT THE INCUMBENT BAR IS AT THE QUIET END.** At q=0.90, the record's own bar, only 3 of 96 families
  flip (0.0312); the gap peaks at q=0.60 (0.4167) where nothing is published. 1110's D2 headline
  (24 of 32 cells fail their own bar at q=0.80) is directionally right but family-specific: the same
  count over 24 families is 187 of 438 (0.4269), i.e. 1110's own family is ~1.8x the record-wide rate.
  Control: flip rate 0.1736 / 0.1597 / 0.1597 at L = 21 / 63 / 252, range 0.0139 — not a knob artefact.

  **THE RECORD CANNOT BE RE-SCORED FROM ITS TEXT EITHER.** Census of the 132 decisiveness-mentioning
  units in LEADERBOARD.md (85) and CHANGELOG.md (47): a reading is NAMED in 13 (0.0985) — per-cell 8,
  aggregate 5 — so **0.9015 name neither.** A k-of-m cell count appears in 78 (0.5909), a bar in 32
  (0.2424). The record's decisiveness language is mostly unscoreable under either reading as written.

  **CAPITAL ARM (rule 8, licensing on warm-up..2016 ONLY, OOS 2017-2026 read ONCE) — KILL.** The gate
  licenses moving off the 1101 anchor rung to the IS-best-Sharpe rung only if the family is decisive
  under the reading tested. Paired PER-CELL minus AGG over all 576 (family x dial) pairs:
  d(OOS Sharpe) **-0.0005 (SE 0.0017, t -0.27)**; over the 54 pairs (0.0938) where the readings pick
  DIFFERENT books, **-0.0050 (SE 0.0184, t -0.27)**, d(OOS CAGR) -0.50pp, d(OOS MaxDD) +1.82pp. Worse
  for the bar as such: licensed-minus-refused mean OOS Sharpe is NEGATIVE for both readings — AGG
  -0.0605 (297 licensed 0.7677 vs 279 refused 0.8282), PER-CELL -0.0241 (237 vs 339) — the same sign
  1252 found on P_boot. Levels, mean over the 24 families: DO-NOTHING 10.99% / 0.7922 / -23.63% with
  4b 8 of 24; ALWAYS-ACT 11.65% / 0.7986 / -25.59% with 4b 3 of 24. RULES v2 U56 OOS 9.47% / 1.2778 /
  -12.05%; SPY U56 OOS 15.28% / 0.8745 / -33.72%.

  **KEEP PATHS over all 1,200 chooser rows: 4b 296, 4a 0.** Every 4b pass is an already-committed
  anchor rung the gate declined to leave (U56 A/N-ladder 15.6% / 1.14 / -19.1%, halves 1.20 / 1.10).
  No gate at any claim set or bar produced a book the record did not already hold. Binding leg is the
  DD cap again: L_DD fails 904 of 1,200 (0.753), then L_H2 / L_OOS 618, L_H1 / L_CAGR 371.

  **RECOMMENDED TO THE SUNDAY REVIEW, PUBLISHING ONLY (changes no book, no RULES version).** A
  committed decisiveness claim must state its READING and its CELL COUNT: "decisive per cell at q=0.90
  (weakest cell 0.912, 9 of 9 cells clear)" or "decisive in aggregate at q=0.90 (pooled 0.934; 4 of 9
  cells fail their own bar)". Since the flip direction is empty in one direction at every cell tested,
  an aggregate claim that does not state its per-cell fail count is an upper bound on its own strength
  and must be written as one. The cost is nil (every run already computes the per-cell shares it pools);
  the benefit is bounded and now measured — 0.1267 of verdict pairs record-wide, 0.0312 at the bar the
  record actually publishes against, over 0.9015 of units that name no reading at all.

  **NOT CLAIMED:** that any specific committed row flips, that the per-cell reading is the better bar
  for capital (it is not: t -0.27), or that either reading's licensing adds OOS Sharpe (both are
  negative). The single best of the 48 published chooser cells, PERCELL/CS_VSBEST/q=0.60 at OOS Sharpe
  0.8157, is a max over 48 and is reported as such.

- 2026-09-18 (lane C, idea 1285 is-the-STANDING-G-0.60-BOOK-s-4b-PASS-a-REBALANCE-FREQUENCY-ARTEFACT) — **ANSWERED: NO — (A) NOT AN ARTEFACT, WITH A NARROWING. THE STANDING U56 G = 0.60 BOOK CLEARS 4b AT W, M AND Q AT ALL THREE COSTED RUNGS {10, 25, 50} bps AND AT D UP TO 25. THE CADENCE GAIN IS NOT IDEA 943's TURNOVER REBATE, AND A RULE-8 CHOOSER ALLOWED TO MOVE THE CADENCE LOSES OOS SHARPE (t -3.55). BUT THE SEPARATION FROM A TURNOVER-MATCHED TWIN IS A WEEKLY FACT: +0.1244 (z +2.30) AT W AGAINST +0.041 / +0.042 / +0.059 AT D / M / Q. KEEP-4b CONFIRMED AND NARROWED TO (U56, WEEKLY). MEMO WRITTEN; NO NEW BOOK, NO RULES CHANGE (rule 6).** 8 of 9 gate items, the 9th a pre-declared gate REFUTED and kept as a result; 68s, offline, deterministic.

  **WHY THIS IDEA.** SECOND numbered item in QUEUE.md's '## Open' (1284 is first, lane A's). Price-only; no EDGAR / Form 4 / 8-K / options / live data. No eligibility descent taken: the question is answered by re-pricing the STANDING book itself on three panels, both KEEP paths at every one of 3,360 published cells, and the run carries a rule-8 capital arm in which the cadence is a chooser dial and 2017-2026 is read once.

  **THE QUESTION.** The record's single confirmed 4b candidate is fixed at weekly decide-Friday and has never been re-priced on any other cadence. Idea 1286 (same day) found that on B135 ONE extra day of fill delay costs the identical construction 4b's drawdown leg outright (-16.84% -> -20.44%). If one day of timing does that, the cadence dial may be carrying more of the standing pass than the composite is.

  **THE CONFOUND, AND HOW IT WAS CONTROLLED.** Idea 943 established that the W -> M improvement is a TURNOVER REBATE EVERY BOOK COLLECTS (+0.0466 / +0.3334 / +0.7624 / +1.4501 for a gross-matched null at 0 / 10 / 25 / 50 bps), so no cadence LEVEL is a fact about a book. Every reading here is therefore published beside a SAME-CADENCE turnover-matched control — RAND: identical N = 20, H = 126, cadence, gross and eligibility set, ranking replaced by a uniform draw, 12 seeds — and the match is published, not assumed: turnover/yr at U56 G = 0.60 is RANK **3.28 / 2.32 / 1.74 / 1.32** against RAND **3.60 / 2.78 / 2.02 / 1.56** at D / W / M / Q. Every difference is ALSO published in a TURNOVER-NEUTRAL form charging the control `c x (turn_RANK / turn_RAND)` so the two pay identical annual drag. DIAL 1 = cadence {D, W, M, Q}; DIAL 2 = cost {10, 25, 50} bps with 0 as a free reference column. Gross is not a dial: all five rungs published everywhere, chosen in sample by the rule-8 arm only.

  **ARM A — THE PASS SURVIVES THE DIAL ON U56.** At G = 0.60, 10 bps, t+1: **D 11.89% / 1.0610 / -18.77% (OOS 1.0871), W 12.59% / 1.1517 / -15.51% (halves 1.2123 / 1.1121, OOS 13.78% / 1.1826 / -15.51%), M 11.51% / 1.0671 / -18.77% (OOS 1.0562), Q 12.37% / 1.1513 / -16.11% (OOS 1.1884)**, against SPY 15.13% / 0.8849 / -33.72% (OOS 0.8747) and live RULES v2 8.62% / 1.2018 / -12.05% (OOS 1.2781). 4b pass counts over the three costed rungs: **U56 D 2/3, W 3/3, M 3/3, Q 3/3** — D's single failure is the CAGR floor at 50 bps. The weekly choice is not what buys the pass.

  **ARM C — AND IT IS NOT 943's REBATE.** Gain relative to W at 10 bps on U56: RANK **-0.0908 / -0.0847 / -0.0004** at D / M / Q against the same-cadence NULL's **+0.0177 / +0.0057 / +0.0612**; excess **-0.1084 / -0.0904 / -0.0616**, negative at all 12 U56 cells. The rebate is plainly there — slowing to Q buys a RANDOM book +0.0612 at 10 bps and +0.1117 at 50 — and the book does not collect it. **W is the null's worst cadence and the book's best.** That is the cleanest single fact in this run.

  **ARM B — BUT THE MEASURABLE EDGE IS A WEEKLY FACT, AND THE RECORD SHOULD SAY SO.** RANK - RAND(median of 12) at U56 G = 0.60, 10 bps: **W +0.1244 (z +2.30, RANK above ALL 12 seeds)** against **D +0.0412 (z +0.60), M +0.0421 (z +1.29), Q +0.0587 (z +1.18)**. The turnover-neutral re-charge moves the W figure by 0.005 (+0.1193), so the separation is not a cost artefact. The twin passes 4b at **0 or 1 of 12 seeds at EVERY cadence**, so no cadence makes the pass reproducible without the ranking — but only at W is the ranking's contribution larger than its own seed spread. On B135 the separation is ~zero at every cadence (z -0.24 to +0.76); on SMALL663 it is NEGATIVE at every cadence, worst at M (-0.1730, z -2.06).

  **ARM D — CAPITAL, RULE 8, OOS READ ONCE. THE CADENCE IS A NEGATIVE-VALUE DIAL.** Two pre-declared choosers per (panel, arm, seed, cost): FREE picks (cadence, gross) on warm-up..2016-12-31, FROZEN_W pins the standing weekly cadence and picks gross only. **d(OOS Sharpe) = FREE - FROZEN_W = -0.0369 (SE 0.0104, t -3.55) over 168 cells**, positive 53 / negative 67 / identical picks 48; by arm **RANK -0.0611** (n = 12; 1 positive, 4 negative, 7 identical), RAND -0.0381 (n = 144), SPYONLY +0.0025. Letting the chooser move the cadence costs OOS Sharpe, so freezing the book at weekly loses nothing. U56 RANK chooser picks **W / 0.60** at 0 / 10 / 25 bps -> OOS Sharpe 1.2034 / **1.1826** / 1.1512, 4b PASS; only at 50 bps does IS drift to Q/1.00 (OOS 1.1389, fails DD — as does FROZEN_W's W/1.00). Rule-8 4b passes: **RANK 12/24, RAND 13/288, SPYONLY 0/24**. **4a is 0 of 336 chooser rows and 0 of all 3,360 published cells**, which is rule 4's own stated reason for path 4b.

  **A PRE-DECLARED GATE REFUTED, AND KEPT AS A RESULT (rule 7).** G4 was written to assert that SPYONLY — gross g of NAV in SPY, rest cash — is cadence-invariant and therefore a fixed ruler. **It FAILS.** Below gross 1.00 the arm carries a CASH SLEEVE, and rebalancing back to gross g IS a trade, so a book with no stock selection whatsoever is still a cadence object: its own D/W/M/Q Sharpe spread at G = 0.60 on U56 runs **0.0080 / 0.0099 / 0.0153 / 0.0243** at 0 / 10 / 25 / 50 bps. The failed pre-declaration is left in the gate table rather than edited away, the spread is published beside the book's, and the invariant that DOES hold is added as G4b: at gross 1.00, with no cash sleeve, the arm has exactly one distinct Sharpe and exactly zero turnover. Nothing in the headline rests on the refuted claim — SPY itself, not SPYONLY, is 4b's benchmark.

  **THE BINDING LEG.** Failing-4b-leg share: RANK (n = 203 failing cells) **DD 0.8670**, CAGR 0.5074, H2 0.4926, OOS 0.4187, H1 0.3941; RAND (n = 2,815) DD 0.7542, CAGR 0.6188; SPYONLY (n = 240) H1 0.8958, DD 0.8000. DD still binds for 0.867 of the book's failures, as the CHANGELOG says, and the cadence dial moves that leg materially (U56 MaxDD -15.51% at W against -18.77% at both D and M) **without changing the verdict on this panel** — which is why the pass survives while the edge narrows.

  **GATES, 8 OF 9 ITEMS PASSING.** G1 fast runner == engine.backtest on the standing book, **2.08e-17**. G2 SPYONLY at gross 1.00 / 0 bps reproduces each panel's own SPY Sharpe, **0.00e+00**. G3 CAGR non-increasing in the cost rung, **0** violations across 3,360 cells. G4 REFUTED (above). G4b exact. G5 SPY never a panel constituent. G6 every chooser decided on IS rows only. G7 RANK turnover/yr non-increasing as the cadence slows, **0 of 9** violations. **G8 reproduces idea 1281's committed standing-rung triple (U56 G = 0.60, W, 10 bps: 12.59% / 1.1517 / -15.51%, OOS 1.1826) to max|dSharpe| 0.0000** — the two runs' machinery agrees exactly.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B135 are CURRENT-constituent lists; SMALL663 is a current sub-$2B screen with the house `max_1d_move >= 1.0` filter applied FIRST (52 of 715 names dropped). A current-constituent list is a list of SURVIVORS, so a RANDOM draw from it is a draw from winners and the RAND control is **FLATTERED** — which biases ARM B against the separations it reports, making the weekly +0.1244 conservative. All absolute 4b verdicts and OOS triples are UPPER BOUNDS. The cadence contrast is read on identical windows of one tape and is first-order immune to a bias that moves all four cadences together.

  **MEMO.** research/backtests/2026-09-18_is-the-STANDING-G-0.60-BOOK-s-4b-PASS-a-REBALANCE-FREQUENCY-ARTEFACT_C.memo.md — confirmation memo with exact RULES wording, recommending NO enactment this week: carry the book as the record's single confirmed 4b candidate, now narrowed to U56 **and to the weekly cadence**, and let the paper / Alpaca record accumulate per the 2026-09-03 memo's standing >= 8-week caveat.

- 2026-09-18 (lane cloud, idea 1286 is-the-STANDING-G-0.60-BOOK-s-4b-PASS-PAID-FOR-BY-SELECTION-or-BOUGHT-BY-EXPOSURE) — **ANSWERED: (A) PAID FOR, AND ONLY ON U56. HOLDING N, H, CADENCE, GROSS, COST AND EXECUTION IDENTICAL AND REMOVING ONLY THE RANKING, THE RANDOM TWIN PASSES 4b AT 0 OF 12 SEEDS AT EVERY ONE OF 8 (COST, DELAY) GRID POINTS, AND THE STANDING BOOK PASSES AT ALL 8 — INCLUDING 25 bps WITH A t+2 FILL. THE RECORD'S SINGLE 4b CANDIDATE IS CONFIRMED AND NARROWED TO U56. MEMO WRITTEN; NO RULES CHANGE (rule 6).** 6 of 6 gates, 35s, offline, deterministic.

  **WHY THIS IDEA, AND WHY THE FALLBACK.** The LAST numbered items in QUEUE.md's '## Open' (943, 905, 825) are all STALE Open duplicates of entries other lanes already claimed, so none was available; everything above them up to 1139 is record-bookkeeping. The sprint's DOCUMENTED FALLBACK was used and labelled as such rather than as 'none eligible': three NEW price-only ideas stress-testing the standing KEEP-4b candidate were filed as 1284 / 1285 / 1286 and the LAST was claimed. Price-only; no EDGAR / Form 4 / 8-K / options / spin-offs / live data.

  **THE QUESTION.** Ideas 1281 and 1282 both land on the U56 gross rung G = 0.60 as the one book on the record's four ladders passing 4b under EVERY drawdown key tried. The CHANGELOG's standing diagnosis is that the DD cap is the only binding 4b leg and every exposure mechanism is reproduced by a flat gross cut. Put those together and the uncomfortable question follows: if the binding leg is drawdown and drawdown is bought by cutting gross, what is the three-leg composite SELECTION contributing? Nobody had run the control that answers it.

  **THE CONSTRUCTION.** Four arms, identical in everything but selection, on three panels. **RANK** = the standing book (top N=20 by the frozen 21/252 + 0/126 + 0/63 composite among names above their own 200d MA with vol20 < 0.60, min-hold H=126, equal weight). **RAND** = IDENTICAL mechanics, 20 slots, min-hold 126, equal weight, drawn uniformly at random from the SAME eligible set, 12 seeds — it matches breadth, holding period, turnover shape and exposure and removes ONLY the ranking. **BREADTH** = equal weight over every eligible name, no N, no ranking. **SPYONLY** = g of NAV in SPY, rest cash. DIAL 1 = cost {0, 10, 25, 50} bps; DIAL 2 = fill delay {t+1, t+2}. GROSS is NOT a dial — all five rungs {0.50, 0.60, 0.75, 0.85, 1.00} are reported at every grid point and the rule-8 arm chooses it in sample. 1,800 published cells, 360 chooser rows.

  **THE STANDING RUNG SURVIVES THE STRESS IT HAD NEVER BEEN GIVEN.** On U56 at G = 0.60 it passes 4b at ALL EIGHT (cost, delay) grid points. At the harshest — **25 bps with a t+2 fill — full 12.04% / 1.0976 / -17.06%, halves 1.1689 / 1.0503, OOS 13.21% / 1.1266 / -17.06%**, against SPY 15.13% / 0.8849 / -33.72% (OOS 0.8747) and live RULES v2 8.62% / 1.2018 / -12.05% (OOS 1.2781). At 50 bps and t+2 it still reads 11.39% / 1.0445 / -17.08% and still passes.

  **AND THE SELECTION IS PAID FOR.** RANK - RAND(median of 12) full Sharpe on U56 at G = 0.60: **+0.1152 / +0.1244 / +0.1383 / +0.1615** at 0 / 10 / 25 / 50 bps on t+1 (z **+2.05 / +2.23 / +2.50 / +2.95**, RANK above ALL 12 seeds), and +0.0807 / +0.0903 / +0.1048 / +0.1289 at t+2. The twin passes 4b at **0 of 12 seeds at every one of the 8 grid points.** The gain **WIDENS with cost on all three panels**, because the ranked book is the CHEAPEST of the three to run: turnover per year at G = 0.60 is **RANK 2.32, RAND 2.77, BREADTH 6.60** on U56. That is the opposite of the usual "the edge dies at realistic costs" failure mode and it is the most robust thing in this run.

  **IT IS A U56 FACT AND MUST BE WRITTEN AS ONE.** On B135 the separation is ~zero (z -0.26 to +0.78) and ONE extra day of fill delay costs the book 4b's DD leg outright: MaxDD at G = 0.60 goes **-16.84% (t+1) -> -20.44% (t+2)** against the cap's -20.23%, so 4b FAILS(DD) at all four t+2 cost rungs. On SMALL663 the ranking is **WORSE than random at 6 of 8 grid points** (-0.0348 / -0.0319 / -0.0275 / -0.0200 at t+1, pctile 0.333) and every 4b leg fails at every point.

  **CAPITAL, RULE 8, OOS READ ONCE.** Gross chosen on warm-up..2016 only (highest IS Sharpe among grosses passing all four IS-computable 4b legs; fallback highest IS Sharpe, declared in advance), 2017-2026 read once. Over all 24 (panel, cost, delay) cells: **RANK - RAND +0.0678 (SE 0.0186, t +3.66, positive 21 of 24)**; **RANK - BREADTH +0.1384 (SE 0.0274, t +5.04, 21 of 24)**; RANK - SPYONLY -0.0277 (SE 0.0652, t -0.42). Rule-8 4b passes: **RANK 9/24, BREADTH 5/24, RAND 11/288, SPYONLY 0/24.** 4a is **0 of 360 chooser rows and 0 of all 1,800 published cells**, which is rule 4's own stated reason for path 4b.

  **THE ONE NEGATIVE NUMBER, DECOMPOSED RATHER THAN POOLED.** RANK - SPYONLY's negative mean is **entirely SMALL663**: by panel it runs U56 **+0.202 to +0.329** and B135 +0.018 to +0.167 at every cell, against SMALL663 **-0.400 to -0.526** at every cell. On large caps the whole apparatus beats plain de-grossed SPY by a wide margin; on small caps it is beaten by it, badly, and the pooled mean hides both.

  **AN HONEST CAVEAT PUBLISHED WITH THE RESULT.** SPYONLY's 0-of-120 4b record is **MECHANICAL, not evidence**: a constant-gross SPY-plus-cash book has EXACTLY SPY's Sharpe (gate G2, 0.00e+00 at gross 1.00) and 4b's three Sharpe legs are strict '>', so it cannot pass at any gross. Its failing-leg profile confirms it — H1 and H2 named in **1.000** of its failures. The informative SPYONLY comparison is the OOS Sharpe difference above, and it is reported as such rather than as a pass count.

  **THE BINDING LEG — THE DIAGNOSIS HOLDS AND IS INCOMPLETE.** Share of each arm's FAILING cells naming each leg: RANK DD **0.8713** (H1 0.3960, H2 0.5050, OOS 0.3960, CAGR 0.4752); RAND DD 0.7489; BREADTH CAGR 0.8532; SPYONLY H1 and H2 1.0000. DD binds for 0.871 of RANK's failures, as the CHANGELOG says — **but holding gross FIXED at 0.60 and removing only the ranking still costs 12 of 12 seeds their pass. Exposure sets the level; selection decides who clears it.** That sentence is the finding worth carrying forward.

  **GATES, ALL 6 PASSING.** G1 fast runner == engine.backtest on the standing book, **2.08e-17**. G2 SPYONLY at gross 1.00 and 0 bps reproduces each panel's OWN SPY Sharpe, **0.00e+00** — the exposure control is provably the benchmark it claims to be. G3 CAGR non-increasing in the cost rung, **0** violations across all 1,800 cells. G4 SPYONLY's Sharpe invariant to the fill delay (it never trades intra-week), max distinct value **1**. G5 SPY never a panel constituent. G6 gross chosen on IS rows only (structural).

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9) — IT CUTS AGAINST THIS RUN'S OWN HEADLINE, WHICH IS THE POINT.** U56 and B135 are CURRENT-constituent lists; SMALL663 is a current sub-$2B screen with the house `max_1d_move >= 1.0` filter applied FIRST (52 of 715 names dropped). A current-constituent list is a list of SURVIVORS, so a RANDOM draw from it is a draw from winners and **RAND is FLATTERED** — which biases the run AGAINST the (A) it reports, so the true RANK - RAND separation on a vintage-honest panel is at least the +0.068 measured here. The reverse is also stated: SMALL663's negative separation is NOT evidence the composite is worthless on small caps, only that it is not visible above a survivor-flattered control. All absolute 4b / 4a verdicts and OOS triples are upper bounds.

  **MEMO.** research/backtests/2026-09-18_standing-G060-selection-value_cloud.memo.md — confirmation memo with exact RULES wording, recommending NO enactment this week: carry the book as the record's single confirmed 4b candidate, narrowed to U56 and to fills no slower than t+2 at <= 50 bps, and let the paper / Alpaca live record accumulate per the 2026-09-03 memo's standing >= 8-week caveat.

THE CROSSOVER IS AT p ~ 0.96-0.98 ON THE TWO LARGE-CAP PANELS, IT DOES NOT EXIST AT ALL ON SMALL663, AND THE QUEUE'S OWN NOMINATED Q_0.99 IS MORE UN-RESOLVABLE THAN THE MAXDD IT WOULD REPLACE ON 2 OF 3 PANELS. THE SUBSTITUTION MOVES 7 OF 60 WHOLE-4b VERDICTS, EVERY ONE pass->fail, RESOLVES WORSE, AND COSTS OOS SHARPE AT ALL SIX RUNGS. KILL (capital), NO NEW BOOK, NO RULES CHANGE.** 7 of 7 gates, 21s, offline, deterministic.

  **WHY THIS IDEA.** FIRST numbered item in QUEUE.md's '## Open'. No eligibility descent taken: the substitution is tested on REAL books — the record's own four ladders around its anchor, 20 distinct books on each of three panels — and the run carries a rule-8 capital arm in which every (p, cap multiple) cell supplies an IS-only chooser and 2017-2026 is read once. Price-only; no EDGAR / Form 4 / 8-K / options / spin-offs / live data.

  **THE CONSTRUCTION.** DIAL 1 = quantile p {0.90, 0.95, 0.975, 0.99, 0.995, 0.999, 1.00} of the |drawdown| path; p = 1.00 IS |MaxDD| exactly (gate G2, 0.00e+00), so the ladder provably contains the incumbent it is scored against. DIAL 2 = panel {U56, B135, SMALL663}. The cap multiple is REPORTED and not tuned — PROTOCOL's own 0.60 carries every headline, with 0.50 / 0.75 / 1.00 published beside it. Books = N {5,10,15,20,30,40} + H {5,10,21,42,63,90,126,189,252} + G {0.50,0.60,0.75,0.85,1.00} + C {D,W,M} around the committed anchor N=20 H=126 G=0.75 C=W, de-duplicated to 20 per panel. The re-keyed leg is Q_p(book) <= m * Q_p(SPY) with the other four 4b legs held at their own values, so every flip is attributable.

  **ARM 0 — THE PREMISE DOES NOT REPRODUCE AS A p = 0.999 FACT, WHICH IS THE HEADLINE.** b_sd = OLS slope of log SD(Q_p) on log(tape fraction), anchor book, DISJOINT windows (idea 1146 lane cloud's own geometry finding, not the nested convention): **U56 -0.0847 / -0.0024 / +0.0403 / +0.0554 / +0.0505 / +0.0513 / +0.0482** and **B135 -0.1106 / -0.0396 / -0.0008 / +0.0150 / +0.0098 / +0.0072 / +0.0122** over Q900..Q1000, against **SMALL663 +0.2156 / +0.2130 / +0.2094 / +0.1913 / +0.1798 / +0.1806 / +0.1779 — positive at EVERY rung including p = 0.90.** 1146's committed -0.0926 at p = 0.90 reproduces in SIGN on both large-cap panels, so its low end is real; but the flip sits **two to three rungs below 0.999**, not at it, and on the small panel there is nothing to switch off. And the rung the queue nominated is on the wrong side of its own boundary: **Q_0.99's b_sd (+0.0554 U56, +0.1913 SMALL663) EXCEEDS MaxDD's (+0.0482, +0.1779)**. "The whole effect lives in the top ~0.1%" is a U56-and-B135 statement that was read as a record-wide one.

  **ARM 1 — THE RE-KEY MOVES SOMETHING AT EVERY RUNG, WHICH IS THE SHARP CONTRAST WITH 1282.** Share of (panel, book, window) DD cells bit-identical to the incumbent: **0.0000 at every p < 1.00**, against yesterday's trailing-window family where K63 / K126 / K252 were 0.6566 / 0.9394 / 0.9545 degenerate and only K21 re-keyed anything. A quantile of the drawdown path is a genuinely different statistic at every rung. DD-leg flip share at 0.60: **Q900 0.2033, Q950 0.1867, Q975 0.1733, Q990 0.1667, Q995 0.1533, Q999 0.0867**; by panel **U56 0.3500, B135 0.1500, SMALL663 0.0000** — the substitution is a large-cap-only event, because no small-cap book comes near the cap under any key.

  **ARM 2 — IT IS NOT BETTER RESOLVED, AND THE REASON IS WORTH KEEPING.** Paired circular block bootstrap (L = 63, B = 300, book and SPY resampled on the SAME draws, fixed seed) of the margin M = m*Q_p(SPY) - Q_p(book), at the protocol multiple. Share of DD verdicts decided inside 1 SD of their own margin: **Q900 0.4833 / Q950 0.5833 / Q975 0.4833 / Q990 0.4833 / Q995 0.4500 / Q999 0.2333 / MaxDD 0.3167**; median |M|/SD **1.0486 / 0.9250 / 1.0027 / 1.0870 / 1.1439 / 1.7050 / 1.6282**; median SD **0.0270 ... 0.0379**. The noise does fall as the tail is sawn off — a 29% reduction from MaxDD to Q_0.90, exactly what 1146's reading predicts — and it buys nothing, because **the MARGIN shrinks faster than the noise**: the cap 0.60*Q_p(SPY) and the book's own Q_p converge on each other, so |M|/SD FALLS from 1.628 to 1.087 at Q_0.99 and the share of verdicts decided inside their own 1 SD RISES from 0.317 to 0.483. Only Q_0.999 resolves better than the incumbent, and it is the rung that barely moves anything.

  **ARM 3 — THE VERDICT CENSUS THE QUEUE ASKED FOR, NAMED, AND PURELY DESTRUCTIVE.** Whole 4b, full window, m = 0.60. Incumbent passes **10 of 60**; under Q900 1, Q950 3, Q975 3, **Q990 3**, Q995 3, Q999 4. Changed counts 9 / 7 / 7 / **7** / 7 / 6 — and **pass->fail in every single case; not one fail->pass anywhere on the ladder.** Named at p = 0.99, all on the DD leg: **U56 N=15 (+0.0009 -> -0.0166), U56 N=20 — THE COMMITTED 2026-09-04 ANCHOR — (+0.0110 -> -0.0085), U56 H=5 (+0.0177 -> -0.0066), U56 H=10 (+0.0084 -> -0.0248), U56 H=21 (+0.0004 -> -0.0070), B135 N=10 (+0.0003 -> -0.0260), B135 N=15 (+0.0057 -> -0.0162).** Adopting Q_0.99 would retire the book the live rules were derived from, on a statistic that per ARM 2 resolves that very verdict WORSE than the one it replaces. The standing U56 gross rung **G=0.60** (full 12.59% / 1.1517 / -15.51%, halves 1.2123 / 1.1121) passes 4b under EVERY p, so nothing capital-worthy is gained either.

  **ARM 4 — CAPITAL, RULE 8, OOS READ ONCE. KILL.** Each (panel, p, m) cell buys the highest-IS-Sharpe book passing all four IS-computable 4b legs under that cell's key (fallback, declared in advance: highest IS Sharpe), chosen on warm-up..2016-12-31. d(OOS Sharpe) against the MaxDD-keyed chooser over all 12 (panel, multiple) cells is **NEGATIVE AT ALL SIX RUNGS: Q900 -0.0344 (SE 0.0354), Q950 -0.0137 (0.0249), Q975 -0.0194 (0.0238), Q990 -0.0260 (0.0222), Q995 -0.0152 (0.0257), Q999 -0.0036 (0.0036)**, picks differing in 5 / 4 / 3 / 2 / 3 / 1 of 12. No rung is individually significant, which is itself the point: the substitution buys nothing measurable and loses on every reading. **0 of 84 chooser rows pass 4a**; 33 of 84 pass 4b on their OWN key, which per 1282's finding is the re-keying inflation artefact and not 33 new books. Published books: 4a **0 of 60**, 4b **10 of 60** on the incumbent key. Best quantile-keyed chooser, U56 p = 0.995 at m = 0.60 -> **G = 0.60**: full **12.59% / 1.1517 / -15.51%**, halves 1.2123 / 1.1121, OOS **13.78% / 1.1826 / -15.51%** — against SPY OOS 15.28% / 0.8747 / -33.72%, live RULES v2 OOS 9.47% / 1.2781 / -12.05%, anchor OOS 1.1832. It is **not a new book**: it is the same standing U56 gross rung 1282 published yesterday, reached by a different key. Nothing is proposed.

  **SCHEMA LINE OFFERED TO THE RECORD (not adopted here; rule 6 reserves that for a Sunday review).** *A "the effect lives in the top q of the distribution" claim is a claim about a PANEL until it is read on more than one. 1146's p = 0.999 boundary reproduces in SIGN at its low end on U56 and B135, sits two to three rungs lower than stated on both, and does not exist on SMALL663, where b_sd is positive at every quantile including 0.90. Any committed quantile boundary should name the panels it was measured on and the panels it was not.*

G2 Q_1.00 == |MaxDD| exactly, **0.00e+00** on all 60 books. G3 Q_p non-decreasing in p, **0** violations on all 60. G4 the incumbent cell reproduces PROTOCOL 4b's DD leg as written (MaxDD(book) >= 0.60*MaxDD(SPY), both negative), **0** disagreements on all 60. G5 bootstrap SD stable across rng streams, median relative move **0.0934**. G6 every chooser decided on IS rows only (structural). G7 SPY never a constituent (asserted in Panel.__init__).

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B135 are CURRENT-constituent lists; SMALL663 is a current sub-$2B screen with the house `max_1d_move >= 1.0` filter applied FIRST (52 of 715 names dropped). The headline is a CONTRAST between two keys read on identical windows of one tape and is first-order immune to a bias moving every book's drawdown together. A current-constituent panel UNDERSTATES deep drawdowns specifically — exactly the tail this run truncates — so the contrast is CONSERVATIVE for the quantile key and optimistic for MaxDD: the true case against the substitution is at least this strong. The absolute OOS triples in ARM 4 are not immune and are upper bounds.

- 2026-09-18 (lane C, idea 1282 does-the-WORST-21-DAY-DRAWDOWN-make-a-BETTER-RISK-CAP-than-MAXDD-in-KEEP-4b) — **ANSWERED: (B) DIFFERENT, NOT BETTER. K_21 IS THE ONLY RUNG THAT RE-KEYS PROTOCOL 4b AT ALL, IT IS THE BETTER-RESOLVED STATISTIC MONOTONICALLY IN k, AND IT COSTS OOS SHARPE AT EVERY CELL WHERE IT CHANGES A DECISION. KILL (capital), NO NEW BOOK, NO RULES CHANGE.** 7 of 7 gates, 98s, offline, deterministic.

  **WHY THIS IDEA.** SECOND numbered item in QUEUE.md's '## Open' (1281 is first, lane A's). No eligibility descent taken: the question is answered on REAL books — the record's own four ladders around its anchor, 22 books on each of three panels — and the run carries a rule-8 capital arm in which every (k, cap multiple) cell supplies an IS-only chooser and 2017-2026 is read once. Price-only; no EDGAR / Form 4 / 8-K / options / live data.

  **THE CONSTRUCTION.** DIAL 1 = k {21, 63, 126, 252, FULL}, the trailing-window truncation of the drawdown maximum (idea 1146's KDAY ladder; K_FULL IS MaxDD exactly, gate G2). DIAL 2 = cap multiple {0.50, 0.60, 0.75, 1.00}, 0.60 being PROTOCOL rule 4b's own. All 20 cells published on every panel, window and book. The re-keyed leg is M(k, m) = K_k(book) - m * K_k(SPY), both negative, pass iff M >= 0; the other three 4b legs are held at their own values so a flip is attributable. Four arms, all pre-declared: does the re-key MOVE anything, is it better RESOLVED (paired circular block bootstrap, L=63, B=400, book and SPY on the SAME draws), does it FORECAST (Spearman of the IS reading against realised OOS drawdown), and does it PAY (rule 8).

  **ARM 1 — ONLY K21 IS A DIFFERENT CAP, AND 1146's DEGENERACY REPRODUCES AT THE CAP.** Share of (panel, book, window) cells whose DD margin is bit-identical to the incumbent's: **K63 0.6566, K126 0.9394, K252 0.9545, K21 0.0000**. DD-leg flip share at the protocol multiple: **K21 0.2121, K63 0.0202, K126 0.0000, K252 0.0000** (by panel U56 0.3939, B136 0.1970, SMALL663 0.0455). Re-keying on anything but k=21 is a relabelling.

  **THE FLIP CENSUS THE QUEUE ASKED FOR, NAMED.** On the FULL window at 0.60, **17 of 66 committed-style whole-4b passes become 12 — 8 pass->fail and 3 fail->pass**. Lost: U56 N=15, N=20, H=21, H=126, G=0.75, C=W and B136 N=10, N=15. Gained: U56 N=5, N=10, H=252. B136 N=10 is the sharpest case — its incumbent DD margin is **+0.0003** and its K21 margin **-0.0082**. The standing U56 gross rung **G=0.60** (FULL 12.59% / 1.1517 / -15.51%, halves 1.2123 / 1.1121) passes 4b under BOTH keys, so no capital-worthy book in the record is disturbed.

  **ARM 2 — K_21 REALLY IS BETTER RESOLVED, AND MONOTONICALLY.** Share of DD verdicts decided inside 1 SD of their own paired-bootstrap margin, at the protocol multiple: **K21 0.3030 / K63 0.3232 / K126 0.3384 / K252 0.3687 / KFULL 0.3990**; median |M|/SD **2.308 / 1.965 / 1.888 / 1.648 / 1.375**; median SD **0.0225 / 0.0263 / 0.0299 / 0.0329 / 0.0358**. The ordering holds at every one of the four cap multiples and moves < 0.0016 between L=42 and L=126. Truncation buys resolution, monotonically in how much it truncates — this is the one leg of "better" that K_21 wins outright.

  **ARM 3 — IT BUYS ALMOST NO FORECAST, AND K21 IS NOT THE ARGMAX.** Panel-mean Spearman of the IS reading against realised OOS MaxDD: **K21 +0.7763, K63 +0.8115, K126 +0.7517, K252 +0.7543, KFULL +0.7547**. Against realised OOS K_21: K21 +0.7259, K63 +0.7615, KFULL +0.6563. K_21 beats MAXDD by +0.0216 and is beaten by K_63, a rung that re-keys nothing.

  **ARM 4 — CAPITAL, RULE 8, OOS READ ONCE. KILL.** Each cell's chooser buys the highest-IS-Sharpe book passing all four IS 4b legs under that cell's key (fallback: highest IS Sharpe, declared in advance). K21-keyed minus MAXDD-keyed at the same multiple, over 12 cells: **mean d(OOS Sharpe) -0.0311, SE 0.0153**; the picks differ in 4 of 12 and **every differing pick is worse** (-0.0616 / -0.0640 / -0.0788 / -0.1688, all on B136). Over all 48 re-keyed cells -0.0065 (SE 0.0044, t -1.47). The best K21-keyed chooser, U56 @ 0.60 -> N=40: full **13.44% / 1.1347 / -22.46%**, halves 1.2663 / 1.0427, OOS **14.16% / 1.1212 / -22.46%** (halves 1.2694 / 0.9590) — against SPY OOS 15.28% / 0.8747 / -33.72%, live RULES v2 OOS 9.47% / 1.2781 / -12.05%, and the anchor H=126 OOS 17.28% / 1.1832 / -19.13%. **0 of 60 OOS chooser rows pass 4a; 0 of 9 distinct picks pass 4a on the full sample.**

  **WHY IT LOSES, WHICH IS THE FINDING WORTH KEEPING.** The same multiple is not the same bar under a different key. At 0.60, K21's DD leg passes **0.6364 of books IN SAMPLE against MAXDD's 0.3333**, and **0.3485 OUT OF SAMPLE against MAXDD's 0.4242** — looser in the calm window, tighter in the one holding 2020 and 2022, because a book's K_21/MaxDD ratio is a function of how much crash the window contains. So the re-key does not tighten or loosen the cap; it re-sets it differently in every window. In sample that admits 14 books instead of 6 on U56 and 14 instead of 5 on B136, and the chooser spends the extra room on higher-IS-Sharpe, deeper-drawdown books. The same arithmetic inflates the apparent pass count: **4b on the cell's own key passes 15 of 60 OOS chooser rows against 5 of 60 on the incumbent key, with no book changing.**

  **SCHEMA LINE OFFERED TO THE RECORD (not adopted here; rule 6 reserves that for a Sunday review).** *A risk cap is a (STATISTIC, MULTIPLE) pair and the multiple is not transferable. Re-keying PROTOCOL 4b's DD leg from MaxDD to K_21 at the unchanged 0.60 moves the in-sample pass rate from 0.3333 to 0.6364 and the out-of-sample rate from 0.4242 to 0.3485 on the same 66 books — any proposal to change the key must re-derive the multiple against the window it will be applied in, and state both.*

  **GATES, ALL 7 PASSING.** G1 fast runner == engine.backtest at the anchor, **2.08e-17** (the engine-facing frame is rolled back one row because build() bakes the t+1 lag into its own rows). G2 K_FULL == MaxDD exactly, **0.00e+00**, at k=0 and at k >> T — the ladder provably contains the statistic it is compared against. G3 the vectorised sparse-table rolling maximum == idea 1146's own deque reference, **0.00e+00** at k = 21/63/126/252. G4 |K_k| non-decreasing in k on a real book (nested trailing windows), **0** violations. G5 the incumbent cell reproduces PROTOCOL 4b's L_DD on all 66 books, **0.00e+00**. G6 bootstrap SD stable across rng streams, median relative move **0.0288**. G7 every chooser decided on IS rows only (structural).

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B136 are CURRENT-constituent lists; SMALL663 is a current sub-$2B screen with the house `max_1d_move >= 1.0` filter applied first (51 of 715 names dropped). The headline is a CONTRAST between two keys measured on identical windows of one tape — which books a cap admits, and how well each cap resolves its own verdict — and is first-order immune to a bias moving every book's drawdown together; the absolute OOS triples in the capital arm are not, and are quoted against SPY and RULES v2 on the same panel.

- 2026-09-18 (lane B, idea 1139 should-a-committed-FLOOR-KEYED-CLAIM-be-required-to-quote-its-RUNG-LIST-rather-than-its-RUNG-COUNT) — **ANSWERED: YES, THE CLAUSE IS NECESSARY, AND IT BINDS ONLY WHERE THE BAR IS NOT SATURATED. THE RUNG COUNT FAILS TO DETERMINE THE VERDICT AT 21 OF 162 (panel, statistic, k) CELLS AND EVERY ONE OF THE 21 IS ON THE DRAWDOWN OR 4b-VERDICT LEG. CAPITAL ARM: KILL.**

  **WHY THIS IDEA.** LAST numbered item in QUEUE.md's '## Open' (everything below it is lane annotation). No eligibility skip taken: lane B's own 1265 correction applies — a census CAN carry a capital arm — and ARM C is one, a real rule-8 GO/NO-GO gate over 3,066 decisions on three panels with 2017-2026 read once.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE.** CLAIM_SET {CORE = LEADERBOARD+CHANGELOG, WIDE = + every committed research/*.md and research/backtests/*.md} x REQUIRED_COLS {R_LIST = the rung values, R_LIST_PLUS = list + ladder axis + the statistic the floor is over}. All 4 cells published.

  **THE CENSUS, ON THIS RUN'S OWN DENOMINATOR (idea 894 stamp, tree e929781b226c).** WIDE: **5,006 floor-keyed sentences over 1,216 committed files; 85.7% state NEITHER a rung list nor a rung count, 11.1% state a COUNT only, and 3.2% are checkable today.** Under R_LIST_PLUS checkable falls to **4 of 5,006 (0.1%)** — the clause in its strong form would newly require something of 99.9% of the corpus. CORE: 1,810 / 79.8% neither / 5.0% checkable. **The wide denominator is an UPPER bound and is published as one:** the bare word 'floor' also names bars that are not ladder claims (4b's 'CAGR floor', 877's 'seed floor'). On the STRICT denominator — the sentence names a ladder or rung at all — WIDE falls to **1,241 claims, 7.7% checkable, 18.9% count-only** and CORE to 564 / 10.8% / 20.7%. H_UNCHECKABLE FIRES on every reading.

  **THE MECHANISM, AND IT IS THE REASON THE CLAUSE SHOULD BE NARROW. THE SHARPE LEGS ARE SATURATED.** INF_FLOOR over the record's committed 9-rung H ladder {5,10,21,42,63,90,126,189,252}, each of six floor statistics carrying PROTOCOL 4b's OWN bar. On U56, S_IS / S_OOS / S_FULL / S_CAGR fire at **100.0% of C(9,k) subsets at every k = 1..9** (floors 0.9976-1.2466 against bars 0.8747-0.8989): nothing can fail, so the rung COUNT trivially determines the verdict and the rung LIST buys nothing. **The drawdown leg is the one that discriminates:** U56 S_DD fires at **44.4% / 16.7% / 4.8% / 0.8% / 0.0% at k = 1..5** (bar -0.2023, floor range [-0.2553, -0.1846]), S_4B identically, B135 S_DD 11.1% at k=1 then 0, SMALL663 silent on all six legs at every k. Across all cells the count fails at **21 of 162 (13.0%)** and all 21 are DD or verdict cells. H_COUNT_INSUFF FIRES.

  **1131's ADD-ONE-RUNG SILENCING DOES NOT REPRODUCE HERE, AND THAT IS PUBLISHED AS A NON-REPRODUCTION RATHER THAN A REFUTATION.** Adding 1131's H=210 MOVES the floor (B135 S_DD -0.2869 -> -0.2872; U56 S_OOS 1.0530 -> 1.0496) but flips the verdict at **0 of 18 (panel, statistic) cells**. The construction differs — this run's books are the frozen 2026-09-04 composite at N=20 / MAXVOL=0.60 / GROSS=0.75 with the H dial walked, and the bar is 4b's own leg bar. The rung-CHOICE dependence 1131 reports IS reproduced at matched k; the particular H=210 silencing is not.

  **CAPITAL, RULE 8, OOS READ ONCE. KILL.** The trigger run as a real gate (floor of an IS-only statistic over rung subset R clears its 4b bar -> allocate to the IS-Sharpe-argmax H in R; else DO NOTHING and hold live RULES v2): 511 subsets x 2 gate keys x 3 panels = **3,066 decisions, 4a 0 of 3,066**, gate-minus-do-nothing OOS Sharpe mean **-0.1100** (G_SHARPE) and **-0.0169** (G_DD), **positive at 2 of 3,066** (both SMALL663, max +0.0160) and at **0 of 1,022 on U56 and B135**. The 42 nominal 4b passes are not new books: all 4 of 30 grid rungs clearing 4b outright are U56 H = 5 / 10 / 21 / 126, every one an already-published rung of the incumbent's own ladder, and the best reachable cell (U56 H=126, OOS 17.28% / 1.1832 / -19.13%) IS the standing 2026-09-04 book. Under G_SHARPE the full-ladder pick is H=189 on U56 — OOS 1.0530, failing 4b on the DD leg.

- 2026-09-18 (lane cloud, idea 1146 does-a-TRUNCATED-MAXIMUM-behave-like-ULCER-or-like-MAXDD) — **ANSWERED (C) THERE IS NO SIGNATURE TO SWITCH OFF. 1140's MAXDD-vs-ULCER SEPARATION IS A WINDOW-GEOMETRY ARTEFACT: IT EXISTS ON NESTED TAIL-ANCHORED WINDOWS AND VANISHES ON DISJOINT WINDOWS OF THE SAME LENGTHS, ON THE SAME TAPE, WITH THE SAME BOOTSTRAP. KILL (capital), NO NEW BOOK, NO RULES CHANGE.** 3 of 4 gates (G3 fails BY DESIGN — its failure is the headline), 91s, offline, deterministic.

  **WHY THIS IDEA.** LAST numbered item in QUEUE.md's '## Open'. No eligibility descent was taken: the truncation ladder is measured on REAL books (1095's 9-rung H ladder) on three panels and the run carries a rule-8 capital arm in which every truncation rung is used as an IS chooser and 2017-2026 is read once. Price-only; no EDGAR / Form 4 / 8-K / options / spin-offs / live data.

  **THE CONSTRUCTION.** Dial 1 = TRUNCATION RULE, 12 rungs in two families that meet at MAXDD: **Q50 Q75 Q90 Q95 Q99 Q995 Q100** (quantiles of the |drawdown| path; Q100 IS |MaxDD| exactly, gate G4) and **K21 K63 K126 K252 KFULL** (worst drawdown inside a trailing k-day window; KFULL IS |MaxDD| exactly), plus **ULCER** and **CALMAR** as the two reference statistics 1140 contrasts MAXDD against, measured on the identical windows so the comparison is not across constructions. Dial 2 = SUB-TAPE FRACTION L in {0.125 .. 1.00}, eight rungs. At each (statistic, L): GAP = the realised cross-rung spread over the nine H rungs, and SD = a circular block bootstrap SD (L=63, B=200, seed fixed) for the anchor book, both averaged over that fraction's **DISJOINT** windows; b_GAP and b_SD are the OLS slopes on log L. **42 (panel, statistic) exponent pairs, 336 (panel, statistic, L) curve points, 27 published books, 42 chooser picks.**

  **THE WINDOW GEOMETRY IS THE RESULT, SO IT IS BOTH PUBLISHED.** A longer NESTED tail-anchored window CONTAINS the shorter one, so a maximum over it cannot fall — part of the growth is built into the geometry, which is exactly the misspecification 1057 measured on fitted intercepts. This run measures on disjoint windows and reports the nested convention at every rung. **Same tape, same L, same bootstrap, geometry only: MAXDD's b_SD reads +0.1009 nested and -0.0520 disjoint on U56, and +0.0606 nested and -0.0147 disjoint on B135, while ULCER stays negative under both (-0.0981 / -0.2500 and -0.1258 / -0.2551). 14 of 42 (panel, statistic) cells FLIP SIGN between the two geometries.** Under 1140's geometry its contrast reproduces on U56 and B135; under disjoint windows it is gone on all three panels. On SMALL663 nothing is separated under either geometry — MAXDD +0.1749 against ULCER +0.1434 disjoint, +0.4020 against +0.4590 nested — so there the growth is a property of the PANEL and not of the statistic.

  **THE QUEUE'S PREDICTION IS REFUTED IN ITS OWN DIRECTION.** Truncation does not switch the signature off. The quantile family is monotone in q with NO crossing at all (U56 b_SD runs -0.5796 / -0.3449 / -0.1327 / -0.0750 / -0.0428 / -0.0514 / -0.0520 over Q50..Q100, every rung negative), and **the ONE rung carrying the MAXDD signature on all three panels and under BOTH geometries is K21 — the MOST truncated rung of the window family** (b_SD +0.1805 / +0.1947 / +0.5668 disjoint, +0.4046 / +0.2986 / +0.8000 nested). Every other rung is 1 of 3. Sawing the tail off does not make a maximum behave like ULCER; shortening the window it maximises over makes the growth STRONGER, because a 21-day worst drawdown is a maximum over many near-independent draws while MAXDD is effectively an order statistic of one.

  **CAPITAL, RULE 8, OOS READ ONCE. KILL.** Every truncation rung run as a chooser (pick the H rung maximising IS CAGR / STAT on warm-up..2016-12-31, read 2017-2026 once): **0 of 42 picks pass 4b on any panel, 0 of 42 pass 4a, 5 of 42 reach the anchor OOS and NONE of the 42 picks IS the anchor.** The best truncated pick, U56/K21: full **15.40% / 1.1070 / -25.01%**, halves **1.2666 / 1.0009**, OOS **16.15% / 1.0720 / -25.01%** — against the anchor's OOS **1.1832 / -19.13%**, SPY **15.13% / 0.8849 / -33.72%** (OOS 0.8747) and live RULES v2 **8.62% / 1.2018 / -12.05%** (OOS 1.2781). The direction the question hoped for is faintly there and nowhere near enough: truncated rungs mean OOS Sharpe **0.8477** and reach the anchor at **4 of 30**, against untruncated (Q100/KFULL) **0.8021** and **0 of 6**. Of the 27 published books, 4a is 0 of 27 (live RULES v2's -12.05% MaxDD, rule 4's own reason for path 4b) and 4b is 4 of 27, all on U56.

  **WHAT THIS MEANS FOR PROTOCOL rule 4b's DD CAP, WHICH IS THE REASON TO CARE.** The alarm 1140 raised — that the record adjudicates its drawdown leg on the one statistic whose noise refuses to converge — does NOT survive a disjoint-window reading: on the two large-cap panels MAXDD's bootstrap SD FALLS with tape length like everything else. The cap is not sitting on a non-converging statistic. What it IS sitting on is a statistic whose noise is panel-dependent (SMALL663 positive for every rung including ULCER), which is a different and smaller problem than the one the queue posed.

  **SCHEMA LINE OFFERED TO THE RECORD (not adopted here; rule 6 reserves that for a Sunday review).** *A committed scaling exponent should state its WINDOW GEOMETRY. Nested tail-anchored and disjoint windows of identical lengths on identical data disagree in SIGN at 14 of 42 (panel, statistic) cells measured here, and the record's headline MAXDD-vs-ULCER contrast is one of them.*

  **GATES.** G2 fast runner == engine.backtest on the anchor, **2.08e-17** (the engine-facing frame is rolled back one row because build() bakes the t+1 lag into its own rows). G4 Q100 and KFULL ARE |MaxDD| exactly, **0.00e+00** — the two truncation families provably meet the untruncated statistic at their untruncated ends, so the ladder is a ladder. G5 ULCER's b_SD is negative on U56, **-0.2500** (1140's own side of the contrast reproduces). **G3 FAILS BY DESIGN AND IS THE HEADLINE:** 1140's committed MAXDD exponents (b_GAP +0.1419, b_SD +0.1654) do not reproduce in SIGN on disjoint windows (this run: +0.2990, **-0.0520**). It is recorded as a failing gate rather than re-toleranced into a pass.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B135 are CURRENT-constituent lists; SMALL663 is a current sub-$2B screen with the house `max_1d_move >= 1.0` filter applied first (52 of 715 names dropped). The headline is a CONTRAST between statistics and between geometries measured on identical windows of one tape — an exponent, not a level — and is first-order immune to a bias shifting every book's drawdown together. A current-constituent panel also UNDERSTATES deep drawdowns specifically, which is the tail this run truncates, so the measured exponents are conservative for the truncated rungs and optimistic for MAXDD; the 4a / 4b legs and the absolute OOS numbers are upper bounds.

- 2026-09-18 (lane cloud, idea 1251 does-the-OBSERVED-vs-BAR-line-explain-the-record-s-OTHER-INVARIANCE-claims) — **ANSWERED (B) AT OS_CORE AND (C) AT OS_WIDE / OS_ALL: THE OBSERVED / BAR LINE IS NOT THE WHOLE STORY, AND AT THE WIDER OUTPUT SET IT DOES NOT EVEN PREDICT THE SIDE.   **WHY THIS IDEA.** Last eligible item in QUEUE.md's '## Open'. Price-only; no EDGAR / Form 4 / 8-K / options / spin-offs / live data.

  **THE CONSTRUCTION.** Dial 1 = KNOB {K_SEED, K_B, K_DRAWKIND, K_RECENTRE}; dial 2 = OUTPUT SET {OS_CORE, OS_WIDE, OS_ALL} (1242's own three, verbatim). 12 cells, every one published. 1242's classifier is INHERITED and NOT re-tuned: SWING = mean over the 72 decisions of (max - min) across the knob's rungs; NOISE = the same across 8 rng streams at the frozen cell; cell 3 iff SWING/NOISE > 2.0. **K_SEED is the CONTROL** — the seed swing IS the yardstick, so its ratio is 1.0000 by construction and every bar output must land in cell 2; a run whose control convicts anything has a broken classifier (G5/G5b PASS). Outcomes (A) line is the whole story / (B) it predicts the side not the size / (C) it breaks were declared before any draw was taken.

  **THE ANSWER.** The OBSERVED half survives intact: **all 20 (knob, OBSERVED output) pairs are bit-identical at every rung (G6)**. The BAR half does not: **17 of 52 (knob, BAR output) pairs are NOISE-BOUND — the cell 1242 found EMPTY on L — and 5 are EXACTLY INVARIANT, a cell the line says cannot exist.** Per knob over OS_WIDE's 13 bar outputs (exact / noise-bound / knob-dependent): **K_SEED 2 / 11 / 0** (ratio 1.0000), **K_B 2 / 11 / 0** (0.600-0.778), **K_DRAWKIND 2 / 3 / 8** (1.53-3.84), **K_RECENTRE 0 / 2 / 11** (0.000-30.40). So the outcome is dial-dependent and both readings are published: (B) at OS_CORE, **(C) at OS_WIDE and OS_ALL**.

  **THE MECHANISM (a POST-HOC reading, labelled as one and used to move no gate).** What predicts invariance is not the OBSERVED / BAR side but **which functional of the draw law the output is**, and each class has an exact reason: (1) OBSERVED (5) — invariant to everything, no draw is read. (2) **LOCATION-FREE draw functionals (B_RECRANGE, B_SD)** — ratio **exactly 0.0000** under recentring, because a range and an SD cannot see a location shift, while both are knob-dependent under the draw law. (3) **THRESHOLD CROSSINGS far from their threshold (B_RESOLVED, B_GAPEXCEEDS)** — exactly invariant to SEED and to B, and B_RESOLVED still moves with the draw LAW; B_GAPEXCEEDS is additionally degenerate at L = 63 (1252's G5b: zero fires), so its invariance is a pinned constant worth nothing and is reported as such. (4) everything else — noise-bound under SEED and B, knob-dependent under DRAW KIND and RECENTRING. **THE RECORD'S OWN DECISIVE BAR, NAMED: B_RESOLVED (P_pick >= 0.90) is EXACTLY invariant to the rng stream and to B over {250, 500, 1000, 2000} on all 72 decisions, and knob-dependent under the draw law.**

  **SCHEMA LINE OFFERED TO THE RECORD (not adopted here; rule 6 reserves that for a Sunday review).** *A committed B_RESOLVED claim need state neither its seed nor its B; it must state its DRAW LAW. A committed range or SD need not state its recentring. Every other bar output must state all four.*

The only setting that matches do-nothing's 24 is **R_DRAW recentring, and it does so by firing ZERO times** — it ties by falling silent, not by being right. The only positive delta is R_OBS at +0.0004 and it still costs 5 of 24 passes; always-act is +0.0095 and costs 18. **Knob-specificity in the bar is real as a statistic and worth nothing as capital.** No new book, no RULES / PROTOCOL change.

Best cells S_OBS_MARGIN @ q0.95 **+0.1839** (welch +8.34) and S_OBS_RATIO @ q0.90 **+0.1404** (welch +5.08). Rank corr(score, d) +0.0942 / -0.1526 / +0.0079 / -0.0472. KILL (capital), NO NEW BOOK.**

  **WHY THIS IDEA.** 1246 is the SECOND item in QUEUE.md's '## Open' and lane C claims the second; it mentions none of EDGAR / Form 4 / 8-K / options / live data, so no skip applied. It is a record-bookkeeping census, and lane B's 1265 correction is accepted — a census CAN carry a rule-8 arm — so this run gave it one (ARM E) instead of descending the list.

  **THE CONSTRUCTION.** Dials (rule 4): CLAIM SET {CS_STRICT, CS_PROX, CS_ALL} x RESOLUTION RULE {R_STRICT, R_LADDER, R_ANY, R_UNION} = 12 cells, all published. Corpus PINNED to `d8729f9^`, the parent of 1239's own commit, because 1230 proved the record's censuses are reflexive and re-harvesting today cannot return 2,016. Attribution is NOT a dial: four paths in fixed precedence A_STEM > A_COL > A_NAME > A_IDEA, each path's marginal reach published. Recovery is by `ast` parse only (no execution): R_STRICT = an axis-NAMED module-level list/tuple of >= 2 literals or an axis-keyed ladder dict; R_LADDER adds CONTENT-typed bare containers; R_ANY adds loops / keyword args / comprehensions over literal lists; R_UNION adds the emitting script's docstring and the sibling paragraphs of the unit's own .md file. A tuple-unpacked or scalar binding is a POINT, never a candidate set, and only 4 of 1,658 claims resolve to a script that pins one.

  **G1-G4: THE CENSUS REPLAYS 1239 EXACTLY BEFORE ANY TRANSFER IS TRUSTED.** 34,089 text units over 1,166 committed .md files + LEADERBOARD + CHANGELOG; the unit-id SET is IDENTICAL to 1239's committed `.census.csv` (G2); CS_ALL 13,049 / CS_PROX 4,349 / CS_STRICT 2,016 and axis-naming 1,403 / 652 / 358 match to the unit; unresolvable 11,646 / 3,697 / **1,658**, the queue's own number. Today's HEAD reads 34,732 units / 1,193 .md files / CS_STRICT 2,111 / unresolvable 1,705 — and that control itself moved by 23 units between two runs of this script an hour apart, because this run's own LEADERBOARD and CHANGELOG entries landed in between. That is 1230's reflexivity live, and the reason the pin exists.

  **THE ANSWER.** Recovery on the headline population: R_STRICT **621 (0.3745)**, R_LADDER **1,273 (0.7678)**, R_ANY **1,402 (0.8456)**, R_UNION **1,434 (0.8649)**; the same shares hold on CS_PROX (0.3700 / 0.7801 / 0.8631 / 0.8804) and CS_ALL (0.3797 / 0.7814 / 0.8734 / 0.8888), so this is a property of the ARCHIVE, not of the claim set. The record mostly walks ladders it does NOT name in the identifier: R_STRICT reaches under half of what R_LADDER does (H_STRICT). Per axis at the headline cell: N 739, GROSS 797, CADENCE 458, H 345; all four recovered for 101 claims.

  **ATTRIBUTION IS NOT THE BINDING CONSTRAINT (pre-declared outcome (D) reads FALSE).** 1,581 of 1,658 (0.9536) resolve to a committed script at the pinned vintage (A_STEM 663 chosen, A_COL 897, A_NAME 21, A_IDEA 0; marginal reach 663 / 897 / 947 / 49). Of the 385 not recovered under R_LADDER, **77 lack a script and 308 have a script carrying no ladder** — 4x as many failures are silent CODE as missing code. A body-paragraph `idea NNNN` mention would have added 39 units and is deliberately not attributed on: it names the parent idea, not the emitter.

  **THE VALIDATION THE TRANSFER HANGS ON, AND ITS HONEST LIMIT.** 1239's 358 prose-resolvable claims are the ground truth: 291 attribute, and the code-recovered axis set CONTAINS the prose-named one at 0.7789 / **0.8476** / 0.8834 / 0.9759 across the four rules. H_AGREE (>= 0.80 at R_LADDER) is SUPPORTED, so the transfer is publishable. But EXACT agreement is only **0.2268** and the sets are DISJOINT 0.1078 of the time: the code names a SUPERSET of the claim's own axes about three times in four, so **a transferred K is an UPPER bound on that claim's candidate set** and a transferred N_eff a LOWER bound on its degeneracy. Every transferred number here is quoted as such.

  **THE TRANSFERRED NUMBER, AND 1239's HEADLINE SURVIVES BEING EXTENDED 4x.** On the 1,273 newly resolved headline claims: median stated N **48.0**, median rebuilt K **13.0**, median N_eff^PR **1.109** (bar-free participation ratio), median N_eff single-linkage **8.0 / 6.0 / 4.0** at 1239's own 0.0040 / 0.0100 / 0.0171 bars. 1239 published 51 stated / 3 effective on its 358. H_NEFF (median N_eff^PR <= 2.0) and H_TAIL (stated >= 10x effective; 48.0 vs 11.1) are both SUPPORTED: **the population 1239 could not read is if anything MORE degenerate than the one it could**, so 'median stated N 51, median effective N 3' was not an artefact of the writing-it-down subset. That is the substantive gain — 1239's silence on 1,658 claims was a property of its prose test, not of the walks.

With 793 / 599 / 487 headline claims citing U56 / B136 / SMALL, 'some citing claim recovers this ladder' is TRUE at 72 of 72 decisions in all 12 cells, so those rows are BIT-IDENTICAL to ACT ON ALL and H_CAPITAL is satisfied trivially rather than earned. - 2026-09-18 (lane cloud, idea 1270 is-the-DD-LEG-a-CONCENTRATION-fact-once-N-and-GROSS-are-moved-TOGETHER-at-MATCHED-EXPOSURE) — **NO. AT LEVEL EXPOSURE BREADTH MAKES THE DRAWDOWN *WORSE* ON BOTH LARGE-CAP PANELS AND COSTS CAGR MONOTONICALLY ON ALL THREE — rank corr(N, CAGR) = -1.00 AT EVERY (PANEL, SLOT), rank corr(N, MaxDD) = -0.60 (U56) / -1.00 (B135) — AND MaxDD IS 2.0x-3.5x MORE AN EXPOSURE OBJECT THAN A BREADTH OBJECT.** Verdict KILL (capital). No book, no RULES change, no PROTOCOL edit. 27 of 28 gates, 81s, offline, deterministic.

  **WHY THIS IDEA.** 1270 is the LAST item standing in QUEUE.md's '## Open' and it yields this protocol's step-3 deliverable directly, so no eligibility skip was needed and the sprint's fallback was NOT used. Price-only; 1269 is left Open for the next lane.

  **THE CONSTRUCTION.** Dial 1 = N {10,20,30,40,50}. Dial 2 = a TARGET REALISED MEAN EXPOSURE {0.55,0.65,0.75,0.85,1.00}, with the gross that attains it SOLVED per cell by bisection and both the solved gross and the realised exposure published — the queue's own "proof the line is level" (**G2: realised == target to 9.0e-13 / 4.7e-13 / 1.1e-12 at every reachable cell**). 150 cells = 5 x 5 x 3 panels x 2 slot conventions, plus 30 UNMATCHED flat-0.75 controls so the matching's own contribution is visible. Frozen at the standing 2026-09-04 book: RAW 3-leg composite, above-200d AND vol20 < 0.60, H = 126, weekly, 10 bps, t+1, warm-up 260. Gross capped at 1.00 — no leverage (rule 2); the 10 unreachable FIXN cells are published at the cap with their realised exposure (0.904..0.999) rather than levered into.

  **THE STRUCTURAL FINDING THAT HAS TO COME FIRST (pre-declared outcome (A)).** **Under the incumbent's own sizing (w = 1/len(held)) the realised exposure does not depend on N at all** — the solved gross is invariant in N to **1.6e-04** at every exposure rung (G5a) — because the book is always fully at its gross whenever it holds any name. So **the iso-exposure line IS the plain N ladder at gross 0.75** and 1081's reading already covered it. The idea's premise (that N buys diversification "with cash") is true only under FIXED 1/N slots, where a thinning screen de-grosses. Both conventions are therefore reported at every cell. Against the same N at flat 0.75, the matching is worth -0.05 / -0.19 / -0.38 / -0.79 / **-2.03**pp of MaxDD and +0.04 / +0.17 / +0.28 / +0.51 / **+1.24**pp of CAGR at U56 N = 10/20/30/40/50; at most -0.23pp / +0.16pp on B135; and **nothing at all on SMALL663**, where n_sel is either N or 0 at every rebalance so FIXN and LEN are literally the same book.

  **ON THE LINE, AT THE COMMITTED 0.75.** U56/LEN MaxDD **-20.76 / -19.12 / -22.07 / -22.45 / -21.73%** with CAGR **17.05 / 15.77 / 14.59 / 13.44 / 12.82%** at N = 10/20/30/40/50: **the committed N=20 is the DRAWDOWN ARGMAX of the whole line** and every broader book at exactly level exposure is worse on BOTH axes. B135/LEN is monotone worsening, -20.19% -> -26.46% (**-6.26pp**) for -3.60pp of CAGR, rank corr(N, MaxDD) -1.00. SMALL663 inverts (+8.54pp from N=10 to N=50, rank corr +0.70) but fails all five 4b legs at 50 of 50 cells, so its breadth effect is not a book. Pre-declared outcome **(C)** fires on both panels that could carry capital; the honest overall reading is **(D)**, panel-specific.

  **THE SURFACE.** Mean MaxDD spread ACROSS N at fixed exposure vs ACROSS EXPOSURE at fixed N: U56 3.34 vs 11.65pp (**3.49x**) and 3.82 vs 10.99pp (2.87x); B135 6.28 vs 12.71pp (2.02x) and 5.63 vs 11.82pp (2.10x); SMALL663 8.45 vs 18.64pp (2.20x). Drawdown on this book is two to three and a half times more a function of HOW MUCH is held than of HOW MANY names hold it.

  **CAPITAL.** 4a **0 of 150**. 4b 35 of 150 (U56 9 + 8, B135 8 + 10, SMALL663 0 + 0); the binding leg is the DD cap at 14 / 15 / 17 / 15 of the large-cap fails and all five legs on SMALL663 — the sixth run running to land there. The 8 4b conversions on B135/LEN all sit at EXPO 0.55-0.75, i.e. **bought by de-grossing and not by breadth**, and B135/FIXN's do-nothing cell already passes, so the conversions are a convention artefact.

  **RULE 8.** (N, EXPO) chosen on warm-up..2016-12-31 by IS Sharpe ALONE, 2017-2026 read once, against do-nothing = (N=20, EXPO=0.75) = the committed book. **The IS-argmax picks EXPO = 1.00 at 6 of 6 (panel, slot) cells — full exposure maximises in-sample Sharpe — and then FAILS 4b's DD cap at 6 of 6.** Deltas -0.0688 / -0.0006 (U56 LEN/FIXN), **-0.0966 / -0.0918** (B135), +0.0207 / +0.0207 (SMALL663), **mean -0.0361**, beating do-nothing at 2 of 6 and only on the panel that fails 4b everywhere. IS/OOS rank corr over the 25 cells -0.55 / +0.08 / -0.25 / -0.06 / -0.03 / -0.03. **0 of 6 rule-8 picks clear either KEEP path.** U56/LEN pick (N=50, EXPO=1.00) OOS 1.1144 / 17.56% / -28.20% against do-nothing 1.1832 / 17.28% / -19.12%; SPY OOS 0.8747 / 15.28% / -33.72%; live RULES v2 OOS Sharpe 1.2781.

  **GATES 27 of 28, AND THE ONE FAILURE IS THE FINDING.** **G5b FAILS on SMALL663** (the solved gross does not move with N under FIXN) and **G5c explains it and passes**: FIXN can only de-gross where 0 < n_sel < N, and that partial-thin share is **0.0000 at every N** on a 663-name pool against 0.0154..0.6592 on U56 — a property of the panel, not the code, published rather than re-specified away. G3 no leverage anywhere. G6 holdings support bit-identical at gross 0.40 vs 0.80, so the exposure dial moves exposure and nothing else. G7 fast runner == engine.backtest 1.74e-17. G4 determinism 0.000e+00 on the re-solved U56 LEN grid. G8 SPY held on 0 days of all three panels. G1 the anchor replays 15.7814% / 1.1522 / -19.1276% against the committed 15.7147% / 1.1480 / -19.1276%, err 4.16e-03 — PUBLISHED, not toleranced: data/prices.csv is refreshed daily and its adjusted closes are restated retroactively, which idea 1203 measured the same day at 2.9e-04 of IS Sharpe and 7.5e-03 of OOS Sharpe.

  **WHAT THE RECORD SHOULD CARRY FORWARD.** The search for the binding 4b drawdown leg inside the book's BREADTH is over. What has never been moved is WHEN a name stops being eligible — entry and exit still read the same 200d MA — which is exactly idea 1269, left Open.

Every level is optimistic and every 4b pass is an UPPER bound. The headline is a SHAPE IN N at level exposure inside one panel over one tape, first-order immune to a level bias that moves all cells together. One direction is not neutral and is stated: a current-constituent panel has no delistings, so the screen never thins for the reason it would thin in life, and the wide-N cells (N=40, 50 on a 55-name panel) are flattered relative to a live panel where the tail names would be gone rather than merely out of favour. That biases the test TOWARD outcome (B), which makes the (C) finding the stronger conclusion.

- 2026-09-18 (lane cloud, idea 1203 does-the-TIE-BREAK-STATISTIC-matter-once-the-TIE-is-DECLARED) — **YES, AND MORE THAN THE BOUND FORM IT WAS INTRODUCED TO REPAIR.
