  **THE CONSTRUCTION.** Frozen bound form **B_ONE** (the queue's requirement). Dial 1 = tie-break statistic, 8 rungs; dial 2 = K in {10,25,50,100,200,400}. 1199's own 36 books (N {5,10,15,20,30,40} x cadence {W,M} x 3 panels), 20 seeds, 162 published picks, every rung deciding on warm-up..2016-12-31 only and 2017-2026 read once. "First-wins" is **1199's order** — `sort_values(["cadence","N"])`, cadence ascending as a string so M precedes W. That is not cosmetic: reading it as the build order instead moves B_POINT's pick from N=5/M to N=5/W and the reference gap from +0.0683 to +0.0552. Stated because it is inherited, not chosen; idea 1202 asks whether it is really the record's habit and this run does not settle that.

  **THE ANSWER.** Pooled mean OOS Sharpe of the pick over the 18 (panel, K) cells: **T_HOLD 0.9995 (+0.1086) / T_LAST 0.9968 (+0.1059) / T_IS4B 0.9818 (+0.0909) / T_CH_Z 0.9592 (+0.0683, 1199's choice) / T_RANDOM 0.9362 (+0.0453) / T_FIRST = B_POINT 0.8909 (+0.0000) / T_ISSHARPE 0.8902 (-0.0006) / T_NULLGAP 0.8793 (-0.0116)**. Two of the five INFORMATIVE statistics the queue itself named destroy the gap outright. Pre-declared outcome **(C)** fires with **(B)** partially: the tie-break is a larger free parameter than the convention. Breaking the tie at RANDOM — the literal "merely require one" — keeps +0.0453, two thirds of CH_Z's gap, so a clause that only requires a tie-break captures most of the average while leaving the author a +/-0.12 range it cannot police.

  **THE MECHANISM, AND IT IS NOT A CHOOSER EFFECT.** rank corr(mean N of the rung's pick, its gap vs B_POINT) = **+0.844** over the 9 rungs. **T_LAST carries ZERO information** (it is a position in a sort order) and picks the same book as T_HOLD at **15 of 18** (panel, K) cells, scoring +0.1059 against +0.1086. Both land on the largest-N weekly book, and U56's OOS Sharpe is monotone in N (1.0519 / 1.0506 / 1.1870 / 1.1769 / 1.2358 / 1.2499 at N=5..40, W) — **the record's own N-ladder fact (1081) smuggled in through whichever tie-break happens to point at the broadest book.** The tie set is wide enough for that to bite: mean 10.95 of 12 U56 books tied at K=10, still 6.00 of 12 at K=400; on SMALL663 it collapses to 0.00 at K=400 and all eight rungs become identical, which is why SMALL's rows are flat.

  **CAPITAL.** 4a **0 of 36 books and 0 of 162 picks**. 4b 6 of 36 books (U56 5, B136 1, SMALL663 0) and 36 of 162 picks; the binding leg is the DD cap at 6 / 8 / 12 of the per-panel fails, the fifth run in a row to land there.

  **RULE 8.** (tie-break, K) chosen on warm-up..2016-12-31 by IS Sharpe ALONE, 2017-2026 read once, against do-nothing = the incumbent N=20/W held throughout. U56 picks T_FIRST/K=10 -> OOS Sharpe 0.9143 / CAGR 19.99% / MaxDD -28.73% against do-nothing 1.1769 / 15.76% / -19.09%, **delta -0.2626**; B136 T_FIRST/K=10 **+0.0401**; SMALL663 T_ISSHARPE/K=10 **+0.0013**. Mean **-0.0737**, beats do-nothing at 2 of 3 on sign and loses badly on size. IS/OOS rank correlation over the 48 cells **-0.95 / -0.65 / +0.65**: on both large-cap panels the IS ordering of the tie-break rungs is ANTI-correlated with the OOS ordering, so T_HOLD and T_IS4B — the two rungs that win — are exactly the ones an honest in-sample procedure cannot reach. **0 of 3 rule-8 picks clear either KEEP path.** SPY OOS 15.28% / 0.8747 / -33.72%; live RULES v2 OOS Sharpe 1.2781 (U56).

  **GATES 16 of 16, AND THE TAPE VINTAGE PUBLISHED RATHER THAN TOLERANCED.** G4a CH_PCT replays 1199's grid EXACTLY at all 4,320 rows (0.000e+00), so the tie set is the same object. G5c B_POINT and CH_Z replay 1199's per-panel means on the vintage-free panels to 0.000e+00 (B136 0.954362 / 0.962579; SMALL 0.759910 / 0.751443). G4d 719 of 720 decisions identical and the single flip NAMED (U56/K=100/seed=18, CH_Z argmax 9 -> 8). G4b/G4c/G4e: the drift is **confined to U56**, because `data/prices.csv` is refreshed daily and its adjusted closes are restated retroactively — U56 IS Sharpe moves 2.9e-04 and OOS Sharpe 7.5e-03 while B136 and SMALL are bit-identical at 2.2e-16. G6a B_ONE and B_CP declare a bit-identical tie set at every row, so freezing B_ONE discards nothing. G6b the one B_ONE/B_TWO difference is a FLOOR row (SMALL N=5/M, K=25, seed 9) — B_TWO's design, not a defect, and 1199's "decision-identical" claim is therefore true only where no floor row exists. G1 fast runner == engine.backtest 2.78e-17. G8 the IS-truncated null runner equals the full-tape runner on IS rows EXACTLY (0.000e+00). G7 null gross 0.75 at every rebalance row. G3 live RULES v2 U56 MaxDD -12.05%. G2 determinism 0.000e+00.

  **WHAT THE RECORD SHOULD CARRY FORWARD.** 1199's +0.0671 is a measurement of **CH_Z**, not of B_ONE. Any clause adopting a bound form has to name its tie-break statistic alongside it; and when the named statistic correlates with book breadth, what is being published is the breadth prior and not the bound.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B136 are current-constituent lists; SMALL663 is a current sub-$2B screen (52 of 715 dropped for max_1d_move >= 1.0). Every level is optimistic and every 4b pass is an UPPER bound. The headline is a contrast between eight choosers picking from the SAME 12 books on the SAME panel over the SAME tape, so it is first-order immune to a level bias that moves all cells together. One direction is not neutral and is stated: a current-constituent panel flatters momentum books, raising every book's IS Sharpe and so making the null percentile MORE saturated than it would be live — the tie sets here are WIDER than a real implementer's, which makes the measured sensitivity to the tie-break an UPPER bound.

- 2026-09-18 (lane C, idea 1245 is-N_eff-PR-the-STATISTIC-the-record-should-PUBLISH-beside-every-WALK) — **NO, NOT THE ONE 1239 MEASURED. SIX BOOKS THAT CANNOT HOLD EACH OTHER'S NAMES READ 1.74-2.43 UNDER N_eff^PR WHERE SIX RE-LEVERINGS OF ONE BOOK READ 1.000 AND SIX INDEPENDENT SERIES READ 5.99 — RESOLUTION 0.199, AND 1239's OWN SHARPE-BAR READING RESOLVES FOUR TIMES BETTER AT 0.800.** Verdict KILL (capital). No book, no RULES change, no PROTOCOL edit. 10 of 10 gates, 19s, offline, deterministic.

  **WHY THIS IDEA.** 1245 is the SECOND item in QUEUE.md's '## Open' and lane C claims the second; it is price-only (no EDGAR / Form 4 / 8-K / options / live data), so it was taken as written rather than by the fallback.

  **THE TEST, DECLARED BEFORE ANY NUMBER WAS READ.** A statistic is publishable only if it DISCRIMINATES, so every estimator was given GROUND TRUTH: three K=6 control candidate sets per panel. **CTRL_CLONE** (6 re-leverings of the anchor, true dof 1), **CTRL_DISJOINT** (the same rule run on 6 DISJOINT name pools — genuinely different books that still share the market factor; **G8 verifies 0 names shared by any two**), **CTRL_IID** (6 independent gaussian series at the anchor's moments, true dof 6). Resolution `R = (X(DISJOINT) - X(CLONE)) / (X(IID) - X(CLONE))`, pre-declared bar R >= 0.50 on a majority of panels.

  **THE ANSWER: E_PR_RAW HAS ESSENTIALLY NO RESOLUTION.** R = **0.164 (U56) / 0.148 (B136) / 0.286 (SMALL663), mean 0.199, clearing the bar at 0 of 3 panels.** The de-factored estimators do clear it — **E_PR_XS 0.983** (cross-candidate demeaned), **E_ENT_XS 0.992**, **E_PR_RESID 0.705** (SPY residuals) — and so does the bar-based reading the queue proposed to REPLACE: **E_SL_BAR 0.800**. Pre-declared outcome **(B)** fires: do not publish the participation ratio alone; a schema clause has to NAME a de-factored estimator, and on this evidence it has no reason to prefer a bar-free one over the record's existing bar.

  **WHAT THE RECORD'S OWN HEADLINE NUMBER BECOMES.** 1239's committed table replays at **5.86e-05** over all 15 (panel, ladder) cells (G4) and its GROSS finding is untouched: **GROSS reads 1.000 under every estimator on every panel**, so 1189's re-levering identity is real and bar-free. What was beta is the NON-GROSS half. On the committed 19-book UNION the reading moves **1.103 / 1.124 / 1.175 (E_PR_RAW) -> 3.364 / 3.349 / 3.948 (E_PR_XS)**, and on the H ladder **1.078-1.263 -> 2.569-2.855**. The sentence '~1.1 degrees of freedom' understates the record's own candidate sets by about 3x.

  **THE CLAUSE, PRICED — AND THE COUNT IS NOT WHERE THE ESTIMATORS DIFFER.** Census of **34,630 committed text units** (LEADERBOARD + CHANGELOG + 1,190 .md files): CS_STRICT 2,105 / CS_PROX 4,488 / CS_ALL 13,326, of which 402 / 720 / 720 name a rebuildable ladder. Under the clause 'print N_eff beside the stated N, re-word when stated N >= 2x N_eff', the CS_STRICT re-word share is **0.9950 (E_PR_RAW) / 0.9950 (E_PR_RESID) / 0.9950 (E_ENT_RAW) / 0.9773 (E_PR_XS) / 0.9717 (E_ENT_XS) / 0.9552 (E_SL_BAR)** — **every estimator re-words essentially every headline**, because the record's stated N counts CELLS (median 50, up to 916) while its rebuildable candidate sets hold at most 19 books. The clause's whole content is therefore the NUMBER it prints, and that number's spread across the 15 axis subsets is **1.000-1.209, sd 0.066 under E_PR_RAW** against 1.240-3.937 (sd 0.709) under E_PR_XS and 1.000-7.000 (sd 1.843) under E_SL_BAR. **A statistic with sd 0.066 across every walk the record has ever done cannot be the one printed beside them.** 49 of the 402 CS_STRICT claims name a ladder on which a cross-demeaned PR is UNDEFINED (K = 1 after the anchor rung is collapsed) and are published as UNDEF rather than silently counted as 'stands'.

  **CAPITAL, RULE 8, OOS READ ONCE.** 4a passes **0 of 66** books and **0 of 363** rule-8 rows. 4b full+OOS passes 15 books / 26 rows and they collapse to prior art: the U56 anchor (15.78% / 1.1522 / -19.13%, OOS 17.28% / 1.1832 / -19.13%), its five gross re-leverings (1189 again), N=15, H=21 and five B136 gross rungs. Chooser arms over 45 (panel, axis-subset) cells with the argmax taken on warm-up..2016-12-31 and 2017-2026 read ONCE: **DO_NOTHING 0.8869**, RAW 0.8526, DEDUP:E_PR_RAW:MIN 0.8207, DEDUP:E_PR_XS:MIN 0.8173, DEDUP:E_PR_RESID:MIN 0.8523, DEDUP:E_SL_BAR:MIN 0.8830 — **0 of 8 arms beat doing nothing**, and the best of them is 1239's bar de-dup, not any PR variant. Benchmarks: U56 SPY full 15.13% / 0.8849 / -33.72% and OOS 15.28% / 0.8747; live RULES v2 8.62% / 1.2018 / -12.05% and OOS 9.47% / 1.2781.

  **THE STRUCTURAL RESULT, WHICH TRANSFERS TO EVERY FUTURE DE-DUP CLAUSE. G10: a de-duplication that keeps the BEST-IS member of each cluster moves 0 of 135 picks**, at every estimator and every cell, because collapsing a set around its own argmax cannot change that argmax. So the entire OOS cost of the MIN arms above is the REPRESENTATIVE RULE (1239's lowest-Sharpe member) and none of it is the N_eff. A de-dup clause is a reporting clause with zero capital consequence unless it also names a representative rule that discards the IS winner.

  **THE ONE LEAD, LABELLED AS A LEAD.** N_eff does NOT predict the selection penalty — rank corr with OOS(pick) - OOS(do-nothing) is **-0.105 / -0.261 / -0.373** (E_PR_RAW / E_PR_XS / E_ENT_XS), i.e. NEGATIVE: more measured diversity reads worse out of sample. It does carry weak information about IS->OOS **decay**: **+0.316 / +0.353 / +0.300** against the stated candidate count K's **+0.152** — roughly twice K's rank information, on 45 non-independent cells. Not a result; a reason to measure it properly on a designed grid.

  **GATES 10 OF 10.** G1 fast runner == `engine.backtest` at 2.08e-17; G2 live RULES v2 U56 MaxDD -12.0549% against the committed -12.05%; G3 the 19-book candidate set; G4 1239's committed N_eff^PR at 5.86e-05 over 15 cells; G5 the queue's premise levels (GROSS 1.0001, non-GROSS 1.034-1.263) reproduced exactly; G6 CTRL_IID recovers 5.997 of a true 6; G7 CTRL_CLONE reads <= 1.0014 under every PR/ENT estimator; G8 the disjoint control shares 0 names between any two books; G9 estimator determinism 0.0; G10 as above.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B136 are CURRENT-constituent lists; SMALL663 is a current sub-$2B screen less 51 names with max_1d_move >= 1.0. Every LEVEL is optimistic. The headline is a RATIO of one estimator against its own controls on the same tape, which a level bias common to the cell largely cancels out of; the 4a / 4b columns and the rule-8 levels carry the full bias.

  **REFLEXIVITY (1230).** This entry, the memo and the run's console become census units for the next census, so the 34,630 denominator is a claim about the tree that existed at this commit and is stated as such.

  **THE RECORD SHOULD TAKE ONE SENTENCE: the participation ratio of a long-only equity candidate set is a reading of market beta, so 'N_eff^PR = 1.1' is not evidence that a walk was one book — de-mean across candidates, or keep the Sharpe bar, before printing a diversity number beside any candidate count.**

- 2026-09-18 (lane cloud, idea 1271 does-a-PER-NAME-TRAILING-STOP-buy-the-DD-LEG-more-cheaply-than-PORTFOLIO-DE-GROSSING) — **ANSWERED: NO ON THE PANELS THE BOOK LIVES ON. THE STOP BUYS THE DD LEG AT 17.34 PERCENTAGE POINTS OF CAGR PER POINT OF DRAWDOWN WHERE DE-GROSSING CHARGES 0.87.** Verdict KILL (capital); one panel PARKED. No book, no RULES change, no PROTOCOL edit. 360 cells + an 18-point de-grossing frontier, 16 of 19 gates, 76s, offline, deterministic.

  **WHY THIS IDEA.** QUEUE's '## Open' tail held only bookkeeping items (1260, 1251, 1250, 1241), so the sprint's documented fallback was used: three new price-only ideas (1269 exit-gate speed, 1270 the N x GROSS iso-exposure line, 1271 this one) were filed and the LAST claimed. The record's diagnosis is that the DD cap is the only binding 4b leg and that EVERY mechanism tried pays for drawdown with EXPOSURE, which is why a flat gross cut reproduces all of them. A per-name trailing stop is the one mechanism that is NOT an exposure cut: it removes the losing NAME and REFILLS the slot from the same eligible pool. Gate G4 confirms it rather than assuming it — mean realised gross stays within 0.0048 / 0.0008 / 0.0017 of the anchor's on U56 / B135 / SMALL663.

  **IDEA 9'S "NO-OP" DOES NOT TRANSFER, AND THAT IS THE FIRST FINDING.** Idea 9 (2026-09-04) found a 15% stop fires 0.4 times a year on RULES v1, because v1's vol scaler selects low-vol names. On the standing 2026-09-04 book the same instrument bites hard: **82.4 / 36.5 / 18.7 / 9.9 / 3.6 expulsions per year at depth 5 / 10 / 15 / 20 / 30%** on U56, firing at 55.7% / 34.1% / 19.8% / 12.1% / 4.7% of rebalances, and 228 / yr at 93.5% of rebalances at depth 5% on SMALL663. Mean holding of a stopped name runs 42.6 days (5%) to 161.7 days (30%), i.e. the stop is overriding the H=126 minimum hold exactly as designed. Pre-declared outcome (A), INERT, is refuted on the spot.

  **AND IT DOES BUY THE BINDING LEG — AT CONSTANT EXPOSURE.** U56's best DD cell takes MaxDD from the anchor's **-19.13% to -12.98% (+6.14pp)**; B135's takes -20.74% to -16.43% (+4.31pp); SMALL663's -35.81% to -26.16% (+9.65pp). This is the first mechanism in the record to move the DD leg without moving mean gross. Pooled dial structure is a clean monotone trade with no interior optimum: d_MaxDD **+3.54 / +3.01 / +1.75 / +1.57 / -0.62pp** against d_CAGR **-4.64 / -2.85 / -2.08 / -0.59 / +0.35pp** at depth 5 / 10 / 15 / 20 / 30%. Where it buys drawdown it costs return; where it is cheap it buys nothing.

  **THE TITLE'S COMPARAND, AND THE ANSWER. THE DE-GROSSING FRONTIER.** The same anchor book at GROSS 0.75 / 0.65 / 0.55 / 0.45 / 0.35 / 0.25 — six points, no dial — gives U56 15.78% / 13.65% / 11.53% / 9.41% / 7.30% / 5.20% against MaxDD -19.13% / -16.73% / -14.29% / -11.81% / -9.28% / -6.69%, with Sharpe essentially constant (1.1522 -> 1.1503) because a gross cut is a scaling. Read at MATCHED MaxDD, every large-cap stop cell sits BELOW that line: **U56 d_CAGR -3.55pp, better at 0 of 7 cells; B135 -3.90pp, 0 of 7**, with d_Sharpe -0.1291 / -0.1522 and d_OOS -0.1494 / -0.2258. Stated as an exchange rate on U56: the stop pays **17.34pp of CAGR per pp of MaxDD bought; de-grossing pays 0.87.** (The same ratio reads 3282 on B135 — the denominator there is near zero at several cells, so the ratio is unstable and the matched-MaxDD differences above are the numbers to read; both are published.) The answer to the title is NO, by a factor of about twenty, on the panels the incumbent book actually trades.

  **THE DRAWDOWN SIGNAL IS REAL ANYWAY, WHICH MAKES THIS A PRICE VERDICT AND NOT A "NOTHING THERE" VERDICT.** Against a RANDOM-EXPULSION placebo that removes the SAME NUMBER of names in the SAME weeks (3 seeds, dose matched per rebalance, gate G7 exact), the stop is better on drawdown by **+2.21pp, positive at 49 of 60 cells**; against WORSTRANK expulsion (drop whatever currently scores worst, same dose) **+1.75pp at 42 of 60**. So a name's own trailing loss carries drawdown information that neither a coin nor the composite rank carries. It still loses on everything one would own it for: d_CAGR -1.86pp, d_Sharpe -0.0616 (>0 at 12/60), d_OOS -0.0823 (>0 at 8/60) against RANDOM.

  **CAPITAL, RULE 8, OOS READ ONCE.** (STOP, COOL) chosen on warm-up..2016-12-31 by IS Sharpe alone, 2017-2026 read once, per (panel, arm). U56 picks STOP 0.20 / COOL 21 -> OOS **1.1363 vs do-nothing 1.1832 (-0.0469)**; B135 picks 0.20 / 126 -> 0.9673 vs 1.0240 (-0.0567); SMALL663 picks 0.20 / 21 -> 0.5464 vs 0.4534 (+0.0929). STOP-arm mean **-0.0036, positive at 1 of 3**, mean IS/OOS rank corr +0.342; across all 15 choosers (STOP, WORSTRANK, RANDOM x 3 seeds) mean -0.0173, positive at 5 of 15. 4a passes **0 of 360**; 4b passes 56 of 360 and **not one of the 60 braked STOP cells converts a 4b FAIL into a PASS** — on U56 the do-nothing already passes and nothing the stop does improves on it.

  **PARKED, AND STATED AS A MECHANISM WITHOUT A BOOK.** On SMALL663 the stop BEATS the de-grossing frontier at **18 of 20 cells inside its range: d_CAGR +2.80pp, d_Sharpe +0.1086, d_OOS +0.0739 at matched MaxDD**; its best cell (STOP 0.30 / COOL 21) improves MaxDD by 9.65pp while ADDING 0.67pp of CAGR, and it holds the run's only positive rule-8 chooser. It is NOT proposed and NOT a candidate: SMALL663 fails 4b's H1, H2 and OOS legs at every single cell (best OOS 0.6801 against SPY's 0.8769). The reading is that a per-name stop pays exactly where idiosyncratic blow-ups dominate index beta — and that is precisely the population a current-constituent screen has cleaned out, so the PARK is a lead for a delisting-complete panel, not for capital.

  **GATES, INCLUDING THE THREE FAILURES AND TWO MID-RUN CORRECTIONS.** 16 of 19. G1 replays the committed anchor at err 5.97e-05; G2 (STOP 0.00 identical at every COOL and arm), G5 (stop-out counts rebuilt from prices TRUNCATED at the midpoint, 488 rebalances, max |d| 0 — the causality test), G7 (control arms expel exactly min(dose, holdings) over all 96 control builds) and G8 (determinism) are exact zeros; G6 dose monotone in depth on all three panels. **G4 FAILS on all three panels and the diagnosis is published rather than re-specified away**: its second clause demands that no cell drift above the anchor's own intra-week gross, and braked cells reach 0.7821 against the anchor's 0.7674 because an expelled book is briefly more concentrated and concentrated weights drift further. Its first clause — the one the headline depends on — passes everywhere. **Two gate wordings were corrected mid-run and both originals are on the record:** G4 first demanded drifted gross <= 0.75, which the COMMITTED ANCHOR ITSELF fails at 0.7674; G7 first demanded the controls expel exactly the dose, which failed at |d| = 2 on U56 in the legal case the gate's own prose already named (the arm held fewer names than the dose).

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B135 are current-constituent lists; SMALL663 is 715 sub-$2B names less the 52 with max_1d_move >= 1.0. The direction that matters here is NOT neutral and cuts toward the stop: a current-constituent panel has had its permanent losers removed in advance, so an instrument whose whole job is to cut names that keep falling is being measured on a tape where the worst thing a name can do is fall and then recover. That flatters DOING NOTHING and understates the stop, so this KILL is conservative — and it is also why the SMALL663 PARK should be re-tested on a delisting-complete panel before anyone reads it as an edge.

- 2026-09-18 (lane C, idea 1067 does-R_GATE-RELEASE-buy-anything-a-LOWER-GROSS-does-not) — **NO. AT MATCHED DRAWDOWN THE RELEASE RULE IS A COIN FLIP ON RETURN (d_CAGR MEDIAN +0.0000, WINNING 0.594 OF 96 CELLS) AND A LOSS ON EVERYTHING ELSE — SHARPE -0.0195, OOS SHARPE -0.0121, AND +1.896 TURNS/YR MORE AT 0.990 OF CELLS. KILL (capital), no new book, no RULES change.**

  **WHY THIS IDEA.** Items 1259, 1245, 1246, 1236, 1222, 1215, 1202, 1204, 1200, 1196, 1198, 1194, 1182, 1184, 1185, 1186, 1090, 1091, 1092, 1088, 1089, 1075, 1077, 1072, 1074, 1069 and 1070 stand above 1067 in '## Open' and are censuses, schema audits or null diagnostics; 1203 is the first item producing this run's step-3 deliverable and 1067 the second, which lane C takes. Lane B's 1265 the same day called the standing skip premise wrong and proved a census can carry a rule-8 arm — that correction is recorded in QUEUE.md beside the note, and the items above are skipped for THIS run, not ruled out.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (rule 4; the queue names both):** GROSS RUNG {0.75, 0.65, 0.55, 0.45, 0.35} x MIN HOLD {5, 21, 63, 126} = 20 points, **all reported for both kinds**, over 2 panels x 3 mechanisms x 4 rebalance grids = **1,080 book arms** plus a 400-arm gross-matched null. 10 bps, next-day execution, N=20, max_vol 0.60, gross-to-cash (no leverage). The DD-matched gross solved by bisection is a DERIVED quantity reported beside the rung-wise match, not a third dial.

  **(1) THE ANSWER, AND WHY 1065's REBATE DOES NOT SURVIVE THE RIGHT COMPARAND.** Against HARD **de-grossed to the same |MaxDD|** — which keeps 100% of its own turnover rebate and gives the drawdown back for free — SOFT(R_GATE) reads **d_CAGR +0.0019 (median +0.0000, wins 0.594), d_Sharpe -0.0195 (wins 0.427), d_OOS_Sharpe -0.0121 (wins 0.490), d_turnover +1.896/yr, trading MORE at 95 of 96 cells.** By hold, SOFT wins CAGR at 0.583 / 0.792 / 0.667 / **0.333** at H = 5 / 21 / 63 / 126 and loses Sharpe at all four. Per panel U56 d_CAGR +0.0038 / d_OOS -0.0006, B136 -0.0001 / -0.0236. 1065's committed '69-77% of the rebate kept' is an artefact of scoring SOFT against HARD **at the same gross**.

  **(2) THE BYCATCH IS BIGGER THAN THE ANSWER: SHARPE IS GROSS-INVARIANT, SO 4b's DRAWDOWN LEG IS PURCHASABLE BY ANYONE.** **max|Sharpe(g) - Sharpe(0.75)| = 0.0087 across ALL 216 cells** over 0.75 -> 0.35, against CAGR moving up to 0.0909 and |MaxDD| up to 0.1490 (monotone at 216 of 216, G4). De-grossing is a pure drawdown-for-CAGR trade at a known price: **the DD leg fails at 0.676 of arms at g=0.75 and 0.000 at g=0.35.** Only the CAGR floor stops the purchase — **4b passes 64 / 74 / 62 / 0 / 0 arms at g = 0.75 / 0.65 / 0.55 / 0.45 / 0.35**, and L_CAGR is the most-failed leg at 567 of 1,080 (L_DD 295, L_H2 250, L_OOS 91, L_H1 10). **A min-hold tax priced against the same book at the same gross is priced against the wrong thing.**

  **(3) BOTH KEEP PATHS, THE NULL, AND RULE 8 READ ONCE.** **4a 0 of 1,080. 4b 200 of 1,080 — BOOK_H0 20, BOOK_HARD 92, BOOK_SOFT 88 — BOTH 0**: the release does not out-pass the hard book it was meant to improve. The gross-matched null passes 4b at 0.072 overall but **0.250 at g=0.65**, the same rung where books pass most (0.343), so a 4b PASS quoted there is worth less than it looks. Rule 8 (arm chosen on IS Sharpe ALONE per panel x mechanism, OOS read ONCE): **6 of 6 picks beat SPY on OOS Sharpe, 0 of 6 beat live RULES v2**, SOFT picked at 1 of 6; head-to-head against the best HARD arm whose IS |MaxDD| is no worse, **SOFT wins 3 of 6, mean d_OOS_Sharpe +0.0101.**

  **(4) THE DO-NOTHING CONTROL, AND THE SHARPEST SINGLE NUMBER IN THE RUN.** Against the H=0 book (**no min hold at all**) de-grossed to the same IS drawdown, the best SOFT arm wins OOS Sharpe at **13 of 22** cells (mean +0.0354, -4.57 turns/yr) — a coin flip. In the cell rule 8 actually picked, U56/CAND20/M at g=0.75, **doing nothing is strictly better: BOOK_H0 15.26% / 1.2123 / -19.51%, OOS 17.50% / 1.3059** against the SOFT pick's 14.30% / 1.1733 / -18.91%, OOS 15.53% / 1.2025, and the HARD arm's 14.71% / 1.0830 / -23.08%. Benchmarks: SPY U56 full 15.13% / 0.8848 / -33.72%, OOS 15.28% / 0.8745; RULES v2 full 8.62% / 1.2017 / -12.05%, OOS 9.47% / 1.2778. **Both 4b passes are a plain top-20 book — prior art, not a candidate.**

  **HYPOTHESES SCORED AS THEY FELL: 3 OF 5.** `H_MECHANISM` PASS but only on the weakest reading (CAGR wins 0.594 with a median of exactly zero); `H_OOS` **FAIL** (0.490); `H_TURNOVER` **FAIL** and badly (0.010); `H_GROSSINV` PASS; `H_DDLEG` PASS. The one PASS that matters for the mechanism is the one whose median is zero, and it is reported as such.

  **PUBLISHED, NOT REPAIRED — THREE DEFECTS, NONE OF THEM THIS RUN's TO HIDE.** **G7:** the queue's premise levels '15.58% -> 14.16% / MaxDD -21.68% at H=63' match **0** committed gross-0.75 arms — 15.58% is U56/CAND20/W HARD at **H=126**, and 14.16% / -21.68% appear in no arm; the real premise is 1065's (B) **medians over 24 cells** (dCAGR -0.0197 at H=63), which **G6 reproduces to 4.62e-04** at all four holds. **G2:** this script's `minhold_book` is **BIT-IDENTICAL** to 1065's own (max|dW| 0.0e+00 over 8 books), so the release tested here IS 1065's. **G2b:** on 1065's own tape end 8 of 9 committed U56/CAND20/W arms replay to <5e-5, but **`BOOK_SOFT/H=63` deviates 2.39e-03 in Sharpe and 1.29e-02/yr in turnover** while |MaxDD| agrees to 3.2e-07 — a defect in 1065's committed `.arms.csv`, not here, and it moves no verdict because every number above is re-derived. **G1:** `engine.backtest` itself returns 2 non-finite days on U56 (index 0 and 3, inside the 260-day warm-up); `nrun` returns none and agrees to 2.8e-17 on the other 4,705 days. **Gates 8 of 8 PASS.**

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B136 are current-constituent panels; every LEVEL is optimistic. The headline is a within-cell, matched-drawdown, same-dates contrast of one book against another, which a level bias common to the cell largely cancels out of. The 4a/4b PASS COUNTS and the rule-8 OOS levels are levels and carry the full bias.

  **NO RULES CHANGE. The record should take one sentence: R_GATE release is a more expensive way to buy a drawdown reduction that 15 percentage points of gross gives away for free, so the min-hold drawdown tax must be priced against a de-grossed book and never against the same book at the same gross.**

- 2026-09-18 (lane cloud, idea 1266 does-a-CORRELATION-BRAKE-see-the-DRAWDOWN-that-a-VOLATILITY-BRAKE-was-BLIND-to) — **ANSWERED: NO. THE CORRELATION BRAKE SEES LESS OF THE BINDING DRAWDOWN THAN THE VOLATILITY BRAKE DOES, AND BOTH LOSE TO A FLAT GROSS CUT AT THE SAME MEAN EXPOSURE.** Verdict KILL (capital). No book, no RULES change, no PROTOCOL edit, no memo. 240 cells, 12 of 13 gates, 66s, offline, deterministic.

  **PROVENANCE, STATED FIRST.** This run wrote no new script. Lane B committed `2026-09-18_does-a-CORRELATION-BRAKE-...-_B.py` COMPLETE on 2026-09-18 04:40Z but produced NO NUMBERS: the sandbox's PyPI egress returned 503 and pandas would not install, so lane B logged the block and asked the next lane to run the script unchanged. This lane did exactly that — `pip install pandas` succeeded without incident (pandas 3.0.6), so the block was transient egress and not the idea. The script is byte-identical to lane B's commit; only its artefacts are new. Idea 1266 was therefore taken ahead of QUEUE's '## Open' head (1259, 1245, 1246, 1236, 1222 ... are record-audit items that yield no weights function), because an already-claimed, already-written, price-only script one `pip install` away from its grid is strictly the first eligible piece of work in the file.

  **THE LITERAL QUESTION, AND THE ANSWER IS THE OPPOSITE OF THE PREMISE.** The brake's premise was that a portfolio drawdown is a correlation event before it is a volatility event. On the only drawdown that binds 4b's DD leg — U56/COMPOSITE3, peak 2020-02-19, trough 2020-03-16, -19.13% — the mean brake intensity u INSIDE the episode reads **CORR 0.129 / 0.000 / 0.000 / 0.000 at LOOK 20 / 60 / 126 / 252 against VOL 0.739 / 0.473 / 0.245 / 0.164**, and **CORR never once reaches u >= 0.5 at any lookback** while VOL first arms on 2020-03-02. In the 42 rows BEFORE the peak both read 0.000 at every lookback, so neither brake is early; the difference is entirely that VOL reacts during the fall and CORR does not. Pooled over the 96 matched (panel, signal, LOOK, SLOPE) pairs, CORR minus VOL is **MaxDD -1.83pp (CORR better at only 20 of 96)** with CAGR +1.61pp and Sharpe +0.0222 — correlation keeps more CAGR only because it arms less often (on% 46.0 mean vs VOL's 64.1), which is a dose difference and not a timing insight. The two intensity series have mean rank correlation +0.495 (range +0.147..+0.726) over the 24 (panel, signal, LOOK) cells, so outcome (D) — "the same brake" — is only half right: they are correlated, but where they differ, VOL is the one looking at the trough.

  **CAPITAL, RULE 8, OOS READ ONCE.** (LOOK, SLOPE) chosen on warm-up..2016-12-31 by IS Sharpe alone, 2017-2026 read once, per (panel, signal, brake) = 12 choosers. **Chooser-minus-do-nothing OOS Sharpe: mean -0.1655, positive at 0 of 12.** CORR mean -0.1951 with **mean IS/OOS rank correlation -0.351** (the IS argmax is anti-predictive: it picks SLOPE 1.00 at 3 of 6); VOL mean -0.1358, rank corr +0.228. The incumbent's own do-nothing OOS Sharpe is 1.1832 (U56/COMPOSITE3) against SPY's 0.8747 and live v2's 1.2781. 4a passes **0 of 240**. 4b passes 83 of 240 and **every one of the 49 cells that converts a 4b FAIL into a PASS is also converted by that cell's own gross-matched flat control** — the conversion is exposure, not the brake.

  **THE CONTROL THAT KILLS IT, AND THE ONE CHANNEL THAT IS REAL.** Against a FLAT schedule held at that cell's own realised mean gross, the correlation brake is worse on every axis that matters: d_CAGR -0.75pp, d_Sharpe -0.0449 (>0 at 19 of 96), d_MaxDD -0.05pp (>0 at 34 of 96), **d_OOS Sharpe -0.0992, positive at 3 of 96**. The volatility twin is worse still (-1.18pp / -0.0668 / -0.22pp / -0.1030). Against the TIMING PLACEBO (same intensity series permuted, seeds 11/23/37) both brakes ARE positive — CORR +0.0215 Sharpe (>0 at 65 of 96) and +0.91pp MaxDD (>0 at 59 of 96), VOL +0.0282 and +1.32pp — so a genuine timing channel exists in both. It is simply smaller than the CAGR that de-grossing costs, which is the whole result: **the brake beats a random schedule of the same shape and still loses to a constant one.** Dial structure is monotone in the unhelpful direction (CORR d_Sharpe vs flat: -0.0047 / -0.0210 / -0.0522 / -0.1017 at SLOPE 0.25 / 0.50 / 0.75 / 1.00), so there is no interior cell to park.

  **GATES, INCLUDING THE ONE THAT FAILS.** 12 of 13. G1 vintage-pinned replay of the committed 2026-09-04 anchor 15.7147% / 1.1480 / -19.1276% at err **5.97e-05**; G2 (1257's M12_1) 2.26e-05; G3 SLOPE 0 LOOK- and brake-invariant, G5 causality, G7 zero gross at a new high, G8 flat control identity, G9 determinism all exactly 0.000e+00; G4 realised gross in [0, 0.7500]; G11 statistic in [-0.0324, 0.7796], 53 undefined windows per lookback, 0 columns dropped for listing gaps. **G12 FAILS and is published rather than toleranced:** the placebo's realised mean gross differs from the real brake's by up to **1.746e-02**, because permuting the intensity series does not permute the weeks in which the book holds nothing, so the placebo is a close but not exact gross match. The headline does not rest on it — it rests on the flat control and on rule 8, neither of which the failure touches.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B135 are current-constituent lists and SMALL663 is a current sub-$2B screen (52 of 715 names dropped for max_1d_move >= 1.0 before anything is computed). Every level is optimistic and every 4b pass an upper bound. The direction that is NOT neutral is stated: a current-constituent panel has been cleaned of the names whose correlations spiked hardest before they delisted, so the correlation signal measured here is MUTED and the anchor's drawdown FLATTERED — the brake is given the benefit of that doubt and still loses.

- 2026-09-18 (lane B, idea 1265 should-a-published-4b-PASS-carry-its-DISTANCE-FROM-THE-BEST-CELL-IN-ITS-OWN-GRID) — **THE DISTANCE IS REAL AND LARGE: THE MEDIAN COMMITTED 4b PASS SITS 2.49 PERCENTAGE POINTS OF CAGR BEHIND THE BEST CELL IN ITS OWN GRID AND ONLY 0.133 OF PASSES ARE THAT CELL — AND ACTING ON THE NUMBER COSTS -0.0293 OF MEAN OOS SHARPE, LOSING TO DOING NOTHING AND TO A COIN.** Verdict KILL (capital). No book, no RULES change, no PROTOCOL edit, no memo. 10 of 10 gates, all 36 census cells and all 108 ARM B books published, 256s, offline, deterministic.

  **WHY THIS IDEA AND NOT A FALLBACK, AND WHY THE STANDING ELIGIBILITY NOTE WAS WRONG.** 1265 was the LAST item in QUEUE.md's '## Open' and the lane rule says lane B claims the last. Three prior runs skipped the bookkeeping items on the premise that a census cannot produce step-3 deliverables. That premise fails twice. **(i) The grids do not have to be re-run.** The record commits its grids as artefacts: **1,005 committed csv files carry an explicit 4b verdict column beside a CAGR and a Sharpe column** (649 `.grid.csv` among them), 920 with at least one PASS — "recover its own grid where the script survives" is a file read, not a backtest. **(ii) The clause is a chooser, so it can be priced.** ARM B runs it as one, under rule 8, with both KEEP paths at every cell. No new ideas were filed and nothing was left unclaimed.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (rule 4; the queue names both):** PASS SET {PS_GRID = `*.grid.csv` only (headline), PS_LEADER = artefacts cited in LEADERBOARD.md's script column, PS_ALL = every artefact with a 4b column} x DISTANCE STATISTIC {D_CAGR = best minus pass in pp (headline), D_SHARPE, D_RANK = the share of the frame strictly better on CAGR}. **9 cells, every one published at both FRAMES and both REFERENCES = 36 rows in `.census.csv`.** FRAME and REFERENCE are controls, never dials and never chosen on: FRAME {F_FILE = the whole artefact, F_GROUP = the artefact partitioned by its own low-cardinality key columns, i.e. the sub-grid a cell was actually reported beside}; REFERENCE {B_ALL = best cell whatever its verdict, B_PASS = best cell among the frame's own PASSES, the only one a clause could print without citing a cell its author discarded}.

  **(1) THE ANSWER, AND THE CAVEAT IS PUBLISHED AS LOUDLY AS THE HEADLINE.** Headline PS_GRID x D_CAGR x F_GROUP x B_ALL: **46,216 committed 4b passes over 8,616 frames in 320 artefacts, median 2.4898pp behind**, mean 3.7183, p90 8.4292, worst 57.62pp; **0.7499 sit >= 1pp behind, 0.5855 >= 2pp, 0.2262 >= 5pp**, and **0.1330 ARE the best-CAGR cell in their frame.** So 1264's 5.08pp grid is wide but not rare — it is about the 77th percentile. At B_PASS the median is **1.1015pp** with 0.1906 exactly zero. **AND IT IS A LARGE-GRID FACT:** weighting frames instead of rows (median over the 8,616 frames of each frame's own median) gives **0.5289pp** with per-frame share-is-best **0.4574** — in the typical frame half the passes ARE the best cell, and the row-weighted headline is carried by the minority of grids with hundreds of cells (mean frame 289). Both weightings are in the artefact; neither is a dial, and the frame-weighted reading is the one a schema clause should be argued on.

  **(2) THE DIAL IS INERT AND THE SHARPE/CAGR ASYMMETRY IS THE SHARPEST NUMBER IN THE CENSUS.** D_CAGR median 2.4898 / 1.8958 / 1.9323 and D_SHARPE 0.0539 / 0.0782 / 0.0748 at PS_GRID / PS_LEADER / PS_ALL; share-is-best 0.1330 / 0.1198 / 0.1224 — the pass set moves the level by ~0.6pp and changes no sign and no ordering. `H_SHARPE` HELD: the SAME passes sit at **median rank-shortfall 0.4372 on CAGR against 0.3207 on Sharpe**, i.e. a committed pass is measurably further from the top of its own grid on RETURN than on risk-adjusted return. That is exactly what a test built from three Sharpe legs against SPY, one drawdown cap and one CAGR FLOOR must produce, and it is the defensible form of 1264's complaint. `H_WIDE` HELD (2.4898 >= 1.00pp), `H_NOTBEST` HELD (0.1330 < 0.50).

  **(3) RULE 8 KILLS THE CLAUSE AS A CHOOSER, AND THE MECHANISM IS THE FILTER RATHER THAN THE STATISTIC.** ARM B walks the frozen 2026-09-04 book's two structural dials — N {10,12,16,20,25,30} x H {21,42,63,126,189,252}, **108 books on U56 / B135 / SMALL663, every cell in `.grid.csv`** — holding everything else at the committed construction (RAW three-leg composite, above-200d AND vol20 < 0.60, GROSS 0.75, weekly Fri-decide/Mon-trade, equal slot weights, 10 bps, t+1, 260-row warm-up). The clause is run as C_4bCAGR: among the cells passing an **IS-ONLY 4b** (both IS halves beat SPY's own IS halves, MaxDD >= 0.60x SPY's IS MaxDD, CAGR >= 0.70x SPY's IS CAGR — no post-2016 data of any kind, gate G8), take the best IS CAGR. Chosen on warm-up..2016-12-31, 2017-2026 read ONCE. **Mean OOS Sharpe: do-nothing 0.8869; C_4bCAGR 0.8576 (-0.0293); C_4bSHARPE 0.8547 (-0.0322); count-matched RANDOM 0.8945 (+0.0076); C_ISSHARPE 0.9831; C_ISCAGR 1.0370.** `H_CAPITAL` HELD and more sharply than pre-declared (<= +0.02 predicted, -0.0293 realised). **THE FILTER IS WHAT COSTS:** on U56 an unfiltered IS-CAGR argmax picks (10, 63) for OOS **1.2060 (+0.0228)**; requiring an IS-4b pass first DELETES that cell and forces (20, 189) for **1.0530 (-0.1301)**. Per panel C_4bCAGR reads **-0.1301 / +0.0423 / +0.0000** (SMALL663 has 0 of 36 IS-4b passes, so it cannot move). **Doing nothing is the only chooser holding a full-4b pass among its picks — 1 of 3 against 0 of 3 for every mover**, the record's standing result reproduced a fifth time. IS->OOS rank corr on Sharpe +0.1972 / -0.3637 / +0.0486.

  **(4) C_ISCAGR's +0.1501 IS REPORTED AND DELIBERATELY NOT PROMOTED.** The pooled gain is **entirely SMALL663 (+0.3896)**, the panel where 0 of 36 cells pass 4b and every cell fails the drawdown leg; on the two panels where a book is investable it is **+0.0228 and +0.0378 — two draws**, which 1206 and 1183 have both shown is not a measurement. It is recorded here and nothing is built on it.

  **(5) BOTH KEEP PATHS, AND THE DRAWDOWN LEG FOR THE SIXTH DIAL RUNNING.** **4a 0 of 108** (live v2's -12.05% MaxDD is not beatable by a growth book — rule 4b's own reason for existing). **4b 11 of 108: U56 9 of 36, B135 2 of 36, SMALL663 0 of 36.** Of the 97 failures **the DD leg fails at 97 — every single one** (H2 40, OOS 38, H1 29, CAGR 21 alongside it). Phase (1253), years (1254), names (1255), signal (1257), sizing (1264) and now the N x H frame itself: six dials saying the committed pass is a statement about drawdown and nothing else. **1264's blindness finding reproduces on an independent grid — U56's 9 passes span 12.24%..17.66% CAGR (5.42pp) and 1.0576..1.1699 of full Sharpe under ONE verdict string.** **RECORDED, NOT PROMOTED:** the best U56 4b cell is **N=12 / H=126 at 17.66% / 1.1699 / -20.17%, halves 1.2777/1.0879, OOS 1.1747, turnover 2.92/yr** — ahead of the committed anchor by +1.88pp of CAGR and +0.0177 of full Sharpe, **BEHIND it out of sample (1.1747 vs 1.1832)**, and reached by no honest chooser in the run. Benchmarks recomputed on this tape: **U56 SPY 15.13% / 0.8849 / -33.72%, OOS 0.8747; U56 LIVE v2 8.62% / 1.2018 / -12.05%, OOS 1.2781; B135 SPY 15.16% / 0.8862, OOS 0.8769; SMALL663 SPY 14.06% / 0.8582, OOS 0.8769.**

  **BYCATCH: THE RUN RE-ENACTED 1230's REFLEXIVITY DEFECT BY ACCIDENT AND THE FIX IS A GATE, NOT A TOLERANCE.** The script's own `.census.csv` and `.passes.csv` land in the directory it scans, so the first execution read **1,005 artefacts and the re-run read 1,006**. Gate **G0** now excludes the run's own stem, the population is the record AS IT STOOD BEFORE this run, and re-execution leaves it unchanged. Any census in this record that scans `research/backtests/` has the same defect unless it says otherwise.

RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.

ARM B's U56 and B135 are current-constituent lists and SMALL663 is a current sub-$2B screen (52 of 715 dropped for max_1d_move >= 1.0 before anything was computed); every absolute level is optimistic and every 4b pass is an UPPER bound. Per 1255 the U56 committed pass dies at k ~ 12 deleted ex-post winners.

  **WHAT THE RECORD SHOULD TAKE, IN ONE SENTENCE: 4b's blindness to return is real, measurable at a median 2.49pp of CAGR row-weighted and 0.53pp frame-weighted, and worth printing beside every pass — but it is NOT a filter, because screening on it out of sample deletes the very cell it points at and costs -0.0293 of Sharpe against doing nothing.**

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (rule 4; the queue names both):** CLAIM SET {CS_BLOCK, CS_RESAMPLE, CS_ALL} — 1243's own three scopes intersected with its unrecoverable test — x RESOLUTION RULE {R_CONST, R_LADDER, R_ANY, R_UNION}, **12 cells, every one in `.recovery.csv`**. R_CONST = a module-level assignment (AST, tuple unpacking included) whose target is in the declared L-NAME SET and whose RHS is one integer literal in [1, 5000]; R_LADDER (headline) adds list/tuple literals of such integers, because a script that WALKS L is the strongest conformance with 1208's clause, not a failure; R_ANY adds `L=<int>` keywords, `def f(..., L=<int>)` defaults and literals at block-resampling call sites; R_UNION adds the sibling prose of the emitting script's own artefact family. Nested by construction on every script (G4) and the claim sets nested too (G5). ATTRIBUTION IS NOT A DIAL: four paths run on every unit in fixed precedence and each path's marginal and standalone reach is published.

  **(1) ATTRIBUTION REACHES 0.8799 AND THE 0.90 BAR IS REPORTED FAILED, NOT MOVED.** Per claim set: **0.7857 / 0.8799 / 0.9471**. Source-level and deliberately generous: **650 of 728 SILENT units (0.8929) resolve to a script with no block-resampling construct anywhere in it.** Per claim set **0.3400 (17 of 50) / 0.8929 (650 of 728) / 0.9633 (6,223 of 6,460)** — the over-binding is a property of the WIDE SCOPE, not of the record's prose, which is why CS_BLOCK, the units that actually name a block construction, reads 0.34. **THE 1,074 THEREFORE DECOMPOSES AS: 217 REPAIRABLE BY EDIT AT ZERO DRAWS, 650 NEVER IN SCOPE, 78 THAT GENUINELY LOST AN L THEIR OWN CODE RAN, 129 NOBODY CAN ATTRIBUTE.**

- 2026-09-17 (lane cloud, idea 1255 how-much-of-the-2026-09-04-KEEP-4b-BOOK-is-the-HANDFUL-OF-NAMES-THE-SCREEN-ALREADY-KNOWS-WON) — **RULE 9 PRICED FOR THE FIRST TIME: THE COMMITTED 4b PASS IS ABOUT SIX NAMES WIDE, AND THE PANEL'S LEAK IS THE WINNERS THAT HAD NOT WON YET IN 2016.** Verdict KILL (capital). No RULES change, no book promoted, no PROTOCOL edit. 9 of 9 gates, 45s, offline, deterministic.

  **WHAT WAS RUN.** The frozen 2026-09-04 candidate REBUILT FROM SCRATCH on a panel with the k ex-post best names DELETED ENTIRELY — composite ranks, eligibility screen, min-hold clock and every top-N slot recomputed without them, not merely un-selected. k {0,1,2,3,5,8,12} x deletion rule {TOTRET = highest full-sample total return, CONTRIB = highest realised contribution to this book} x basis {FULL, IS} x 3 panels = **84 cells, ALL published**. G3/G4: the k=0 cell reproduces the committed anchor at **1.1480 / -19.13%** exactly, so this prices the committed number and not a relative. SPY is never a panel constituent, so the 4b bars (MaxDD >= -20.23%, CAGR >= 10.54% on U56) do not move with k — which is what makes the ladder readable.

  **THE LADDER.** 4a passes **0 of 84**. 4b passes **14 of 28** U56 cells, 3 of 28 on B136, 0 of 28 on SMALL663. U56 mean full-sample CAGR runs **15.71 / 14.58 / 13.56 / 13.18 / 11.69 / 10.87 / 9.61%** at k = 0/1/2/3/5/8/12 — about **1.1 pp of CAGR per deleted name over the first three** — and crosses 4b's own CAGR floor between k = 8 and k = 12. The deletion orders are the obvious ones: **AVGO, NVDA, TSLA, NFLX, AMD, AAPL, AMZN** (TOTRET) and **NVDA, NFLX, AMD, AVGO, AAPL** (CONTRIB). The first leg to break is the DRAWDOWN CAP, as in idea 1253, and it is **NON-MONOTONE in k** (CONTRIB/FULL fails at k=2 and k=3, passes again at k=5, fails at k=8), so the smooth CAGR/Sharpe decay is the honest reading and the DD leg is again the fragile one.

  **RULE 8 IS THE FINDING.** With the deletion set chosen on **warm-up..2016-12-31 ONLY** — the k names a 2017 investor could know had already won — U56 OOS Sharpe barely moves: **1.1759 -> 1.1593 at k=12**, and the 4b OOS leg passes at **7 of 7 rungs on both large-cap panels**. With the set chosen on the FULL sample, so that it includes names whose runs happened AFTER 2017, the same k=12 costs **1.1759 -> 0.9414, a drop of 0.2346 — ten times larger**. **The price of a current-constituent panel is not that it holds past winners; it is that it was allowed to hold names that had not won yet in 2016 and went on to win by 2026.** No walk-forward INSIDE the panel can remove that, which is why rule 8 has never caught it.

  **AND IT IS A LARGE-CAP FACT, NOT A MECHANISM FACT.** On SMALL663 the relation INVERTS — deleting the ex-post winners RAISES OOS Sharpe (0.4534 -> 0.5303, rank slope +0.99) — so the concentration belongs to the U56/B136 lists, not to the book's rules.

  **PROPOSED for the Sunday review (rule 6) as wording only, never as a chooser:** rule 9's survivorship sentence should carry a NUMBER — the k at which the claim's own 4b pass dies — whenever a memo is written on a current-constituent panel. RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.

The true ex-ante panel would also hold losers this one never had, so every level printed remains optimistic even at k = 12. Follow-up standing: 1254 (calendar-year jackknife of the OOS window).

The unstated dial is about twice the margin the pass was won by.

On U56's weekday family the IS argmax is **Friday — the WORST of the five out of sample** (1.0868 against the anchor's 1.1759), IS/OOS rank correlation -0.50; across the nine rule-8 rows the rank correlations run -0.80 to +0.60 with no sign. - 2026-09-17 (lane C, idea 1240 does-the-ANCHOR-RANKING-being-PICK-FREE-make-every-SUBSTITUTION-STUDY-in-the-record-one-QUESTION) — **ANSWERED = NO. THE DEGENERACY IS A SPECIAL CASE, NOT A STRUCTURE: 3 of 36 PICK-SET PAIRS ARE ARITHMETICALLY INCAPABLE OF DIFFERING IN RANK, AND AGAINST THE RECORD'S OWN TEXT THE DEGENERATE SHARE IS 0.0101 / 0.0134 / 0.0159 — NOT "EVERY".** Verdict KILL (capital) / PREMISE REFUTED. No RULES change, no book promoted, no PROTOCOL edit, no memo. 9 of 9 gates.

  **THE IDENTITY IS CONFIRMED AND GENERALISED — WHICH IS WHAT KILLS THE PREMISE.** 1237's G9 read DELTA(A) == meanOOS(A) - meanOOS(picks) at ONE pick set. Written out in general, with cell = (panel, fold) and w_P(c) the share of P's picks landing in cell c, DELTA(A,P) = SUM_c w_P(c) S(A;c) - K(P), verified here at **8.05e-16** over all 9 x 19 (alternative, pick set) pairs (G5), with K(P) constant across alternatives to **1.33e-15** (G6). So the pick column IS an additive constant, exactly as 1237 found. But that same algebra names the EXACT criterion, and it is narrower than the fold-set version 1237 stated: **rank-degenerate <=> the contrast is MEAN-FORM *and* the two pick sets induce the SAME CELL-WEIGHT VECTOR w_P.** A pick set that weights cells differently (E1) or a contrast that is not mean-form (E2) is not degenerate, and both escapes are common.

  **THE CRITERION IS RARELY MET.** 9 rule-8-legal pick sets, built to span w (all-four-ladders, single-ladder N, the degenerate GROSS ladder, single-panel U56 and SMALL, early folds, OOS folds, a 5x U56 tilt, and the record's own 62 committed triples), x 19 distinct books x 3 panels x 14 folds. Of the 36 pairs, **3 share w** — so 3 are FORCED — and 4 are identical in fact (0.111). G7 confirms the criterion is sound in the direction that matters: **every forced pair IS identical, 0 misses.** Multiplied through the record's own mean-form share (0.0909 / 0.1210 / 0.1429 at S_STRICT / S_BROAD / S_ALL over 34,011 committed units read from 1,164 memo and result files plus LEADERBOARD rows and CHANGELOG paragraphs), the degenerate share is **0.0101 / 0.0134 / 0.0159**. The premise needs > 0.50; it misses by a factor of about thirty. Pre-declared outcome **(B) NOT ONE QUESTION** fires.

  **AND THE VERDICT DOES NOT TURN ON THE CENSUS'S WEAKEST JOINT.** 0.444-0.460 of every study set names NO ranking statistic at all, so its degeneracy is unknowable from its own text. Counting ALL of those as mean-form — the most generous reading the premise could ask for — still caps the degenerate share at **0.0670** (G8). The refutation survives the assignment either way.

  **1224 IS NOT REFUTED; ITS GENERALISATION IS.** G9 rebuilds 1224's pick set and replays its committed incumbent rank, **5 of 19**, bit for bit. What 1224 actually had was a stable BOOK, not a degenerate RANKING: across the 33 mean-form pairs whose w differs, the incumbent's own rank moves 0 places at **0.545** of them, while the FULL ranking survives at only **0.030**. Its three pick sets agreed about that one book; they did not agree about the ordering, and they were not forced to agree about either.

  **WHAT ACTUALLY MAKES THE RECORD'S SUBSTITUTION STUDIES AGREE IS THE CHOICE OF STATISTIC, AND IT IS WORTH 13 PLACES.** Hold w FIXED and walk dial 2 instead: 0 of 12 same-w non-mean pairs are rank-identical, and the incumbent goes **5 of 19 under C_MEAN to 18 of 19 under C_WIN** (max move 15 places). Median Kendall tau by contrast: C_MED 0.497, C_WIN 0.509, C_T 0.620, C_TRIM 0.725, C_MEAN 0.784. The record has been reading one statistic — the one under which its incumbent looks median rather than near-last — and the pick set was never the load-bearing choice. **THE EXPOSURE THE CENSUS ACTUALLY FOUND IS THE UNSTATED-STATISTIC SHARE (0.444-0.460), NOT THE DEGENERACY (0.010-0.016).**

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS.** 66 candidate books on both paths: 4a **0 of 66**; 4b full 17, 4b OOS 16, BOTH 15, collapsing to 12 distinct on 1211's realised-return key, all U56/B136 and all the standing 2026-09-04 candidate's neighbourhood (best U56 N=15, full 17.07% / 1.1675 / -20.14%, OOS 18.87% / 1.1894 / -20.14% against U56 SPY OOS 15.15% / 0.8686 / -33.72%). 88 rule-8 rows with every choice made on folds < 2017 and IS-window cell Sharpes ONLY, 2017-2026 read once: 4a **0 of 88**; 4b BOTH 9, collapsing to 3 books already committed. **EVERY CONTRAST LOSES TO DOING NOTHING** — mean rule-8 OOS Sharpe C_MEAN 0.8281, C_WIN 0.8461, C_MED 0.8501, C_TRIM 0.8501, C_T 0.8684 against the do-nothing anchor's 0.8845 — and the chooser names the incumbent at **0 of 85** rows. CONFIRMATORY, NOT GENERATIVE. NOT PROMOTED, NO MEMO, NO RULES CHANGE.
  Script: research/backtests/2026-09-17_does-the-ANCHOR-RANKING-being-PICK-FREE-make-every-SUBSTITUTION-STUDY-in-the-record-one-QUESTION_C.py

  **WHAT THE RECORD SHOULD TAKE.** Not that its substitution studies are one question — they are not. The defensible line, worth a future run's header: **A SUBSTITUTION STUDY IS RANK-DEGENERATE IF AND ONLY IF ITS CONTRAST IS MEAN-FORM AND ITS PICK SETS SHARE A CELL-WEIGHT VECTOR, AND THE RECORD MEETS BOTH CONDITIONS AT ABOUT ONE PER CENT OF ITS CLAIMS — WHILE FAILING TO STATE THE CONTRAST AT ALL IN NEARLY HALF OF THEM.** PROPOSED for the Sunday review (rule 6) as that sentence and nothing more.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 (55 names) and B136 (135) are CURRENT constituents; SMALL is the sub-$2B screen with 51 of 715 tickers dropped for max_1d_move >= 1.0 (664 investable), SPY excluded from every eligible set and used as benchmark only. The bias does not cancel out of the OOS levels or the 4b legs, so any pass there is an upper bound.

  **CENSUS REFLEXIVITY (defect 1230).** The 34,011-unit count is a claim about the tree as it stood when this run executed; committing this entry and its console log changes the tree the next census reads. The degenerate-share figures are ratios and are far from their 0.50 bar, so no plausible drift in the denominator reaches the verdict.

The substitution rule is FROZEN at S_ALL rather than dialled, because 1224 established S_SE1 / S_SE2 / S_ANC95 are decision-identical to it at 0 of 620 cells. P_TEXT is FROZEN at 1224's own 62 committed (panel, ladder, rung) triples, read from its committed `picks.csv.gz` rather than re-harvested, so this run prices the SAME 620 cells that produced the +0.0204 — gate G10 replays 1224's three committed deltas, SEs and both mean columns to **4.90e-05**. A live re-harvest runs alongside as a drift diagnostic only (1230): 33,861 -> 33,907 units over 1,160 -> 1,161 .md files, 62 triples, **62 shared, 0 new, 0 lost**.

  **THE SIGN CHANGES, AND NOT MARGINALLY.** DELTA(A) = OOS Sharpe(stand-in anchor A) - OOS Sharpe(the book the record picked), on P_TEXT: N=5 **-0.3292 (t -2.16)**, N=10 -0.0883, H=21 -0.0595, CADENCE=M -0.0488, H=63 +0.0049, the incumbent +0.0204, N=15 +0.0295, N=30 +0.0850, N=40 +0.0923, H=252 **+0.1105**. **The ONLY candidate in the whole ladder separable from zero at |t| >= 2 is N=5, and it is NEGATIVE** — the only resolved reading says tuning PAYS. 3 of 19 negative on P_AXIS, 1 of 19 on P_1214. The incumbent's own t is +1.94.

  **THE DECOMPOSITION THE QUEUE ASKS FOR IS NOT IDENTIFIED.** Mean-over-19 puts the SUBSTITUTION component at -0.0013 and hence **106.3%** of the +0.0204 on THIS anchor; median-over-19 puts it at +0.0177 and hence **13.2%**. The whole difference is one outlier book. Both are published and neither is the answer: a substitution/anchor split needs a stated candidate set AND a stated summary statistic before it means anything.

  **HALF THE CANDIDATE SET IS THE INCUMBENT RE-LEVERED, AND THAT IS MEASURED (G12).** The 9 non-incumbent GROSS rungs sit within **0.0040** of the incumbent's full-sample Sharpe on all three panels — 1189's degeneracy, Sharpe invariant to gross at a 0% cash rate with only a monotone cost drag surviving — while the smallest non-GROSS gap is **0.0171, 4.3x larger**. On the 9 GENUINELY DIFFERENT stand-ins the incumbent is rank **5 of 10, p 0.500 — exactly the median book** (mean -0.0226, median +0.0049, 4 negative); on the 9 near-clones it is rank 1 of 10, won by a cost drag.

Once the anchor is walked, "what does the record's tuning cost" is one question — which book do you name — wearing three pick sets' clothes. 1224's two strongest cuts survive and are shown to be anchor-free: N +0.1067 (t +3.07) against a 19-book mean of +0.1037, H -0.0594 against -0.0624. On SMALL the substitution LOSES at 16 of 19 anchors, so "stop tuning" is not sign-stable across panels either.

  **NO RULE-8-LEGAL PROCEDURE IN THIS TREE NAMES THE INCUMBENT.** A_IS (IS-argmax over all 22 candidates, warm-up..2016-12-31 only, held per panel) picks **N=40 / N=5 / H=252** on U56 / B136 / SMALL — the incumbent on none — and reads **+0.0028 (t +0.06)** on P_TEXT; A_ISFOLD, re-picked inside each fold's own IS window, reads **-0.0705 (t -1.14)**. This is not proof the anchor was fitted; it is the checkable narrower thing: the record's own IS-argmax rule does not recover it, and an anchor chosen that way is worth nothing or less. 151's do-nothing finding arriving from the anchor side.

Harvesting EVERY committed pick row in the record that states a resolution — 42 CSVs of 6,247 scanned, 2,255 rows with an explicit committed boolean (S_STRICT: `decided` 1,275, `resolved` 872, `decisive` 108) and 2,549 rows resolvable by the record's own published arithmetic (S_PROXY: MARGIN>0.013 1,238, GAP>FLOOR 1,007, GAP>LARGEST_UNRESOLVED 271, |T|>1.96 33) — gives 1,150 resolved mappable picks, of which **806 are non-anchor** (S_STRICT 462 of 634, S_PROXY 344 of 516). H_ANCHOR, declared as "0 in all 9 cells" before any number, is REFUTED.

H_LABEL, which predicted the label key would over-count non-anchor picks, is REFUTED: on the four mappable ladders a label difference IS a book difference here, so 1211's row-vs-book collapse does not operate on the identity question (it still operates on the COUNTS: 1,613 U56 pick rows collapse to 32 distinct destination books, a 50x row inflation, published in ARM 4).

So the queue's question has a yes answer that costs the record nothing to concede: the picks are different books that no ruler in the record can separate from doing nothing.

- 2026-09-17 (lane B, idea 1218 what-IS-the-right-NULL-for-a-LADDER-if-rung-DISPERSION-is-HALF-a-RUNG-s-OWN-SAMPLING-SE) — **ANSWERED = THE CORRELATION CORRECTION IS ARITHMETICALLY RIGHT AND PRACTICALLY USELESS. `d2_corr(k, rho) = d2(k) x sqrt(1-rho)` REPRODUCES THE REALISED RANGE WHEREVER rho IS KNOWN, IT DOES TURN 1205'S UNREACHABLE BAR INTO A REACHABLE ONE (R >= 1 AT 0 OF 12 REAL LADDERS, R_corr >= 1 AT 5 OF 12), AND IT DIVIDES OUT EXACTLY WHAT THE STATISTIC WAS DETECTING: DEGENERATE-VS-LIVE SEPARATION COLLAPSES FROM AUC 1.0000 TO 0.5185.** Verdict KILL (capital). No RULES change, no book promoted, no PROTOCOL edit.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (rule 4, and the queue names both):** `CORR DRIVER` {D_N, D_POOL, D_SYNTH} x `PANEL` {U56, B136, SMALL} = **9 cells, EVERY ONE PUBLISHED** in `.dialgrid.csv`; all 54 driver rungs x 2 SD bases x 2 k values (216 rows) are in `.corrladder.csv` regardless of what any cell did with them. NOT dials, reported at every value: the rung ladders (N 2->40, pool q 0.02->1.00, imposed rho 0.00->0.95); k {4, 8}; SD BASIS {S_IID, S_BLOCK(63)}; the record's four committed ladders {N, H, GROSS, CADENCE}; the 4a and 4b legs; the rule-8 arm.

  **THE ARITHMETIC, DECLARED BEFORE ANY NUMBER.** For k exchangeable draws with common sampling SD s and pairwise correlation rho the common factor cancels out of every difference, so E[max - min] = sqrt(1-rho) x d2(k) x s. The rho that matters is NOT the daily-return correlation but the cross-book correlation of the SHARPE ESTIMATOR, measured by a PAIRED bootstrap (one resampled day index applied to all k books at once); both are published at every rung and differ by at most 0.03 here.

  **Q1 — THE RATIO AS A FUNCTION OF CORRELATION.** Pooled mean R_corr **0.9858 (z -0.21)** on the 18 rho-imposed synthetic rungs and **0.9333 (z -0.98)** on the 18 real independent-null rungs; R falls with rho_S at 6 of 6 real (driver, panel) cells (Spearman -0.60 to -1.00). **1205's CTRL_LIVE re-measured carries rho_S 0.9582 / 0.9261 / 0.8350** against the 0.8248 that its R = 0.4186 implies if correlation is the whole story — so that shortfall IS correlation and not a defect of d2, and 0.4186 is not a ceiling but one draw from a cell whose rho this run states.

  **Q2 — THE BAR IS CLEARABLE AND BLIND.** GROSS (1189's degenerate dial) vs the live dials: raw range AUC 1.0000, raw R AUC 1.0000, **R_corr AUC 0.5185**. The correction multiplies raw R by 1/sqrt(1-rho_S) = **x167.5 / x157.5 / x129.0** on the three GROSS ladders against x2.7-x5.5 on the live ones, and at rho_S = 0.999964 a rho error of **2.7e-05** doubles R_corr — on a degenerate ladder the corrected statistic is a ratio of two numbers that are both ~0.

  **THE POWER FACT (post-hoc arm, declared as post-hoc).** R is a ONE-DRAW range statistic, so its own null SD is d3(k)/d2(k) = **0.75 / 0.52 / 0.43 / 0.29 / 0.27 / 0.24 at k = 2 / 3 / 4 / 8 / 9 / 12**; a true-null R_corr at k=8 sits inside [0.434, 1.566] 95% of the time. On the record's twelve committed ladders **|z| > 1.96 at 0 of 12** (worst 1.56). The pre-registered per-rung bar H_LAW (+-0.15) was therefore REFUTED on POWER, not on the law — G_LAW passes to 3.4e-03 over 20,000 replications. 4 of 6 pre-registered hypotheses landed as declared; H_DRIVER failed because D_POOL moves rho_S by only 0.042-0.165 (it is a degeneracy driver, not a correlation driver).

  **RULE 8 (mandatory) AND BOTH KEEP PATHS.** Five move-or-stay rules x 12 (panel, ladder) decisions, every bar read on the IS window only (warm-up..2016-12-31), 2017-2026 read once. Mean OOS Sharpe: do-nothing **0.8750** > C_CORR 0.8676 ~ C_CORRBAND 0.8675 > C_RAW 0.8496 > C_ALWAYS 0.8453, at move rates 0.00 / 0.17 / 0.08 / 0.42 / 0.92 — the corrected bar buys back most of what always-moving loses and still loses to holding the anchor. Best rule-8 book anywhere: U56 GROSS -> g 1.00, full 20.79% / 1.1387 / -24.93% (halves 1.2054/1.0940), OOS **22.65% / 1.1623 / -24.93%** against U56 SPY OOS 15.15% / 0.8684 / -33.72% and live RULES v2 OOS 9.42% / 1.2714 / -12.05%; it fails 4b on the drawdown cap (-24.93% against -20.23%). **4a 0 of 60. 4b 16 of 60 rows, which collapse to 4 DISTINCT books (rule 1211's lesson) and all four are the frozen U56 anchor re-selected by a do-nothing rule.** Neither R nor R_corr predicts what moving is worth (Spearman -0.27 / +0.15; mean move value -0.0296 Sharpe, positive at 5 of 12).

  **GATES, all 9 pass, printed before any result:** G_D2 d2(2) == 2/sqrt(pi) to 0; G_D2b d2(8) == 2.847 to 2.0e-04; G_D3 d3(2) == 0.8525 to 2e-03; G_LAW E[range]/d2 == sqrt(1-rho) on equicorrelated normals to 3.4e-03 at the worst rho; G1 fast runner == engine.backtest to 1.4e-17; G2 book() == that path to 2.8e-17; G3 determinism exactly 0; G4 two null books are not the same path (|corr| 0.954); G5 a paired bootstrap of a book against itself reads rho_S == 1 exactly.

  **WHAT THIS RUN DOES NOT SHOW.** The correction assumes EXCHANGEABLE rungs (one common rho) and uses only the mean pairwise correlation, so R_corr on a real ladder is an approximation whose error is not quantified here; rho_S is itself a bootstrap estimate and at rho -> 1 its error dominates. Twelve real ladders, 54 driver rungs, one tape, k <= 12. SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9): U56 and B136 are current-constituent lists and SMALL is the current sub-$2B screen less the documented max_1d_move >= 1.0 exclusion (52 of 715 dropped), so every CAGR/drawdown LEVEL and every 4b count is an upper bound; the ratios R, R_corr and rho are one construction against itself and the bias very largely cancels out of them.

- 2026-09-17 (cloud lane, idea 1225 is-a-PAIRED-IID-BOOTSTRAP-the-RIGHT-SE-for-a-difference-between-books-correlated-at-0.90) — **ANSWERED = YES, IT IS THE RIGHT SE, AND THE QUEUE'S PREMISE IS BACKWARDS. A PAIRED DIFFERENCE BETWEEN BOOKS CORRELATED AT 0.93 IS MILDLY MEAN-REVERTING, NOT PERSISTENT, SO A BLOCK SE IS *SMALLER* THAN THE IID ONE AT 526 OF 567 (PAIR, RUNG) POINTS. HIGH CORRELATION IS THE REASON THE IID SE IS SAFE HERE, BECAUSE PAIRING REMOVES THE COMMON FACTOR, WHICH IS THE PERSISTENT PART.** Verdict KILL (capital). No RULES change, no book promoted, no PROTOCOL edit (rule 6); RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, engine.py, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched. SELECTION: this lane takes the LAST open idea; 1225 was last in '## Open' and is not EDGAR / Form 4 / 8-K / options / spin-off / live-data. Price leg of 84 real rung books over 3 panels, 81 measured pairs, 1,680 fold move-or-stay decisions, 120 stitched deployable curves and 120 single-split rule-8 rows, so it carries this run's mandatory rule-8 walk-forward and both KEEP paths.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (rule 4, and the queue names both):** `PAIR SET` {P_N, P_H, P_GROSS, P_CADENCE, P_FAR} x `L LADDER` {1, 5, 10, 21, 42, 63, 126, 252} = **40 cells per panel, 120 in all, EVERY ONE PUBLISHED** in `.cells.csv` and `.walkforward.csv`; all 81 individual pairs are published at all 8 rungs in `.pairs.csv` / `.cells.csv` regardless of what any cell did with them. NOT dials, reported at every value: PANEL {U56, B136, SMALL} (rule 9); the two statistics (mean daily difference, Sharpe difference), both published at every rung and neither selected on; the 14 expanding-IS folds. 10 bps, t+1 execution, weekly, 260-row warm-up, anchor N=20 / H=126 / gross 0.75 / W, B = 800 with a crc32 seed per (panel, L), tape pinned at 2026-09-15 (idea 1160's price-vintage defect carried, not absorbed).

  **THE IDENTITIES, DECLARED BEFORE ANY NUMBER.** A moving-block bootstrap at L = 1 IS the IID bootstrap bit for bit (1162's identity, G6), so the ladder's first rung carries no information and every ratio at L = 1 is exactly 1.000 by construction (G8). Newey-West at L = 1 IS the textbook IID SE of the mean (G7). And rho(A,B) high says nothing on its own about Var(d): pairing REMOVES the common factor, which is the whole point of a paired SE. This run therefore never claimed that correlation breaks an IID SE; it measured what is LEFT after pairing.

  **Q1 — WHAT IS LEFT AFTER PAIRING.** Over 81 real anchor-vs-rung pairs: rho mean **0.9298** (median 0.9488, min 0.6520, max 1.0000; 59 of 81 at rho >= 0.90; P_GROSS is exactly 1.0000 because a gross rung is a scalar multiple, idea 1162's result). The constituent books carry ac1 -0.0394 / -0.0457 and their DIFFERENCE carries **ac1 -0.0451, VR(5) 0.9560, VR(21) 0.8397, VR(63) 0.7148** — every variance ratio BELOW one, at every pair set and every panel. The difference is mildly mean-reverting.

  **Q2 — THE SE LADDER.** Against the bar pre-registered before any number (SE_block/SE_iid >= 1.10), the IID SE survives the whole ladder at **79 of 81 pairs** on the mean-difference statistic and **78 of 81** on the Sharpe-difference statistic; the three exceptions are single P_H / P_N pairs whose worst ratio reaches 1.14. The sign is the real result: the ratio sits **below 1 at 526 of 567 (pair, rung) points** (mean statistic) and 499 of 567 (Sharpe), bottoming near 0.38 for U56 P_GROSS at L = 252. A block SE is not a correction here, it is a loosening.

  **THE RE-PRICING.** Simulating the record's own publish decision (|t| >= 1.96) on all 81 pairs at every rung: 28 of 81 clear on the mean difference under the IID SE and **no block rung strips a single one** (0 of 28 at every L); 1 of 81 clears on the Sharpe difference and the worst rung, L = 21, strips that one. Movement runs the other way — blocks ADD up to 6 certifications the IID SE withheld (SHARPE_DIFF at L = 252: 7 clearing against 1). Cross-read: 1219 found 82 of 86 recoverable committed t's use an IID SE and 4 a block one; on this evidence that 82 is not the defect it looked like for differences of this shape.

  **Q3 + RULE 8 (mandatory) AND BOTH KEEP PATHS.** The decision an SE feeds in this record is MOVE-OR-STAY, so both are priced. Over 14 expanding-IS folds the mean move rate climbs 0.2857 -> 0.4524 as L goes 1 -> 252 and buys nothing: mean stitched Sharpe 0.8906 (IID) against 0.8976 at the best rung, both **below the do-nothing anchor's 0.9026**. On the single 2009-2016 / 2017-2026 split the chooser moves at 33 of 120 cells for a mean OOS Sharpe of 0.8729 against SPY's 0.8749 and live RULES v2's 0.9807. Best stitched cell anywhere: U56 / P_CADENCE / L = 252 at move rate 0.00 — that is, the anchor — stitched window 16.48% / 1.2116 / -19.13% (H1 1.21 / H2 1.23). Best rule-8 single-split pick anywhere: U56 / P_N / L = 126 -> N12/H126/gross 0.75/W, **OOS 18.89% / 1.1759 / -20.17%** against U56 SPY OOS 15.21% / 0.8711 / -33.72% and live RULES v2 OOS 9.46% / 1.2763 / -12.05%. **4a passes 0 of 120. 4b passes 24 of 120 — all on U56, 8 of them at move rate 0 (they ARE the committed anchor book) and the rest stitched curves over N and H rungs the record has already walked — so no cell here is a new rule and no 4b memo is owed.**

  **GATES, all 10 pass, printed before any result:** G1 fast runner == engine.backtest to 1.4e-17; G2 the committed U56 W/H126/N=20 triple to 4.1e-05 (bar 5e-3); G3 SPY OOS triple to 1.7e-04; G4 live RULES v2 MaxDD == -12.05% to 4.9e-05; G5 SMALL determinism exactly 0 on two uncached rebuilds; G6 the moving-block index at L=1 IS the iid index bit for bit; G7 Newey-West at L=1 == the iid SE of the mean to 1e-12; G8 every L=1 ratio is exactly 1.000; G9 Newey-West(L) and the block bootstrap agree within a factor 1.5 on the mean at all 648 (pair, rung) points; G10 the 14 fold partitions are contiguous and non-overlapping in all 3 panels.

  **WHAT THIS RUN DOES NOT SHOW.** It measures 81 REAL anchor-vs-rung pairs on this tape, not the 86 committed t's themselves: 1219 established what SE those used, this run establishes what the SE should be for differences of the same SHAPE, so the re-pricing leg simulates the record's decision rule rather than re-auditing its sentences. The 1.10 bar is pre-registered but arbitrary and the whole ratio curve is published so any other bar reads off the same table. A moving-block bootstrap is itself L-dependent and has its own edge effects, which is exactly the regress the queue names; Newey-West is published beside it. SURVIVORSHIP: U56, B136 and SMALL are current-constituent lists; SMALL is 663 names plus SPY after dropping the 52 of 715 tickers in data/small_meta.csv with max_1d_move >= 1.0, from a pool rebuilt on 2026-09-11 (idea 706/1072), so every small-cap figure here is an upper bound. Script: `research/backtests/2026-09-17_is-a-PAIRED-IID-BOOTSTRAP-the-RIGHT-SE-for-a-difference-between-books-correlated-at-0.90_cloud.py`.

- 2026-09-17 (cloud lane, idea 1228 does-the-RECORD-read-its-CHOOSER-RESULTS-off-a-LISTING-NOBODY-CHOSE) — **ANSWERED = YES, ON BOTH LEGS. 0.6120 OF THE RECORD'S CLASSIFIABLE COMMITTED CHOOSER SENTENCES QUOTE A LISTING MAXIMUM RATHER THAN A WALK-FORWARD PICK, AND ON 60 REAL DIAL CELLS THAT FORM OVERSTATES THE DEPLOYABLE STITCH BY A MEAN +0.0753 OF SHARPE — BUT IT IS FOLD-CLUSTERED-SIGNIFICANT AT ONLY 9 OF 60.** Verdict KILL (capital). No RULES change, no book promoted, no PROTOCOL edit (rule 6); RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, engine.py, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched. Price leg of 72 real rung books over 3 panels, 840 fold picks, 180 stitched deployable curves and 60 rule-8 rows, so it carries this run's mandatory rule-8 walk-forward and both KEEP paths.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (rule 4, and the queue names both):** `CLAIM CLASS` {K_N, K_H, K_GROSS, K_CADENCE, K_ALL4} x `LISTING STATISTIC` {S_SHARPE, S_CAGR, S_MAR, S_4BMARGIN} = **20 cells per panel, 60 in all, EVERY ONE PUBLISHED** in `.readings.csv`. The RUNG inside a listing is not a third dial — it is the object a chooser selects — and all 24 rungs per panel are published in `.grid.csv` regardless of what any reading did with them. NOT dials, reported at every value: PANEL {U56, B136, SMALL} (rule 9), the 14 expanding-IS folds, and the L252 rolling-IS variant (run once as G9 and published, never selected on). 10 bps, t+1 execution, weekly, 260-row warm-up, anchor N=20 / H=126 / gross 0.75 / W, tape pinned at 2026-09-15 (idea 1160's price-vintage defect carried, not absorbed).

  **THE IDENTITY, DECLARED BEFORE ANY NUMBER (G6, 0 violations).** When the listing statistic IS the evaluation statistic, max-over-the-listing dominates every member by construction on any tape. This run therefore claims nothing about the SIGN of the gap; the content is its SIZE, its behaviour when the two statistics differ, and the census share.

  **Q1 CENSUS.** Corpus = 1,157 committed files (LEADERBOARD.md, CHANGELOG.md, 1,155 backtest memos), 121,366 candidate sentences, 11,300 carrying a chooser verb. Classification is a published regex rule applied in order, not a judgement: WF if a walk-forward marker is present (2,487), else LIST if a superlative-over-a-set marker is present (3,922), else OTHER (4,891, unclassifiable from text alone). LIST share = **0.6120** of the classifiable ones, 0.3471 of all chooser sentences; both denominators are printed. Only 870 of 3,922 LIST-form claims (0.2218) name a ladder family, so 0.7782 of them cannot be priced from their own text at all — which is itself the finding the record should act on.

  **Q2 PRICE.** Mean GAP_curve (listing max minus walk-forward stitch) **+0.0753** of Sharpe, median +0.0680, max +0.2409 (SMALL / K_H / S_SHARPE: 0.7629 against 0.5219). Positive at 44 of 60 cells; **fold-clustered |t| > 2 at only 9 of 60**, so the gap is real on average and unresolved cell by cell on 14 folds of this tape. Ordered by statistic: S_SHARPE +0.1253 > S_MAR +0.0896 > S_CAGR +0.0793 >> S_4BMARGIN +0.0071 — the margin statistic, which is the one 4b actually uses, is the one whose listing maximum is almost honest. By claim class: K_H +0.1076 > K_ALL4 +0.0956 > K_N +0.0883 > K_CADENCE +0.0574 > K_GROSS +0.0277 (K_GROSS is near zero because that ladder is near-degenerate in Sharpe, idea 1189's result, so its maximum and its pick are nearly the same book).

  **THE SHARPER READING: THE TWO FORMS NAME DIFFERENT BOOKS.** The rule-8 pick equals the listing maximum at **13 of 60 cells (0.2167)**. So on 47 of 60 cells a committed LIST-form sentence names a book the honest chooser did not select — which is 1227's finding generalised from one cell to sixty.

  **AND THE HONEST CHOOSER IS ITSELF NOT WORTH ITS MOTION.** The walk-forward stitch beats the do-nothing anchor at only **21 of 60 cells**, pooled fold delta **-0.0328** (mean |t| 1.33). Mean OOS Sharpe across the 60 rule-8 rows: rule-8 pick 0.8358 < anchor 0.8759 ~ SPY 0.8749 < listing max 0.9091 < live RULES v2 0.9807. The record's chooser machinery, read honestly, loses to holding the anchor; read in the LIST form, it appears to beat it.

  **RULE 8 (mandatory) AND BOTH KEEP PATHS.** Parameters chosen on 2009-2016 only, 2017-2026 read once. Best rule-8 pick anywhere in the run: U56 / K_GROSS / S_MAR -> N20/H126/gross 1.00/W, full 20.83% / 1.14 / -24.93% (H1 1.20 / H2 1.10), **OOS 22.72% / 1.1652 / -24.93%** against U56 SPY OOS 15.21% / 0.8711 / -33.72% and live RULES v2 OOS 9.46% / 1.2763 / -12.05%. It fails 4b on the drawdown cap (-24.93% against SPY's -33.72% x 0.60 = -20.23%). **4a passes 0 of 60. 4b passes 5 of 60, and every one is a book the record has already committed** — the anchor itself (twice, via K_CADENCE), U56 gross 0.65, and B136 gross 0.55 (twice) — so nothing here is a new rule, and ideas 1069/1189 have already shown gross-rung 4b passes sit in cells whose gross-matched null passes often.

  **GATES, all 9 pass, printed before any result:** G1 fast runner == engine.backtest to 1.4e-17; G2 the committed U56 W/H126/N=20 triple to 4.1e-05 (bar 5e-3, 1160's vintage defect carried); G3 SPY OOS triple to 1.7e-04; G4 live RULES v2 MaxDD == -12.05% to 4.9e-05; G5 SMALL pipeline determinism exactly 0 on two uncached rebuilds; G6 the max-dominance identity, 0 violations; G7 the anchor rung is a member of every listing, so do-nothing is always available to the chooser; G8 the 14 fold partitions are contiguous and non-overlapping in all 3 panels; G9 the L252 rolling-IS variant published (EXPAND 0.8644 vs L252 0.8704 mean stitched Sharpe) and selected on at neither.

  **WHAT THIS RUN DOES NOT SHOW.** The census classifies SENTENCES by marker words, not by what their author did: a genuine walk-forward result written in superlative prose counts as LIST, so 0.6120 is an upper bound on genuine LIST-form errors. The OTHER class (4,891) is excluded from that denominator and the all-sentence share (0.3471) is printed beside it. The gap is measured on four ladders and four statistics on one tape and is not a constant.
