A WITHIN-length spread and a BETWEEN-length move both contrast the same books over stretches of the same inflated tape, so the bias very largely cancels out of the RATIO — the whole quantity this run measures. It does NOT cancel out of the 4b legs, measured against SPY, a real index, so the 16 full-sample 4b passes are an UPPER bound.

  Script `research/backtests/2026-09-16_should-a-SUB-TAPE-COMPARISON-be-required-to-publish-its-REGIME-to-LENGTH-RATIO_cloud.py`, 10 CSVs, console log, result memo, 6 LEADERBOARD rows. Follow-ups filed: 1155 (is the count-inflation of max-minus-min a general defect in the record's spread and band claims), 1156 (can a split-half claim be made regime-checkable without a third tape), 1157 (is the SMALL/MAXDD cell the one place where tape length beats regime).
- 2026-09-16 (lane B, idea 1157 is-the-SMALL-MAXDD-CELL-the-ONE-PLACE-where-TAPE-LENGTH-BEATS-REGIME) — **ANSWERED = NO. PREMISE REFUTED TWICE OVER, AND THE CELL IS MECHANICAL. 1148's only sub-1 cell in 72 reproduces here BIT FOR BIT (0.794259x, G7, |diff| 0.00e+00) and is neither a SMALL-panel fact nor a single-episode fact: it is the first crossing of a line EVERY panel crosses as soon as the fraction ladder gets finer, and the line itself is drawn by arithmetic.**

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (PROTOCOL rule 4):** `FRACTION LADDER` {F_1140 = 1/1,1/2,1/3 (1148's own); F_FINE = 1/1,1/2,1/3,1/4,1/6, the HEADLINE and the "finer sub-tape ladder" this idea asks for; F_FINER = 1/1,1/2,1/3,1/4,1/5,1/6,1/8} x `TAPE` {T_OWN = each panel on its own tape, 1148's own; T_MATCHED = every panel restricted to the SMALL panel's own trading days, the LENGTH-MATCHED LARGE-CAP CONTROL this idea asks for} = **6 combinations, ALL published**. PANEL (3) x STATISTIC (6) x RATIO DEFINITION (3) x PARTITION {ALIGNED, OFFSET} x EPISODE {KEEP, DROP} are NOT dials — all **432 cells dumped**.

  **IT IS NOT THE ONE PLACE.** MAXDD at R_MATCHED, T_OWN, ALIGNED/KEEP: F_1140 **U56 1.172 / B136 1.307 / SMALL 0.794**; F_FINE **0.691 / 0.740 / 0.551**; F_FINER **0.652 / 0.694 / 0.468**. The F_1140 -> F_FINER multiplier is **0.39x-0.59x on all six (tape, panel) cells** — one factor, every panel. Per rung the reversal is sharper: at F_1140 SMALL has **74%** of its 27 books sub-1 against U56 **15%** and B136 **22%**; at F_FINE it is **27 of 27 on all three panels**. H_FINE is SUPPORTED for the reason that kills the premise — the finer ladder does not confirm SMALL, it drags the large caps down to it.

  **IT IS NOT A SMALL-PANEL FACT — H_PANEL REFUTED.** On the length-matched control, T_MATCHED / F_FINE MAXDD reads **SMALL 0.560x, U56 0.619x, B136 0.579x**: indistinguishable, with B136 BELOW SMALL. The tape dial moves the cell by **0.009** (0.551 -> 0.560); the fraction-ladder dial moves it by **0.326** (0.794 -> 0.468).

  **IT IS NOT A SINGLE-EPISODE ARTEFACT — H_EPISODE REFUTED.** The SMALL anchor book's full-tape MaxDD is **-35.81%, trough 2020-03-18**; dropping all of **2020** moves the headline cell **0.551 -> 0.620**, still far below 1. Only in 1148's own coarse corner (F_1140 + OFFSET) does the drop cross the line (0.553 -> 1.288), so *that particular reading* is part-episode and the finding on any finer ladder is not. The drop removes a whole calendar year, more than the drawdown — an over-removal that can only make H_EPISODE EASIER to support, and it fails anyway.

  **THE MECHANISM, WHICH IS THE PART WORTH KEEPING: 1148's OWN DEFECT, IN ITS BETWEEN TERM.** 1148 count-matched the WITHIN term (max-minus-min grows with k) and left the BETWEEN term as `|median at 1/1 - median at 1/f_max|` — a range over the ladder's ENDPOINTS, which grows as the ladder is extended while the length-matched within spread does not. SMALL between runs **10.53 -> 15.39 -> 17.49** across the three ladders against within **8.36 / 8.48 / 8.18**; U56 between **4.43 -> 6.92 -> 7.06** against within **5.19 / 4.78 / 4.60**. The ratio is therefore not a property of the statistic and the tape but of **how far down the fraction ladder a run happened to cut**, and two runs quoting it are not comparable. Why SMALL crossed FIRST at 1/3 is then a LEVEL fact and nothing more: its MaxDD is **-35.81%** against U56's **-19.13%**, so both terms are ~1.9x larger and the between term is the one that outgrows.

  **AND THE WHOLE READING IS MECHANICAL — H_NULL SUPPORTED ON EVERY PANEL.** A moving-block bootstrap (L=63, the record's own) of the anchor book has NO regime ordering by construction, only length. Its MAXDD R_MATCHED median: **U56 0.681, B136 0.678, SMALL 0.633**, with **0.92-0.98 of 200 draws sub-1** on every panel (IID nulls 0.583-0.704). The observed SMALL cell **0.551x** sits INSIDE its own null's 90% band **[0.441, 0.881]**. H_CONTINUUM **SUPPORTED 18 of 18** — MAXDD is among the two lowest-ratio statistics in every (ladder, tape, panel) cell — so 1148's cell is a threshold crossing in a continuum, not a phenomenon. Across all 432 cells R_MATCHED reads sub-1 **69 times** and **14 of the 18** headline sub-1 readings are MAXDD. "Tape length beats regime for MaxDD" says only that a maximum taken over more points grows with the points.

  **GATES 7 of 7, printed before any result number.** G1 fast runner == `engine.backtest` 1.39e-17; G2 CROSS-RUN the committed U56 W/H126/N=20 triple 3.18e-07; G3 SPY OOS triple 1.70e-04; G4 live RULES v2 MaxDD == committed -12.05% at 4.95e-05; G5 determinism of the SMALL pipeline 0.00e+00; G6 1148's committed `regime.csv` present, its MAXDD row quoted verbatim from the file; **G7 CROSS-RUN 1148's SMALL/MAXDD R_MATCHED 0.794259x reproduced by a verbatim replica of its Arm-B code at |diff| 0.00e+00** — the premise this run refutes is refuted on a number it first reproduces exactly.

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS — NOTHING PROPOSED AS A BOOK.** The fraction-ladder dial is a READ of already-computed returns and leaves the 81 T_OWN rung books byte-identical; the tape dial only TRUNCATES. Scored at all **162 rungs** because rule 4 requires it. Rung chosen on IS 2009-2016 ALONE, per ladder, three choosers, OOS read ONCE: **10 of 72 picks clear 4b full, 10 of 72 clear 4b OOS, 0 of 72 clear 4a**; the IS chooser takes the OOS-best rung 20 of 72 times. Whole grid: **4b full 31, 4b OOS 32, 4a 0** — T_OWN U56 10/27, B136 6/27, **SMALL 0/27**; T_MATCHED U56 12/27, B136 3/27, **SMALL 0/27**. Binding leg among the 131 failures: **L_DD sole at 51**, L_CAGR sole at 18, L_H2 sole at 1. Every passing pick is the standing anchor or a neighbouring GROSS/N rung of it — **U56 / W / H=126 / N=20 / gross 0.75, full 15.58% / 1.1397 / -19.13%, halves 1.2037 / 1.0971, OOS 16.97% / 1.1643 / -19.13%** against **SPY OOS 15.21% / 0.8711 / -33.72%** and **live RULES v2 OOS 1.2762 / -12.05%**. Length-matching the U56 tape to SMALL's costs the anchor **1.48pp of CAGR and 0.078 of Sharpe** (14.10% / 1.0616 / -19.21%, OOS 15.93% / 1.1087). NO NEW BOOK.

  **WHAT THE RECORD SHOULD DO WITH IT, STATED NARROWLY (proposed, NOT enacted — rule 6).** 1148's drafted regime-to-length clause must not ship with its between term as written: it should FIX the fraction ladder inside the clause, or count-match the between term the way 1148 count-matched the within term. And no regime-to-length ratio for MAXDD — or any maximum — should be read as evidence without its own resample null beside it, because the null is sub-1 everywhere.

  **THE DECLARED APPROXIMATION, AND ITS DIRECTION.** Every null resamples ONE tape, so it prices sampling error around THIS regime and not regime uncertainty across regimes; a null ratio is therefore biased TOWARD the reading it tests. H_NULL is scored in the direction that favours calling the cell MECHANICAL — the conclusion this run reaches — so that conclusion rests on the evidence hardest to avoid, not easiest.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B136 are CURRENT-CONSTITUENT lists; the SMALL pool is the current constituents of a sub-$2B screen (**663 names after dropping 52 with `max_1d_move >= 1.0` from `data/small_meta.csv`, 715 listed; tape 2010-01-04 -> 2026-09-11**), so every name that fell below the screen, delisted or went to zero is absent and the drawdowns read here are the SHALLOWEST the period could have produced. A within-length spread and a between-length move contrast the same books over stretches of the same inflated tape, so the bias very largely cancels out of the RATIO, and in the null arm — a resample of one book — it cancels exactly. It does NOT cancel out of the 4b legs, measured against SPY, a real index, so the 31 full-sample 4b passes are an UPPER bound.

  Script `research/backtests/2026-09-16_is-the-SMALL-MAXDD-CELL-the-ONE-PLACE-where-TAPE-LENGTH-BEATS-REGIME_B.py`, 7 CSVs, console log, result memo, 6 LEADERBOARD rows. Follow-ups filed: 1158 (should a regime-to-length ratio fix its fraction ladder or count-match its between term), 1159 (does any committed maximum-keyed claim in the record carry a resample null), 1160 (is the MaxDD LEVEL the whole of the SMALL panel's regime-to-length difference).

## 2026-09-17 — idea 1217 (cloud lane, idea 2 of 2): should a BOUNDARY IS-ARGMAX be REPORTABLE as a PICK at all?

  **ANSWERED = YES AS A SCHEMA FLAG, NO AS A CHOOSER — AND THE QUESTION IS SECOND-ORDER, BECAUSE NO IS-ARGMAX PICK PAYS ON THIS GRID AT ALL.**

  **THE CENSUS.** 40,988 committed pick rows harvested from **71** committed `*.walkforward.csv` / `*.picks.csv` artefacts over 82 (file, ladder) reconstructions, against DIAL 1 BOUNDARY DEFINITION {B_NONE, B_STRICT, B_WIDE} x DIAL 2 CLAIM SET {C_LADRUNG, C_PICK, C_ALL}, all 9 cells published. **B_STRICT would have SUPPRESSED 19,313 of 40,988 committed pick rows = 47.1%** (C_LADRUNG 46.8%, C_PICK 49.3%); B_WIDE 83.2%, of which **39.4%** sit on ladders shorter than 2k+1 rungs and are boundary *by construction* — reported in its own column, never folded into the headline. **RECOVERY LIMIT, STATED NOT HIDDEN (1152's finding):** the record rarely states a ladder's rung LIST beside a pick, so each list is reconstructed from the distinct rung values the file itself publishes; an under-estimated list INFLATES the boundary rate, so **47.1% is an upper bound**. In the record as published the suppressed picks are not the bad ones — mean committed OOS Sharpe **1.3371** against 1.2559 kept, mean committed OOS MaxDD **-13.09%** against -13.72%. That asymmetry is precisely why the right instrument is a flag and not a filter.

  **WHERE THE HABIT LIVES, ON THE LIVE GRID.** At B_STRICT, primary cadence: **GROSS 0.9585** of its picks are boundary (modal rung **1.00**, the ladder's TOP, on a statistic that runs 1.1463 to 1.1485 across the whole ladder — a spread of **0.0022**), **H 0.6528** (modal rung 252, also the top), **N 0.2383**, **CADENCE 0.0000** (unordered, never flagged). Claim set ALL4 0.4624, NG 0.2971. Gates **G13/G16 reproduce 1205 exactly**: U56 GROSS=1.00 MaxDD **-24.9276%** against its committed -24.93%, frozen rung **-19.1276%** against -19.13%. This is 1189's "flat in gross" and 1209's monotone-ladder result arriving as a **SELECTION** fact: GROSS's argmax is the ladder's end because the ladder ran out, not because the data located a maximum.

  **WHAT SUPPRESSION COSTS OR SAVES.** 24 cells published (2 suppression choosers x 2 widths x 2 claim sets x 2 cadences), paired per-fold deltas against CH_ARGMAX restricted to the folds where the rule FIRED, SE clustered on folds. **B_STRICT is a wash** — CH_SUPP_INTERIOR dSharpe **-0.0054** (ALL4) / **-0.0115** (NG), dMaxDD +1.03pp / -0.04pp. **B_WIDE goes the other way on both legs and both sets** — **+0.0479 / +0.0567** of Sharpe, **+2.82pp / +1.35pp** of drawdown — but **every** paired per-fold t is **|t| <= 0.6** (357 / 511 fired folds at ALL4, 172 / 323 at NG). PRE-DECLARED OUTCOME **(B) REPORT AS IS**; the (A) test was keyed to B_STRICT / CH_SUPP_INTERIOR. **POST-HOC AND LABELLED AS SUCH:** the boundary width behaves like a dial on HOW MUCH PICKING the rule does, not like a diagnostic that separates good picks from bad — the wider the suppression, the closer the chooser gets to doing nothing, and the better it does.

  **THE DOMINANT FACT, WHICH SUBSUMES THE QUESTION.** **Doing nothing beats every pick rule on this grid**: CH_ANCHOR OOS Sharpe **0.8845** / MaxDD **-25.23%** / 4b **4 of 12**, against the record's habit **0.8268** / **-28.74%** / 4b **0 of 12**, and against every suppressed variant. Rule 8 per panel (both dials plus the chooser picked on the pre-2017 folds only, 2017-2026 read ONCE): **U56's IS choice IS the do-nothing cell** — ALL4 / B_NONE / CH_ANCHOR, OOS 17.16% / 1.1759 / -19.13%, 4b 4 of 4; B136 NG / B_STRICT / CH_SUPP_INTERIOR 14.23% / 0.9039 / -23.21% against its habit 15.92% / 0.9285 / -24.15% and its anchor 16.29% / 1.0240 / -20.74%; SMALL NG / B_NONE / CH_ARGMAX 7.30% / 0.4681 / -36.86% against anchor 7.09% / 0.4534 / -35.81%. SPY OOS 15.15% / 0.8686 / -33.72% (B136 and SMALL span 15.33% / 0.8769); live RULES v2 OOS 9.42% / 1.2717 / -12.05%.

  **BOTH KEEP PATHS.** 60 rung books: **4a 0**, 4b full 13, 4b OOS 12, BOTH 12. Boundary rungs specifically (18 books): 4b full **1**, mean OOS Sharpe 0.8417 / MaxDD -26.99%, against interior rungs (42 books) 0.8735 / -24.79%. 288 stitched curves: **4a 0**, 4b full 44, 4b OOS 44. Rule 8 over 288 distinct OOS rows: **4a 0, 4b 44** — every pass an already-committed book. **KILL (capital), NO NEW BOOK, NO MEMO, NO RULES CHANGE.** The boundary rule is **PROPOSED, NOT ENACTED** (rule 6, Sunday review only) as a SCHEMA clause: flag any published pick whose rung sits at its ladder's boundary and require the rung list beside it. It is not a chooser and this run does not offer it as one.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** B136 and SMALL are CURRENT constituents; SMALL is the sub-$2B screen with **51 of 715** tickers dropped for `max_1d_move >= 1.0` (664 investable), SPY benchmark only. The bias does not cancel out of the OOS levels or the 4b legs, so any pass there is an upper bound; the census arm reads committed text and carries no market bias at all.

  **Gates 16 of 16.** Runtime 23s, offline, deterministic. Script `research/backtests/2026-09-17_should-a-BOUNDARY-IS-ARGMAX-be-REPORTABLE-as-a-PICK-at-all_cloud.py`; `RULES.md`, `PROTOCOL.md`, `scan.py`, `bot.py` and `baseline.py` untouched.

## 2026-09-17 — idea 1229 (cloud lane, idea 1 of 2): at what IS WINDOW LENGTH does a HEADLINE FORM separate from its own NL_PERM band?

  **ANSWERED = AT NO WINDOW, AT NO CADENCE, AND THE SIGN IS THE WRONG WAY ROUND.** `L* = NONE` for all three headline forms and both controls. **0 of 18** (form, window) cells separate from their own NL_PERM band at the primary cadence; **1 of 108** across MONTH / QUARTER / YEAR, against **5.4** expected at alpha 0.05 by chance (gate G12) — and that one cell (YEAR / NG / L1260 / H_NARROWEST, p 0.0185) rests on **2 moves**. The grid is 6 IS windows x 3 fold cadences x 5 choosers x 2 comparison sets x 3 panels, every point published; NL_PERM is the chooser's OWN destination multiset re-dealt to random folds, 4,000 reps, seed 12291229. G8 reproduces 1227's committed (window, form) deltas to **4.9e-04**.

  **THE AGGREGATE SIGN IS THE RESULT, AND IT IS NOT A NULL.** Over the **104** cells with a defined band the observed delta sits ABOVE its own NL_PERM mean at **19**; a coin gives 52. Two-sided binomial **p = 3.8e-11**. Mean gap **-0.0298** of Sharpe, median -0.0147, mean p_NL_PERM **0.6678**. **Re-dealing a form's own destinations to random folds BEATS the form's actual timing**, at every cadence and every window. 1227 read its grid as "no form beats holding"; the sharper reading is that the destinations carry whatever there is and the *timing* is actively bad. Anything in the record that reads a headline form as a weak-but-positive chooser is reading the wrong side of its own null.

  **THE REQUIRED PRECISION, WHICH IS WHAT THE QUEUE ASKED FOR.** Only **4 of 54** ALL4 form cells have a positive gap at all. With the band half-width's shrink exponent FITTED (not assumed) on a fold-subsample ladder — fractions 0.25 / 0.50 / 1.00, 40 draws, 400 null reps each, 52 cells, **b = 0.545 mean / 0.494 median**, i.e. near-iid when folds are DROPPED from a fixed span — `F* = F x (w/G)^(1/b)` gives: **H_RUNNERUP / L504 / QUARTER 38 years** of tape, L252 **73**, L1260 **943**; H_NARROWEST, H_ANY and CH_RAW **infinite at every window**. The pre-declared 50-year attainability bar therefore fires outcome (B) on exactly one cell, and **that cell rests on 3 moves in 193 folds**, so 38 years is a LOWER BOUND on the tape this question needs, not a forecast that the tape would settle it.

  **BYCATCH, AND THE MOST REUSABLE THING IN THE RUN: MORE FOLDS ON A FIXED TAPE BUY ~7%, NOT 3.5x.** MONTH (573 folds), QUARTER (193) and YEAR (46) read the **same 17.7 years**. Under iid-in-folds the band would shrink by `sqrt(F_M/F_Y)`, a ratio of **0.283**, from YEAR to MONTH. Measured over 24 (window, form) cells it is **0.934** (Q/Y **0.832** against a predicted 0.488) — an implied cross-cadence exponent **b = 0.027** against 0.5. Read with the subsample fit (b ~ 0.5 when folds are dropped from a fixed span), the two together pin the rule: **the F in any required-precision calculation must come from new calendar years, never from slicing the existing tape finer.** Any committed resolution the record bought by going to a finer fold cadence bought about 7% of what the fold count advertises.

  **BOTH KEEP PATHS AND RULE 8.** 66 rung books: **4a 0**, 4b full 17, 4b OOS 16, BOTH **16**. 540 stitched chooser curves: **4a 0**, 4b full 128, 4b OOS 126. Rule 8 with BOTH dials plus the form chosen on the pre-2017 folds only and 2017-2026 read ONCE: **4a 0 of 270**, 4b **50 of 270**. Every 4b pass on every leg is a U56 or B136 rung of the anchor family the record has already committed (U56 N=15, the N=20/H=126/GROSS=0.75/CADENCE=W anchor, H=21, GROSS 0.50-0.75; B136 GROSS 0.50-0.70). Mean OOS Sharpe of the IS-chosen cell **0.8558** against **doing nothing 0.8845** (**-0.0286**): U56 MONTH/L504/CH_RAW 17.55% / 1.1572 / -20.51% against the anchor's 17.16% / 1.1759 / -19.13%; B136 MONTH/L252/H_RUNNERUP 16.04% / 1.0129 against 16.29% / 1.0240; SMALL MONTH/L504/CH_RAW 6.19% / 0.3974 against 7.09% / 0.4534. U56 SPY OOS 15.15% / 0.8686 / -33.72%, live RULES v2 OOS 9.42% / 1.2717 / -12.05%. **KILL (capital), NO NEW BOOK, NO MEMO, NO RULES CHANGE.**

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** B136 and SMALL are CURRENT constituents; SMALL is the sub-$2B screen with **51 of 715** tickers dropped for `max_1d_move >= 1.0` (664 investable), SPY benchmark only. The bias does not cancel out of the OOS levels or the 4b legs, so any pass there is an upper bound; it largely does cancel out of the gap-to-band ratio and the same-tape band ratio, which are this run's two headlines.

  **Gates 20 of 20.** Runtime 36s, offline, deterministic. Script `research/backtests/2026-09-17_at-what-IS-WINDOW-LENGTH-does-a-HEADLINE-FORM-SEPARATE-FROM-ITS-OWN-NL_PERM-BAND_cloud.py`; `RULES.md`, `PROTOCOL.md`, `scan.py`, `bot.py` and `baseline.py` untouched.

## 2026-09-17 — idea 1162 (cloud lane): is MONOTONICITY of a DIAL a PUBLISHABLE STAMP, or does it cost a run a SECOND GRID?

  **ANSWERED: A STAMP, AND A CHEAP ONE.** On a ladder a run has already built, `S_RHO` (Spearman of the statistic over the dial) costs **0 extra books and 0.062 s = 0.646% of the ladder's own 9.6 s / 120 books**; the margin leg costs **0 extra books** either way (0.0031 s closed-form Lo Sharpe SE, 15.1 s / 96,000 path evaluations if moving-block resampled). The **SECOND GRID** the queue named as the alternative costs **+120 books = +100%**. All six cost rows published.

  **IT CHANGES MORE THAN HALF THE READINGS.** Of 48 argmax claims (4 objectives x 3 panels x 4 dials, every rung of all 120 books published): `S_RHO` **26 of 48**, `S_MARGIN` **32 of 48**, `S_BOTH` **43 of 48**, all 16 (STAMP x CLAIMSET) cells published. **1154's 2x2 replicates on a fresh population: 10 of 32 non-monotone claims still pick an endpoint (0.312 against 1154's 0.405) and 8 of those 10 prefer it by under one SD (0.800 against 0.882).** MONOTONE-and-INTERIOR is **0 of 48** at tau 0.90, so at this threshold the stamp only ever fires in 1154's direction.

  **A CORRECTION THIS RUN MADE TO ITSELF, recorded rather than quietly re-cut.** The first cut scored a preference between two rungs against `sqrt(sd_a^2 + sd_b^2)` and read **48 of 48** claims unresolved — a bar that treats two rungs of one ladder as independent books when they are the same book one parameter apart. On **paired** draws (shared block origins, SD of the difference) the count is **32 of 48**. Gate G10 pins the direction; every margin in the committed files is paired, and the marginal SDs are kept only for spreads.

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS.** IS 2009-2016 only, OOS 2017-2026 untouched, 3 panels x 4 dials x 4 objectives x 4 choosers = **192 picks, all published**. 4b full AND OOS: `CH_ARGMAX` **3 of 48**, `CH_STAMP` **12 of 48**, `CH_STAMPM` **15 of 48**, `CH_CENTER` **5 of 48**. **4a 0 of 192** and **0 of 120** on the ladder itself.

  **THE CONFOUND IS FALSIFIED, NOT WAVED OFF.** `CH_STAMP`'s monotone fallback is the **incumbent** rung — a cell the record already chose with hindsight — and it fires 26 of 48 times. `CH_STAMPM` is the identical chooser whose fallback is the ladder's own **midpoint** and knows nothing of the record; it does **better**, 15 of 48 against 12, mean OOS Sharpe delta on moved picks **+0.0469 against +0.0417**. The stamp's out-of-sample gain is not the incumbent.

  **THE MECHANISM.** On U56/GROSS the Sharpe ladder is **flat** — 1.1387 / 1.1381 / 1.1376 at gross 1.00 / 0.75 / 0.60 — and monotone at **rho 1.000** with the argmax **unresolved**. `CH_ARGMAX` therefore takes gross **1.00** and **fails 4b on the drawdown leg** (MaxDD −24.93% against SPY's −20.23% cap). The stamp refuses the exposure endpoint. This is 1150/1154's "gross is an exposure dial" arriving as a *selection* result rather than a descriptive one.

  **THE ONE 4b BOOK RULE 8 REACHES WITHOUT THE INCUMBENT** is `CH_STAMPM` / U56 / **gross 0.60**: full CAGR **12.41%**, Sharpe **1.1376**, MaxDD **−15.51%** (H1 1.2045 / H2 1.0925); OOS CAGR **13.49%**, Sharpe **1.1609**, MaxDD **−15.51%**; against SPY full 15.06% / 0.8814 / −33.72% and OOS 15.15% / 0.8684 / −33.72%, and live RULES v2 8.60% / 1.1980 / −12.05%. **Memo written with exact RULES wording and recommending PARK**: its Sharpe equals the incumbent's to four decimals while its CAGR is 3.1 pp/yr lower, it is a **de-grossing** and not a discovery, and it fails 4a. A draft PROTOCOL clause on the stamp is **PROPOSED, NOT ENACTED** (rule 6, Sunday review only).

  **POPULATION CAVEAT, stated in the script, the memo and every leaderboard row.** The shares above are "of argmax claims **of this shape**" — a fresh, fully specified population — **not** "of the record's committed sentences". Those claims' cells are not resolvable from prose (1098's `R_STRICT`: 82 of 2,088; idea 1149 still open). Nothing here re-scores a committed claim.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B136 are current-constituent lists; SMALL is the current constituents of a sub-$2B screen (**664 columns after dropping 52 tickers with `max_1d_move >= 1.0` from `data/small_meta.csv`**; tape 2010-01-04 -> 2026-09-11). Every name that delisted, went to zero or fell below the screen is absent, so drawdowns on B136 and SMALL are the shallowest the period could have produced; both are breadth controls, not tradable claims.

  10 of 10 gates PASS, including G1-G3 against the record's committed incumbent anchors and G10 on the paired-SD correction. Script `research/backtests/2026-09-17_is-MONOTONICITY-of-a-DIAL-a-PUBLISHABLE-STAMP-or-does-it-cost-a-run-a-second-grid_cloud.py`, 7 CSVs, console log, result memo, 3 LEADERBOARD rows. **NOTHING ENACTED**; RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.

## 2026-09-17 — idea 1165 (cloud lane): do the RECORD's OTHER committed BLOCK BOOTSTRAP BANDS carry the same MACHINERY SHARE?

  **ANSWERED: THE TERM IS REAL, UNSTABLE ACROSS PANELS, AND IMMATERIAL TO EVERY PUBLISHED BAND.**

  **THIS IS A REAL CENSUS, NOT A FRESH POPULATION** — unusual for a "census the record" idea. All 36 committed `N_BLOCK` bands were **replayed bit for bit** off idea 1159's own committed `.objects.csv` using its `seed_of`, its L=63 and its 1,000 draws: observed values reproduce to **3.553e-15**, null medians to **3.553e-15**, and **36 of 36 INSIDE verdicts identically** (`G_REPLAY_OBS` / `_MED` / `_VER`). 1157's SMALL |MaxDD| 90% band (0.441, 0.881) is quoted from 1159's committed source. Everything below re-scores the record's own numbers.

  **HOW MANY PUBLISHED BANDS MOVE.** Under the parent's own order-destroyed control `X_SHUFFLE1`: **0 of 108** (36 bands x 3 confidence levels). Under `X_SHUFFLEPD`, a fresh permutation per draw with 1,000 of them against the parent's 5: **1 of 108**. 1157's ratio band still overlaps at **4 of 4** ladders under every control. Even `X_NONE` — the **maximal** correction, which removes the entire block-vs-iid gap rather than only the machinery part — flips **2 of 108**, both the same U56/H `OBJ_SPREAD` band at q=0.80 and q=0.90.

  **THE MECHANISM IS ARITHMETIC, AND IT SETTLES THE QUESTION.** The correction factor `phi_machinery` runs **0.828 to 1.147, median |phi−1| = 0.0496**, while the published 90% bands are **0.62 to 4.43 times their own null median wide** (OBJ_MAXDD 0.66-0.89, OBJ_SPREAD 0.62-2.05, OBJ_ARGMAX 0.67-4.43). A 5% recentring cannot move a verdict sitting deep inside a 70%-wide band. **A resample band is too blunt an instrument for the machinery term to reach.**

  **BUT THE SHARE ITSELF DOES NOT REPLICATE, AND THAT IS THE FINDING WORTH KEEPING.** On `OBJ_MAXDD` under the parent's own control: **SMALL +0.868 against its committed +0.841** (replicates), **U56 +0.063 against +0.165**, **B136 −0.046 against +0.164 — a sign flip**. Under the per-draw control the same three read **+0.133 / +0.101 / +0.258**. Across objects it is wilder still (U56 `OBJ_ARGMAX` −0.513, B136 `OBJ_SPREAD` −1.035). Pooled BLOCK/IID with order destroyed is **0.9759 here against the parent's committed 0.9696**. **Read plainly: the parent's POOLED number is solid and its PER-PANEL decomposition is not — only SMALL's 84% survives a control with 1,000 permutations instead of 5, and any future citation should narrow it to SMALL rather than repeat it as a three-panel fact.**

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS.** 81 rung books (base rates 4b full 16, 4b OOS 17, **4a 0 of 81**) plus 4 choosers x 4 ladders x 3 panels = **48 picks, all published**. IS 2009-2016 only, OOS 2017-2026 untouched. 4b full AND OOS: `K_NONULL` **0 of 12**, `K_BLOCK` **1 of 12**, `K_CORR` **1 of 12**, `K_IID` **1 of 12**; **4a 0 of 48**. **The machinery correction moves the pick at 4 of 12 ladders and is WORSE where it moves — mean OOS Sharpe −0.0318, better at only 2 of 4 — so it is not worth making as a selection device either.**

  **THE ONE 4b BOOK A CHOOSER REACHES** is `K_CORR` / U56 / **gross 0.60**: full CAGR **12.41%**, Sharpe **1.1376**, MaxDD **−15.51%**; OOS **13.49% / 1.1609 / −15.51%**; against SPY full 15.06% / 0.8814 / −33.72% and OOS 15.15% / 0.8684 / −33.72%, live RULES v2 8.60% / 1.1980 / −12.05%. **It is the same book idea 1162 reached by a different route the same day.** The memo points at 1162's exact RULES wording rather than duplicating it and **recommends PARK**: a de-grossing of the incumbent, Sharpe equal to four decimals, CAGR 3.1 pp/yr lower, 4a failed.

  **NO PROTOCOL CLAUSE PROPOSED, DELIBERATELY.** The obvious one — require an order-destroyed control beside every published moving-block band — was **priced by this run** and buys **0 of 108** verdict corrections while costing a third null on every banded run. A clause that corrects nothing is not worth a rule.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9), and it bites harder here than usual.** B136 and SMALL are current-constituent lists; SMALL is the current constituents of a sub-$2B screen (**664 columns after dropping 52 tickers with `max_1d_move >= 1.0` from `data/small_meta.csv`**, tape 2010-01-04 -> 2026-09-11). **Every object in this file is a drawdown**, so every delisted, zeroed or screened-out name being absent makes all three panels' drawdowns — and every band and share above — the shallowest the period could have produced. SMALL, the one panel whose machinery share replicates, is also the one most exposed to this.

  12 of 12 gates PASS. Script `research/backtests/2026-09-17_do-the-RECORD-s-OTHER-committed-BLOCK-BOOTSTRAP-BANDS-carry-the-same-MACHINERY-SHARE_cloud.py`, 7 CSVs, console log, result memo, 3 LEADERBOARD rows. **NOTHING ENACTED**; RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.

## 2026-09-17 — idea 1175 (cloud lane, idea 1 of 2): is EVERY 4b-CONDITIONAL OUTCOME in the record UNFALSIFIABLE BY A CONTROLLED TAPE?

  **ANSWERED: IT DEPENDS ENTIRELY ON WHICH CONTROL, AND ONE OF THE TWO IN USE IS NOT A NULL AT ALL.**

  **THE CENSUS SEPARATES TWO LAYERS AND THEY DISAGREE BY TWO ORDERS OF MAGNITUDE.** 5,737 committed CSVs scanned, 1,265 carrying a 4b gate column, 29,267 (file, column) cells examined: only **158 columns (0.0054) are defined ONLY on 4b-pass rows**, across 18 files. The reader-facing layer is where the defect lives: **392 committed sentences restrict an outcome to the 4b-passing set**, in 140 files, **381 of them quoting a number** — O_SHARE 36, O_BINDING 17, O_MARGIN 10, O_CSTAR 2, O_OTHER 327. The machine record is nearly clean; the prose is not.

  **THE ANSWER, AND IT REVERSES THE QUEUE'S FRAMING.** Under `X_PERM` — a per-day permutation of the cross-section among the names observable that day, which preserves the daily equal-weight return path to **5.551e-17**, the daily cross-sectional multiset to **3.331e-16** and the listing mask exactly (0 cells) — the 4b pass rate collapses to **0.033 / 0.117 / 0.000** on U56 / B136 / SMALL against **0.292 / 0.083 / 0.000** on the real panels. That is idea 1171's degeneracy, reproduced. But under `X_ROT`, a per-name circular rotation, it goes the **other way**: **0.550 / 0.400 / 0.083**. The "control" passes 4b **almost twice as often as reality does on U56**. A claim is not unfalsifiable by a controlled tape; it is unfalsifiable by *one* of the two tapes the record calls controlled.

  **THE MECHANISM IS ARITHMETIC AND IT SETTLES WHICH TAPE IS LEGITIMATE.** Mean off-diagonal daily-return correlation, U56: REAL **0.3241**, X_PERM **0.2744**, X_ROT **−0.0002**. Rotation destroys the market factor outright, so a 40-name book on an X_ROT tape is a near-independent portfolio: median book Sharpe **2.0911** and median MaxDD **−5.93%**, against REAL 1.1008 / −18.70% and X_PERM 0.9465 / −21.31%. **A tape that hands every book a Sharpe of 2 and a 6% drawdown is a free lunch, not a null.** X_PERM keeps the factor (0.274 against 0.324) and is the only one of the two that controls selection without also removing the risk being controlled for.

  **THE CHEAPEST REPAIR IS DE-GATING, IT COSTS ZERO EXTRA DRAWS, AND BOTH "SOFTER BENCHMARK" REPAIRS ARE WORSE THAN THE DISEASE.** Two dials (outcome set × repair) = 20 grid points, all published. Definedness on the controlled tapes: **R_DEGATE 0.742 (c*) and 1.000 (margin, binding leg, share)** against **R_NONE 0.197**, **R_SOFT 0.128**, **R_MATCH 0.000**, **R_REFUSE 0.000**. Fidelity: the de-gated real-panel rho(turnover, c*) reads **−0.779 on U56 (n = 24) and −0.769 on B136 (n = 24)** against the gated **−0.930 (U56, n = 7)** and the record's committed **−0.65 / −0.6484 / −0.6154** — same sign, same order of magnitude, **3.4x the domain, zero extra runs**. **R_MATCH is the trap**: a panel-EW benchmark is not softer than SPY, it is harder (U56 EW Sharpe **1.1353** against SPY **0.8815**), and it takes definedness to **0.000 on every panel including the real one**. R_SOFT costs **+720 book runs** and buys 0.128. **c* stays partly undefined even de-gated (0.742) because it needs a positive margin at 0 bps, and SMALL has none: 0 of 24 books.**

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS.** 72 real books, every one published. 4b full **9 of 72**, 4b OOS **15 of 72**, **4a 0 of 72**. The best 4b book (full AND OOS) is `U56 / monthly / n = 40 / gross 1.00`: full CAGR **14.46%**, Sharpe **1.1880**, MaxDD **−17.96%** (H1 1.1924 / H2 1.1856); OOS **16.01% / 1.2866 / −17.96%**; against SPY full 15.06% / 0.8815 / −33.72% and OOS 15.15% / 0.8686 / −33.72%, and live RULES v2 8.60% / 1.1982 / −12.05% (OOS 9.42% / 1.2717 / −12.05%). **Memo written with exact RULES wording and recommending PARK**: it is **not IS-chooser-reachable** (CH_ISSHARPE picks U56/M/n=5/gross=1.00, OOS Sharpe 0.8290), it fails 4a, and it buys +0.0149 of OOS Sharpe over the live book for 5.9 pp more drawdown.

  **ONE POSITIVE SIGNAL, REPORTED AT ITS TRUE WEIGHT.** `CH_GATED` — the 4b-conditional gate used as an IS-only chooser — moves the pick at **2 of 3 panels**, gains **+0.1185 mean OOS Sharpe** (max +0.2636) and is the **only** chooser on this grid to reach a 4b passer at all (**2 of 12 picks**, against 0 for CH_ISSHARPE, CH_MATCH and CH_CSTAR). That is the opposite of the record's recent run of "the gate costs you nothing and buys you nothing" results. **n = 3 panels: this is a PARK-grade signal and a follow-up, not a finding.**

  **NO PROTOCOL CLAUSE PROPOSED.** The obvious one — require a controlled-tape claim to name its tape — is worth having, but this run priced only two tapes on one book family, and rule 6 restricts changes to Sunday review in any case.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56, B136 and SMALL are all current-constituent lists; SMALL is the current constituents of a sub-$2B screen, **664 columns after dropping the 52 tickers with `max_1d_move >= 1.0` from `data/small_meta.csv`** (tape 2010-01-04 → 2026-09-11). Every delisted, zeroed or screened-out name is absent, so every CAGR, drawdown and pass rate above — the 4b base rates and the memo's book included — is the most flattering the period could have produced.

  **11 of 11 gates PASS**, including the fast runner ≡ `engine.backtest` at **0.000e+00** on both cadences, live RULES v2 at 8.60%/1.1982/−12.05% against the committed 8.60%/1.1980/−12.05%, SPY at 15.06%/0.8815/−33.72%, and the standing CAND20 cell at 12.56%/1.0854/−18.31% — MaxDD exact and the Sharpe bracketed by the record's **two** committed values for that same book (1.0596 and 1.0921, 0.0325 apart, a spread 933's own G3 flagged). Script `research/backtests/2026-09-17_is-EVERY-4b-CONDITIONAL-OUTCOME-in-the-record-UNFALSIFIABLE-BY-A-CONTROLLED-TAPE_cloud.py`, 7 CSVs, console log, memo, 4 LEADERBOARD rows. **NOTHING ENACTED**; RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.

## 2026-09-17 — idea 1179 (lane B): what does a ONE-SIDED agreement between a FREE bar and a PAID one buy the record?

  **ANSWERED: NOTHING. THE ONE-SIDEDNESS DOES NOT GENERALISE, IT REVERSES, AND 1170's OWN IS AN ARTEFACT OF TWO CONSTANTS.**

  **THE DIRECTION DOES NOT REPLICATE — IT FLIPS.** Of the three fresh paid-vs-free pairs the queue names, **0 of 3 are one-sided in 1170's direction**. `B_SHARPE` (moving-block Sharpe band vs Lo's analytic SE) is **+0 / −2** and `B_PERMT` (block sign-flip permutation vs paired t) is **+0 / −9** — one-sided the OTHER way, the free bar REFUSING what the paid bar admits — and `B_MEANP` (block bootstrap p vs normal p) is **+1 / −2, TWO-SIDED**. Agreement on the 87 POP+ANCHOR cells: 0.9655 / 0.9770 / 0.8966 against `B_TSTAT`'s 0.8161.

  **AND 1170's OWN PAIR IS A CONSTANT, NOT A METHOD.** Its free bar is 2 × the median SE and its paid bar 3 × the cross-seed spread with a 0.25 pp floor; the free constant that admits the SAME NUMBER of cells is **6.532, not 2.000 — 3.27x** — and at that matched level the committed **+16 / −0 becomes +1 / −1, TWO-SIDED**. `B_SHARPE` goes ONE-SIDED − → **IDENTICAL**, i.e. its disagreement was purely a level offset too. **A looser bar admits a superset BY CONSTRUCTION, and a superset is one-sided BY CONSTRUCTION.** H_ONESIDED, H_UNIVERSAL and H_CONSTANT are all REFUTED. This is a **correction to the record**: 1170's *"the cheap rule never refuses a cell the expensive rule admits, so it is a sound lower bound on refusals"* is arithmetically right and inferentially void, and future citations should quote the boundary number instead.

  **THE SCREEN WORKS ANYWAY, AND THAT IS THE FINDING WORTH KEEPING.** Two dials (bar pair × screen threshold) = **24 grid points, all published**. Flag a cell when |z_free − z\*| ≤ k and pay the full null only there. All four pairs reach **ZERO screened error** — verdicts identical to paying everywhere — at cost **B_SHARPE 0.0230, B_MEANP 0.0575, B_PERMT 0.2529, B_TSTAT 0.2989** of cells (free-only error 0.0230 / 0.0345 / 0.1034 / 0.1839; exact k\* 0.043 / 0.045 / 0.671 / 4.786). **The property that licenses a screen is BOUNDARY CONCENTRATION, not one-sidedness** — strictly weaker, strictly more useful, and present at 4 of 4 pairs: two-sided `B_MEANP` still reaches zero error at 0.0575 cost because its wrong-way disagreement also sits within 0.045 of the bar. H_SCREEN SUPPORTED 4 of 4.

  **THE CENSUS SAYS THE RECORD CANNOT AUDIT ANY OF THIS RETROSPECTIVELY.** 5,747 committed CSVs scanned, 816 carry a bar column, and only **35 (0.0429) publish BOTH a paid and a free bar** — PAID-only 94 (0.1152), FREE-only 687 (0.8419); 0.1377 in a resampling context. Prose is worse: of **59,872 committed sentences**, 843 quote a paid bar and **23 (0.0273)** also quote its free counterpart. **H_AUDITABLE REFUTED.** A screen has to be adopted FORWARD, one extra column at a time.

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS — ZERO CAPITAL CONTENT.** 72 population books and 3 panels × 3 choosers × 4 pairs × 3 regimes = **108 picks, all published**; IS bars rebuilt on 2009–2016 ONLY, picks scored on the untouched 2017–2026 window. 4b full **2 of 72**, 4b OOS **3 of 72**, **4a 0 of 72**; picks 4b full+OOS **0 of 108**, 4a **0 of 108**. **The bar regime (G_FREE / G_PAID / G_SCREEN) moves the pick at 0 of 36 (panel, pair, chooser) cells and |ΔOOS Sharpe| is 0.0000 everywhere — H_CAPITAL REFUTED.** Best 4b book (full AND OOS) `U56 / N20/H126/W`: full **15.55% / 1.1381 / −19.13%** (H1 1.2049 / H2 1.0932), OOS **16.92% / 1.1615 / −19.13%**; against SPY full 15.06% / 0.8814 / −33.72% and OOS 15.15% / 0.8684, live RULES v2 8.60% / 1.1980 / −12.05% (OOS 9.42% / 1.2714). It is **PRIOR ART** — 1170's own incumbent cell — **not IS-chooser-reachable (0 of 108)** and it **fails 4a**, so **no memo and nothing enacted**. Published honestly: `B_PERMT`'s IS pool is nearly empty (mean 0.33 books) and the chooser falls back to the unrestricted 24-book set at 6 of 9 cells under every regime, which is why its three regimes cannot differ — a limitation of that arm, not a result.

  **CLAUSE PROPOSED, NOT ENACTED (PROTOCOL rule 6 — Sunday review only):** *a run reporting that a cheap bar agrees one-sidedly with an expensive one must also report the agreement at a LEVEL-MATCHED cheap constant, the one admitting the same number of cells; and a run publishing a resampled bar should publish its closed-form counterpart in the same row, which costs no draws and is the only way a screen can ever be audited from the record.*

  **A POPULATION CAVEAT PUBLISHED RATHER THAN BURIED.** 45 of the 132 cells are GROSS rungs of one anchor book, and Sharpe and the t on the mean are near scale-invariant, so those cells are near-duplicates that inflate any agreement rate. Every headline is reported on all 132 AND on the 87 non-GROSS cells, and the 87 is the number quoted.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B136 are current-constituent lists; SMALL is the current constituents of a sub-$2B screen (**664 columns after dropping 52 tickers with `max_1d_move >= 1.0` from `data/small_meta.csv`**, tape 2010-01-04 → 2026-09-11). Every CAGR, drawdown and 4b base rate above is the most flattering the period could have produced. It largely cancels out of the headline (an agreement count between two bars on the same cell) and does NOT cancel out of the 4a / 4b legs.

  **12 of 12 gates PASS**, including the fast runner ≡ `engine.backtest` at 1.4e-17, live RULES v2 and SPY against their committed anchors, Lo's analytic SE ≡ the iid-bootstrap SD of the Sharpe on gaussian data, sign-flip ≡ normal p at L=1, and **1170's committed pair replayed bit for bit off its own `.cells.csv`: 0 of 132 verdicts differing on EACH of its two rules and its committed 0.8788 / +16 / −0 reproduced at 0.000e+00**. Hypotheses 1 of 6 SUPPORTED. Script `research/backtests/2026-09-17_what-does-a-ONE-SIDED-agreement-between-a-FREE-bar-and-a-PAID-one-buy-the-record_B.py`, 8 CSVs, console log, result memo, 4 LEADERBOARD rows. **NOTHING ENACTED**; RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.
## 2026-09-17 — idea 1180 (cloud lane, idea 2 of 2): is EVERY committed ESTIMABILITY or RESOLUTION VERDICT in the record itself SEED-STABLE?

  **ANSWERED: NO — AND 1170's 56 → 52 IS THE MILD END OF THE DEFECT, NOT THE ANOMALY.**

  **THE HARVEST CONFIRMS THE QUEUE'S PREMISE AND THEN OVERSHOOTS IT.** The queue said the record "has no seed-count convention". It is worse than that: in the machine layer there is no seed count **at all**. 166 committed CSVs carry an estimability / resolution verdict column — **333 (file, column) verdict cells**, 209 of them boolean — and **50 (0.150) carry a seed axis of any kind while 0 (0.000) state a seed COUNT**. The reader-facing layer is the same story at scale: **779 committed sentences publish an estimability, resolution or "clears its own noise" verdict**, across 180 files, and **16 (0.0205) name the seed count they were read at**.

  **THE ANSWER.** 72 cells (3 panels × 24 books, the same 72 as 1164) × **120 independent null tapes per panel** × 5 seed counts × 25 replicates × 4 verdict sets = **259,200 verdict evaluations, every one published**. The record's headline form is a count — "N of 72 ESTIMABLE". That count's **max − min spread across replicates AT THE SAME SEED COUNT** runs **23–36 cells at S = 3** and is **still 7–13 at S = 33**. The per-cell flip rate (share of the 72 whose verdict is not unanimous across replicates) runs **0.500–1.000 at S = 3** and **0.125–0.407 at S = 33**. Agreement with the S = 33 reference is **0.541–0.859 at S = 3**, and even S = 33 scored against itself reaches only **0.887–0.980**. **A 4-cell move on 72 is what this object does between two draws of the same seed count.** 1170 did not measure a seed-count effect; it measured noise that a 3-seed and a 5-seed reading share.

  **WHAT SEED COUNT WOULD FIX IT: NONE THAT ANYONE WILL RUN.** Fitting log(spread) = a + b·log(S) over the measured ladder, **8 of 12 (verdict set, statistic) pairs decay like sqrt-N** (b = −0.372 to −0.506) and imply **S ≈ 2,510 (V_BAND / CAGR) to 16,798 (V_TSTAT / MaxDD)** to hold the published count to within one cell — against the record's **3** and 1170's **5**, a 500x to 5,600x increase. The other **four pairs do not decay at all**: V_RESOLVE / CAGR **b = −0.092**, V_RESOLVE / MaxDD −0.252, V_SIGN / CAGR −0.261, V_TSTAT / CAGR −0.321. For those **no seed count fixes the verdict**. This is an **EXTRAPOLATION, not a measurement**, and the companion script `2026-09-17_estimability-verdict-seed-stable_cloud.FIT_seedcount.py` says so in its own output and refuses to quote an implied S where b is flat.

  **THE PRACTICAL READING.** An estimability verdict at 3 or 5 seeds is not a cheap version of the right answer; it is a different random variable. Either publish the seed count and the replicate spread beside the verdict, or stop publishing the verdict as a count. **No PROTOCOL clause is proposed** — rule 6 restricts changes to Sunday review, and this run priced one verdict family on one book family.

  **METHOD LIMIT, STATED BECAUSE IT BOUNDS EVERY NUMBER ABOVE.** The S-seed verdicts are scored on seed sets drawn **without replacement from a common pool of 120 null tapes per panel**, not on 120 × 25 freshly drawn tapes. Replicates at the same S are therefore mildly dependent, which makes every flip rate and spread reported here a **LOWER bound** on fully independent re-runs. A verdict already unstable under this construction is unstable a fortiori.

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS.** 72 real books and **378 chooser picks, all published**. 4b full **9 of 72**, 4b OOS **15 of 72**, **4a 0 of 72**, and **4b OOS 0 of 378 picks at every seed count**. The best 4b book (full AND OOS) is `U56 / monthly / n = 40 / gross 1.00`: full **14.46% / 1.1880 / −17.96%** (H1 1.1924 / H2 1.1856), OOS **16.01% / 1.2866 / −17.96%**; against SPY full 15.06% / 0.8815 / −33.72% and OOS 15.15% / 0.8686 / −33.72%, and live RULES v2 8.60% / 1.1982 / −12.05% (OOS 9.42% / 1.2717 / −12.05%). **It is the same book idea 1175 reached the same day on the same family, reproduced to the last digit** — a consistency check between the two runs, not a second piece of evidence for the book. Memo written with exact RULES wording and **recommending PARK**.

  **THE VERDICT IS WORTHLESS AS A SELECTION DEVICE AND ITS SIGN IS PANEL-DEPENDENT.** `CH_ESTIM`'s eligible set collapses monotonically as seeds are added (U56 24 → 6-15 of 24, B136 → 0-9, SMALL → 6), and its out-of-sample effect **splits by panel**: U56 mean OOS Sharpe 0.8290 → **1.1422** at S = 33, B136 1.0051 → 1.0958, but **SMALL 0.4373 → 0.3060**. Two panels up, one down, and never to a 4b pass. **KILL as a capital finding.**

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56, B136 and SMALL are current-constituent lists; SMALL is the current constituents of a sub-$2B screen, **664 columns after dropping the 52 tickers with `max_1d_move >= 1.0` from `data/small_meta.csv`** (tape 2010-01-04 → 2026-09-11). Every delisted, zeroed or screened-out name is absent, so every CAGR, drawdown, base rate and the memo's book are the most flattering the period could have produced.

  **11 of 11 gates PASS**, including the fast runner ≡ `engine.backtest` at **0.000e+00** on both cadences, live RULES v2 at 8.60%/1.1982/−12.05%, SPY at 15.06%/0.8815/−33.72%, `X_PERM` preserving the daily equal-weight return path to **5.551e-17** and the listing mask exactly, and the standing CAND20 cell at 12.56%/1.0854/−18.31% with its Sharpe bracketed by the record's two committed values. Script `research/backtests/2026-09-17_is-EVERY-committed-ESTIMABILITY-or-RESOLUTION-VERDICT-in-the-record-itself-SEED-STABLE_cloud.py` plus the `FIT_seedcount` companion, 8 CSVs, two console logs, result memo, 4 LEADERBOARD rows. **NOTHING ENACTED**; RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.

## 2026-09-17 — idea 1161 (lane B): why does a RESAMPLE NULL sit ABOVE the OBSERVED MAXIMUM at 108 of 108 cells?

  **ANSWERED: IT IS THE BOOK, NOT THE RESAMPLE — AND THEN THE QUEUE'S OWN RESIDUAL STORY DIES TOO.**

  **THE DECISIVE ARM PUTS A KNOWN ANSWER UNDER THE MACHINERY.** `S_PERM` re-orders the ladder's rungs by ONE common random permutation: every rung's marginal is exact, the cross-rung dependence is exact, and there is **no serial structure left to lose**, so a resample null of an S_PERM tape is correctly specified and the truth is ratio 1.000 / share-below-median 0.500. 25 independent pseudo-tapes x 400 draws x 3 nulls x 3 objects x 12 (panel, ladder) = **5,400 calibration cells, every one published**. The machinery reads **1,416 of 2,700 below its own median (0.5244)** against the real panels' **0.824**, with an anchor-object floor of **N_BLOCK 1.0096 / N_IID 0.9998 / N_STAT 1.0020**. **H_MACHINERY REFUTED.**

  **AND THE CHANNEL LADDER SAYS THE SAME THING ONE FACTOR AT A TIME.** Off the record's own N_BLOCK on OBJ_MAXDD, in log(observed / null median), against a total gap of **−0.0603**: **wrap-around +0.0026**, **the Jensen drift channel +0.0046** (each draw forced onto the observed total log return — gate G8, 0.000e+00), **marginal resampling −0.0066** (an EXACT permutation null — gate G7, 0.000e+00). Two of the three move the gap the WRONG WAY. Only `K4_IID` (L = 63 → 1) moves it at all, **−0.1128**, and that is not a repair but a different null: it deletes 63 days of the book's own order. **H_WRAP, H_DRIFT and H_MARGINAL all REFUTED; H_BOOK SUPPORTED — R_PERM, which preserves the observed multiset EXACTLY and destroys only order, still sits above the observed at 36 of 36.**

  **BUT THE RESIDUAL IS NOT THE RECOVERY / MEAN-REVERSION STORY EITHER.** An MA(1) filter carrying the book's **OWN** lag-1 autocorrelation (`S_MA1`) closes **0.278** of the S_PERM (1.0054) → REAL (0.9415) distance. **H_AC1 REFUTED.** The structure that shortens these drawdowns lives at **quarter horizons** — VR(63) 0.53–0.57 against VR(21) 0.67–0.91 — which is exactly why L = 63 → 1 is the one variant that moves anything. **And the sign is the opposite of the naive story:** the LEAST mean-reverting panel carries the LARGEST gap (SMALL ac1 −0.0301 / gap −0.1062; B136 −0.0378 / −0.0861; U56 −0.0501 / −0.0605). That is idea 1162's `H_MEANREV` refutation seen from the other side. **n = 3 distinct books, published as three, and NO rank correlation is reported** — all four anchor rows per panel are the same book, so a correlation over the 12 would be 3 points counted 4 times each.

  **WHAT A PUBLISHED BAND ACTUALLY NEEDS, AND IT IS OBJECT-DEPENDENT.** Machinery floor **OBJ_MAXDD 1.0054** — a LEVEL needs **no** correction and the whole ~6% gap is a fact about the book — against **OBJ_SPREAD 0.9505**, a genuine ~5% machinery bias, and OBJ_ARGMAX 0.9796. **H_OBJECT SUPPORTED.** The spread bias is **idea 1148's count inflation showing up INSIDE the null**, where nobody had checked it: a max-minus-min over k rungs of a *resampled* ladder grows with k too. **H_BAND REFUTED** and worth saying plainly: R_MACH does not improve coverage at all (inside-90% **0.9630 → 0.9630**), because the record's bands were never failing to CONTAIN the observed value — they were mis-centred, and a 1% recentring changes no verdict. Two dials (null type x correction) = **15 cells x 36 = 540 rows, all published**.

  **TWO DEFECTS IN THE PARENT, FOUND WHILE REBUILDING IT, REPORTED AND NOT ABSORBED.** (i) The queue publishes 1159's ratio triple as a bare *"0.936 / 0.851 / 0.964 by null type"* with **no labels**; only 1159's own CSV says which belongs to which (N_BLOCK 0.9358 / N_IID 0.8507 / N_STAT 0.9641), and the two readings are **0.085 apart on a 0.15 effect**. The first cut of this script mapped them in the other order and **gate G9c failed at 8.527e-02** — the gate is the only reason the error did not propagate. (ii) **Every ladder's ANCHOR rung is the SAME book** (N = 20, H = 126, gross 0.75, weekly; gate G12, observed identical at 0.000e+00), so OBJ_MAXDD's 36 cells are **9 distinct (panel x null) measurements replicated four times**, differing only by the null's seed. On distinct cells the parent's headline reads **62 of 81 (0.765)**, not 89 of 108 (0.824). The four replicates agree at **9 of 9** and the replicate spread of the null MEDIAN is **0.0182 (median) / 0.0256 (max)** against a headline gap of **0.0585**, so the "36 of 36" is **not** a seed artefact — but it is **9 measurements, not 36**, and both readings are quoted throughout.

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS.** 72 books per panel (N x H x cadence), **216 in all, every one published**; parameters chosen on 2009–2016 ONLY and read once on 2017–2026. **4b full 12 of 216, 4b OOS 13 of 216, 4a 0 of 216** (U56 11/11, B136 1/2, SMALL 0/0). Best 4b book (full AND OOS) `U56 / weekly / N = 12 / H = 126`: full **17.65% / 1.1658 / −20.17%** (H1 1.2741 / H2 1.0833), OOS **18.78% / 1.1701 / −20.17%**; against SPY full 15.06% / 0.8814 / −33.72% and OOS 15.15% / 0.8684 / −33.72%, and live RULES v2 8.60% / 1.1980 / −12.05% (OOS 9.42% / 1.2714 / −12.05%). **Memo written with exact RULES wording and recommending PARK**: it clears 4b's drawdown cap (0.60 x 33.72% = **20.232%**) by **0.06 pp**, its immediate neighbour `N = 15` has a **higher** OOS Sharpe (**1.1869**) and **fails** at 20.97%, **0 of 180 picks reach it**, and it fails 4a.

  **THE CAPITAL CONTENT, AT ITS TRUE WEIGHT.** `CH_NULLDD` uses the null itself as the selector. The correction moves the pick at **9 of 9 (panel, null) cells — but 3 of 9 once `R_PIVOT` is set aside**, and R_PIVOT is a *reflection*, not a repair: it maps the null through the observed value, turning "the null sits above" into "the null sits below" by construction without pricing why. Mean OOS Sharpe of the pick: **R_NONE 0.7110, R_MACH 0.7110, R_CENTER 0.7241, R_PERM 0.7471** (R_PIVOT 0.9103, n = 9, a curiosity). **H_CAPITAL is recorded SUPPORTED against its declared bar and reported as CAPITAL-IRRELEVANT** — the bar was written before the repair/reflection distinction was visible and this run does not move a bar after seeing the result. Picks clearing 4b full AND OOS 27 of 180, **4a 0 of 180**; by chooser CH_ISCAGR 0 of 45, CH_ISSHARPE 0 of 45, **CH_ISDD 15 of 45**, CH_NULLDD 12 of 45 — a plain IS drawdown chooser reaches a 4b passer more often than the null-normalised one.

  **NO PROTOCOL CLAUSE PROPOSED.** The obvious one — require a max-minus-min null to be count-matched — is worth having, but this run priced one null family on one book family, and rule 6 reserves every PROTOCOL edit to a Sunday review in any case.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56, B136 and SMALL are current-constituent lists; SMALL is the current constituents of a sub-$2B screen, **663 columns after dropping the 52 tickers with `max_1d_move >= 1.0` from `data/small_meta.csv`** (tape 2010-01-04 → 2026-09-11). It bears on this run's headline **directly and in the direction of the finding**: a survivor-only panel has shallower observed drawdowns, which pushes the observed value further BELOW its own null. **The Arm C machinery floor is immune** (a ratio read on the same panel, so the bias divides out); **the residual book gap is NOT**, an unknown part of the ~6% is survivorship, and this run cannot separate them without a point-in-time panel. The comparative conclusion — machinery ≈ 1.00 on a level, 0.95 on a spread — survives regardless, because both are read on the same tapes.

  **15 of 15 gates PASS**, including the fast runner ≡ `engine.backtest` at **1.388e-17**, live RULES v2 MaxDD ≡ −12.05% at 4.949e-05, SPY OOS at 2.894e-03, SMALL determinism 0.000e+00, **`N_BLOCK` at L = 1 ≡ `N_IID` bit for bit (1162's identity, 0.000e+00)**, **`perm_index` rows exact permutations of 0..T−1 for all three nulls (0.000e+00)**, **R_CENTER forcing every draw's total log return onto the observed (0.000e+00)**, `boot_maxdd` on the identity index ≡ the observed |MaxDD| (1.421e-14), and — the two the whole run turns on — **`R_NONE` reproducing 1159's committed OBSERVED values and NULL MEDIANS on 1159's own seeds at 3.553e-15 (G9, G10)**, so every correction is a strict re-processing of the record's own draws and not a different experiment. G2 carries 1163's price-vintage defect openly (this run 0.155520/1.138079/−0.191276 against the committed 0.155787/1.139701/−0.191276, bar declared at 5e-3 and the deviation printed). **HYPOTHESES 3 of 9 SUPPORTED.** Script `research/backtests/2026-09-17_why-does-a-RESAMPLE-NULL-sit-ABOVE-the-OBSERVED-MAXIMUM-at-108-of-108-cells_B.py`, 13 CSVs, console log, result memo, PARK memo, 6 LEADERBOARD rows. **NOTHING ENACTED**; RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.

## 2026-09-17 — idea 1158 (lane B): should a REGIME-to-LENGTH RATIO fix its FRACTION LADDER or count-match its BETWEEN TERM?

  **ANSWERED: NEITHER AS A MEASUREMENT — FREEZE, BECAUSE COUNT-MATCHING PROVABLY CANNOT.**

  **THE DECISIVE ARM HAS NO DATA IN IT.** Gate G11 evaluates each candidate between term on an **exactly log-linear m[f]**, where the right answer is known and identical at every fraction ladder. Over the run's five ladders the between term's max/min is **endpoint range 2.2623x, ladder-SD 1.5298x, COUNT-MATCHED 1.4202x, span-normalised EXACTLY 1.0000x (6.66e-16)**. The queue's repair 2 shrinks 1148's defect by a third and **cannot remove it at any sample size**: a max-minus-min over k ladder points and a mean over its k(k−1)/2 pairs both scale with the ladder's **LOG-SPAN**, and that is the quantity neither named repair divides out. Repair 1 — freezing the ladder in the clause — is comparable **by fiat**, which this run states at every occurrence (gate G9, 0.000e+00) and never counts as evidence.

  **ON THE OBJECT THE RECORD ACTUALLY COMMITS, NOT A CONVENIENT ONE.** 1148's and 1157's cells are a POOLED construction — the per-part median across the rungs of each of the four rung ladders as the within groups, the median over every (rung, part) value as the level. The first cut of this script priced the repairs on a per-book ratio instead, **and gate G7 caught it**. The pooled replica is gated to 1157's own committed `cells.csv`. Median ladder sensitivity / verdict-flip rate, pooled (216 cells): **P_RAW 2.0841 / 0.1759, P_CMATCH 1.8612 / 0.0556, P_CMNORM 2.0174 / 0.0509, P_ELAST 1.9801 / 0.0370**, P_FREEZE 1.0000 / 0.0000 by construction. The independent per-book reading (2,916 cells) gives 2.5232 / 1.7334 / 1.8630 / 4.2004 and is published beside it. **H_CMATCH REFUTED and H_NORM REFUTED** against their declared 1.10 / 0.05 bars. **145,800 per-book and 10,800 pooled ratio readings, every one published**; 2 tuned dials (repair × fraction ladder) = 25 combinations, all reported.

  **WHY EVEN THE EXACTLY-INVARIANT ESTIMATORS FAIL ON DATA, AND WHERE THEY DO NOT.** **H_CURVE REFUTED**: the pooled median R² of m[f] on log(length) is **0.6959** against a declared 0.80, and by statistic **SHARPE 0.1137, CAGR 0.3421, VOL 0.6467** against **MAXDD 0.9284, ULCER 0.8509**. For the moment statistics there is **no length effect to normalise** — the ratio is noise over noise whatever the between term, and H_SPAN is REFUTED (0.1806 of cells predicted within 10%) for the same reason. **Where a real, log-linear length effect exists the repair works and works well**: pooled MAXDD LSENS **1.997 → 1.295 (count-matched) → 1.182 (span-normalised) → 1.168 (elasticity)**, ULCER 3.157 → 1.648 → 1.321. **The clause is a PATH-STATISTIC clause or it is nothing** — which is exactly the statistic 1148's own sub-1 headline cell was about.

  **THE DEFECT ITSELF IS REAL, AND FREEZING IS NOT FREE.** **H_DEFECT SUPPORTED** — the pooled ratio falls **0.5547x (U56) / 0.4894x (B136) / 0.5905x (SMALL)** from F_1140 to F_FINER, reproducing 1157's committed 0.39x–0.59x. **H_BETWEEN SUPPORTED** — median LSENS between **2.2812** against within **1.0167**. **H_NOISE SUPPORTED** — a 60-draw moving-block bootstrap with ONE COMMON index across all 27 rung books puts the finer reading outside the marginal 5–95% band at **4 of 6** cells, and the finer ladder is lower on the SAME resampled tape at ≥0.95 of draws in **5 of 6**. **H_CALIB SUPPORTED** — on an S_PERM tape (one common permutation, every regime ordering destroyed) the repair ranking by LSENS is **identical**, so ladder-stability is a property of the ESTIMATOR and not of this tape. **What freezing costs**: against the count-matched reading on the finest ladder the frozen number restates **0.6782 of 432 pooled cells by more than 25%** and flips the sub-1 verdict at 0.0532.

  **A DEFECT THE GATES FOUND, LABELLED EVERYWHERE AS NOT PRE-DECLARED.** G7 failed on its first run because the committed price cache has **gained one trading day** since 1157 (U56 4,705 rows to 2026-09-15 → 4,706 to 2026-09-16); B136, SMALL and every T_MATCHED panel are unchanged row for row. On the moved panel the record's own committed ratio shifts by a **median 0.0104 and a maximum 0.7762** in relative terms, with **0.1944 of the 72 deterministic cells moving more than 10%**, and **putting the single day back removes 97.57% of the deviation** (G7c). The mechanism is plain: every sub-tape boundary is `n // f`, so one extra row re-cuts every stretch on every rung of the rung ladder and the fraction ladder at once. **Before any ladder or between-term question arises, two runs of this object are not comparable unless both state the tape's LAST DATE and ROW COUNT** — a cheaper and stricter requirement than either repair the queue named. **CENSUS**: 5,975 committed CSVs scanned by header, **164 carry a sub-tape fraction column over 331,218 committed rows**, 120 carry a within/between/ratio column; of **90 committed prose sentences** naming a sub-tape or regime-to-length object, only **30 (0.3333)** name a fraction ladder at all and none names a row count.

  **THE GATE BAR, AND WHY IT IS NOT ZERO.** G2 measures a **price-ADJUSTMENT drift of 1.62e-03** in the book's own CAGR between 1157's cache vintage and today's — the same nominal dates, re-adjusted closes (this run 0.155520/1.138079/−0.191276 against the committed 0.155787/1.139701/−0.191276). A ratio derived from those returns cannot reproduce to machine precision on any tape, so a 1e-9 bar would gate the data vintage rather than this run's arithmetic. G7/G8 are set at **1e-3 relative — three significant figures, tighter than the drift measured in the input** — and read **2.38e-05 / 4.64e-05**. R_MATCHED, which carries 1148's own SAMPLED 200-pair spread, is gated separately at 1.68e-01 (G7b); the two headline gates use the two **deterministic** within-definitions, where no sampler enters.

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS.** 162 books and **264 chooser picks, all published**; parameters chosen on 2009–2016 ONLY and read once on 2017–2026. **4b full 31 of 162, 4b OOS 32 of 162, 4a 0 of 162**; 33 of 264 picks clear 4b full and **4a 0 of 264**. Best 4b book (full AND OOS) `T_MATCHED / U56 / CADENCE = Q`: full **14.33% / 1.0693 / −19.94%** (H1 1.0456 / H2 1.1043), OOS **17.38% / 1.1781 / −19.94%**; against SPY full 15.06% / 0.8814 / −33.72% and OOS 15.15% / 0.8684 / −33.72%, and live RULES v2 8.60% / 1.1980 / −12.05% (OOS 9.42% / 1.2714 / −12.05%). **Memo written with exact RULES wording and recommending PARK**: it clears 4b's drawdown cap (0.60 × 33.72% = **20.232%**) by **0.29 pp**, fails 4a on all three legs, is **0 of 11 choosers reachable on its own family** (every chooser picks D, W or M, never Q), and is a quarterly-cadence rung the record has priced many times.

  **THE CAPITAL CONTENT, AT ITS TRUE WEIGHT.** `CH_RATIO` uses the repaired ratio as the selector, the only route by which a fraction ladder could touch capital. **H_CAPITAL REFUTED**: every CH_RATIO mean OOS Sharpe lies between **0.8146 and 0.8632** against a plain IS-Sharpe chooser's **0.8534**, a maximum move of **0.0486** against the declared 0.05 bar. The repair does move the PICK — 18 or 19 of 24 families on the Sharpe ratio — and buys **nothing** for it. **KILL as a capital finding.**

  **NO PROTOCOL CLAUSE PROPOSED.** The memo drafts the wording a Sunday review would need, and recommends against enacting it: rule 6 reserves every PROTOCOL edit to a Sunday review, and this run priced one ratio family on one book family.

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B136 are current-constituent lists; SMALL is the current constituents of a sub-$2B screen, **663 columns after dropping the 52 tickers with `max_1d_move >= 1.0` from `data/small_meta.csv`** (tape 2010-01-04 → 2026-09-11). Every delisted, zeroed or screened-out name is absent, so every drawdown here is the shallowest the period could have produced. Every headline in this run is a **ratio of ratios read on one tape** (LSENS = R at one ladder over R at another), so the bias very largely divides out of it; it does **not** divide out of the 4b legs, measured against SPY, a real index, so every 4b pass counted above is an **UPPER bound**.

  **14 of 14 gates PASS, HYPOTHESES 5 of 10 SUPPORTED.** Script `research/backtests/2026-09-17_should-a-REGIME-to-LENGTH-RATIO-FIX-its-FRACTION-LADDER-or-COUNT-MATCH-its-BETWEEN-TERM_B.py`, 19 CSVs, console log, PARK memo, 7 LEADERBOARD rows. **NOTHING ENACTED**; RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched.

## 2026-09-17 — RECORD REPAIR (lane B, while resolving the idea-1158 lane collision)

  Commit `3d056ec` (lane cloud, idea 1158) **replaced `research/CHANGELOG.md` wholesale** instead of appending to it: the file went from 1,099 lines to 22, dropping every entry before it. The loss was caught when this lane's rebase conflicted on the same file. `research/CHANGELOG.md` has been rebuilt here as **merge base `405a5b7` + lane B's idea-1158 entry + lane cloud's idea-1158 entry, in that order**, so the full history and BOTH runs' entries are present and nothing of `3d056ec`'s own text is discarded. No other file was affected — `LEADERBOARD.md` (+5) and `QUEUE.md` (+5/-1) were clean appends in that commit. This is a second symptom of defect 932 (two lanes claiming one idea in the same hour) and is recorded here so the next lane can see it happened.

  Lane cloud's idea-1158 entry, preserved verbatim from `3d056ec`, follows.

- 2026-09-17 (lane cloud, idea 1158 should-a-REGIME-to-LENGTH-RATIO-FIX-its-FRACTION-LADDER-or-COUNT-MATCH-its-BETWEEN-TERM) — **ANSWERED = NEITHER, AND THE TWO REPAIRS FIX DIFFERENT THINGS.** No RULES change, no book promoted, no PROTOCOL edit (rule 6); RULES.md, PROTOCOL.md, engine.py, scan.py, bot.py and baseline.py untouched. SELECTION: this lane takes the LAST eligible open idea; 1158 ended '## Open' and is not EDGAR / Form 4 / 8-K / options / spin-off / live-data. Price leg of 81 rung books (78 distinct) and 27 rule-8 picks, so it carries this run's mandatory rule-8 walk-forward and both KEEP paths.

  **THE TWO DIALS AND NO MORE (rule 4, and the queue names both):** `REPAIR` {R_ASIS, R_FROZEN, R_COUNT} x `FRACTION LADDER` {L2, L3, L4, L6, L8} = **15 cells, EVERY ONE PUBLISHED** in `.grid.csv` at every (panel, statistic, construction, partition), 1,080 rows. NOT dials: PANEL {U56, B136, SMALL}; the four DIAL LADDERS that are the ratio's groups (CADENCE, GROSS, H, N = 27 rung books per panel, 1148's grid); STATS {CAGR, VOL, SHARPE, MAXDD, ULCER, CALMAR} (headline MAXDD); PARTITIONS {ALIGNED, OFFSET}; CONSTRUCTIONS {C_POOLED, C_BOOK}; WITHIN terms {R_SPREAD, R_SD, R_MATCHED} (headline R_MATCHED); the jackknife; 3 rule-8 choosers. Tape PINNED at 2026-09-15, the vintage 1148/1157's committed anchors saw.

  **THE ANSWER, SCORED IN THE DECLARED ORDER: (D) NEITHER — because the queue's two candidate repairs are answers to two DIFFERENT questions.** `R_FROZEN` buys COMPARABILITY (**18 of 18** (panel, stat) cells, by construction) and pays for it with the LEVEL and the RESOLUTION: it **misses the resolved value at 11 of 18 cells** (median relative gap **0.3221**), including **all three** cells of the record's headline statistic, and its leave-one-ladder-out jackknife SE of log(ratio) is **0.6723 against R_COUNT-at-L8's 0.1722 — 3.9x wider**. `R_COUNT` is **not a comparability repair at all**: comparable at **2 of 18**, median max/min **2.083x** over the five ladders, worst **17.246x** — removing the endpoint inflation does not stop the medians moving with the ladder — **but it IS a resolution repair (SE ratio 0.2561).** `R_ASIS`, the record's as-committed between term, is comparable at **0 of 18**, median **3.715x**, worst **77.956x**. **The ladder is not only a comparability dial, it is a RESOLUTION dial, and freezing it spends resolution to buy comparability. That is the reading the queue's framing does not anticipate.**

  **THE COMBINATION IS THE CLAUSE THE HEADLINE STATISTIC NEEDS AND ONLY THAT ONE.** `R_BOTH` (frozen ladder AND count-matched between term) is computed and published because the pre-declared scoring lands on (D) and a reader will ask; it is **NOT a fourth dial value and is never selected on**. Comparable 18 of 18 by construction, and it **lands on the resolved value at all three MAXDD cells at gaps 0.0253 / 0.0886 / 0.0151** — an order of magnitude better than freezing alone — **but at only 4 of 18 cells overall, median gap 0.3843, WORSE than R_FROZEN's 0.3221**, because on ratio-valued statistics (SHARPE, CALMAR) the between term passes near zero and both repairs blow up together. **The three MAXDD rows are its best three, not a representative sample, and are not quoted alone.** Filed as 1187.

  **A CORRECTION TO 1148's OWN COMMITTED HEADLINE: its SMALL/MAXDD 0.794x is the FROZEN 3-rung reading; the resolved one on its own finer ladder is 1.174x — the record's number is 32% low against data the record itself later collected.** Filed as 1188.

  **THE CENSUS — THE RECORD CANNOT AUDIT THIS RETROSPECTIVELY.** **26 committed CSVs in research/backtests carry a sub-tape FRACTION column, 42,170 rows in total, and only 4 (0.1538) also publish the LADDER they walked.** Six distinct committed ladder labels exist and the max fraction actually walked runs 0..8 across 21 files. **PROSE: 66 committed sentences assert a regime-to-length reading; 0.1061 state a ladder and 0.2576 quote a number.** **On the two ladders the record ACTUALLY walked — L3 (1140/1148's) and L8 (1157's), both committed — R_ASIS disagrees with itself by a median 1.736x and up to 7.233x (within 1.25 at 3 of 18), R_COUNT by 1.559x (worst 12.675x, 4 of 18), R_FROZEN by 1.000x (18 of 18). Only freezing makes two committed runs comparable, and it does so by refusing to read the finer ladder at all.**

  **GATES 10 of 10 PASS, AND TWO ARE EXACT REPLAYS OF THE RECORD'S OWN NUMBERS.** **G6: 1148/1157's SMALL/MAXDD/R_MATCHED/F_1140 replayed BIT FOR BIT — 0.794259 against the committed 0.794259** (their `ratio_from` verbatim, their Monte-Carlo pair statistic, their seed, their loop order). **G9: 1157's premise REPRODUCED INDEPENDENTLY — R_ASIS(L8)/R_ASIS(L3) = 0.5615x / 0.5148x / 0.5916x against their committed 0.39x-0.59x.** G7 the EXACT all-pairs statistic recovers 0.794259 at 1.89e-07 (the departure removes noise, not signal). **G8 R_FROZEN's ladder-invariance is PROVED at 0.00e+00, not asserted**, because the comparability claim for that repair is definitional. G1 1.39e-17, G5 1157's committed MaxDD levels 7.17e-06, G10 determinism 0.00e+00. **THE VINTAGE, PUBLISHED NOT ABSORBED: G2/G3 read 4.12e-05 / 1.70e-04 on the PINNED tape against 1.62e-03 / 2.89e-03 unpinned** (idea 1163's defect). **NO BOOTSTRAP, AND THE REASON IS DECLARED UP FRONT: a moving-block resample destroys the very object a regime-to-length ratio measures — it would leave the within term almost unchanged while making the between term meaningless, i.e. manufacture this run's own answer — so the resolution measure is a LEAVE-ONE-LADDER-OUT JACKKNIFE over the ratio's own four groups. A stated departure from 1157, not a quiet substitution.**

  **RULE 8 AND BOTH KEEP PATHS — 81 RUNG BOOKS (78 DISTINCT), ALL PUBLISHED.** Benchmarks (pinned): **U56 SPY 15.10% / 0.8829 / -33.72% (halves 0.9588/0.8207), OOS 15.21% / 0.8711; U56 RULES v2 (live) @10 bps 8.62% / 1.2008 / -12.05%, OOS 9.46% / 1.2763; B136 SPY 15.16% / 0.8861 / -33.72%, OOS 15.33% / 0.8767; B136 LIVE 7.98% / 1.0993 / -12.24%, OOS 7.88% / 1.1059; SMALL SPY 14.06% / 0.8581 / -33.72%, OOS 15.33% / 0.8767; SMALL LIVE 4.30% / 0.6637 / -13.89%, OOS 3.75% / 0.5600.** Base rates at 10 bps: **4b full 16 of 81, 4b OOS 17, BOTH 15, 4a 0 of 81** (U56 10/27, B136 6/27, **SMALL 0 of 27 on every path**). **NO NEW CANDIDATE AND THE REASON IS ARITHMETIC: 11 of the 16 passers are the standing incumbent read at a LOWER GROSS RUNG, and their Sharpe runs 1.1389 / 1.1391 / 1.1392 / 1.1394 / 1.1396 / 1.1397 across gross 0.50 -> 0.75 — a spread of 8e-04 over a 1.5x change in gross. That is idea 1177's degenerate Sharpe-vs-gross ladder arriving from a third direction, a cross-run confirmation and not a discovery.** Filed as 1189. The rest are prior art (U56 N=12, B136 N=15, B136 N=10). The one uncommitted cell, **U56 CADENCE=Q (15.40% / 1.1389 / -19.94%, OOS 17.28% / 1.1727)**, is **dominated by the incumbent on every full-sample axis and ahead only out of sample — preferring it would be selecting on the OOS window, which rule 8 exists to forbid, so it is recorded and NOT promoted. No memo.** **THE FREE PARAMETER HAS CAPITAL REACH AND NO CAPITAL VALUE: the repair MOVES the pick at 5 of 6 (panel, ratio-chooser) cells, yet 0 of 27 picks clear 4a and exactly 1 of 27 clears 4b — and that one is U56 / R_FROZEN / CH_HIGHRATIO landing on CADENCE=W, the standing incumbent, reached by the FALSIFICATION CONTROL under the repair that discards data.** CH_LOWRATIO, the reading the record's language implies a run should prefer, loses to CH_ISSHARPE on OOS Sharpe at B136 and SMALL under every repair.

  **PROTOCOL CLAUSE PROPOSED NOT ENACTED (rule 6):** *"no sub-tape ratio without its fraction ladder" — any committed sentence quoting a regime-to-length, within-to-between or sub-tape spread ratio SHALL name the fraction ladder it was computed on and state whether its between term is an endpoint range or a count-matched pairwise mean; a ratio quoted without both is not comparable to any other run's.*

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B136 are CURRENT-CONSTITUENT lists; SMALL is the current output of a sub-$2B screen less the documented `max_1d_move >= 1.0` exclusion (52 of 715 dropped, 663 names plus SPY as benchmark). Every LEVEL is optimistic and every 4a/4b count is an UPPER bound. A RATIO of two spreads in the statistic's own units is far less exposed but **not immune** — a survivorship-flattered panel has a shallower drawdown path and MAXDD is this family's headline statistic — so the levels are published beside every ratio.

  **KILL as a capital finding.** Script `research/backtests/2026-09-17_should-a-REGIME-to-LENGTH-RATIO-FIX-its-FRACTION-LADDER-or-COUNT-MATCH-its-BETWEEN-TERM_cloud.py`, 9 CSVs, console log, result note, 5 LEADERBOARD rows. Follow-ups filed 1187 (does a sub-tape ratio need a statistic class before a clause can be written), 1188 (how many committed sub-tape figures move once the ladder is resolved rather than frozen) and 1189 (is the gross-rung degeneracy of Sharpe universal).

## 2026-09-17 — Research cloud, idea 1199: should a SATURATED PERCENTILE be PUBLISHABLE as a POINT at all?

**ANSWERED = NO, AND THE COST IS NOT RHETORICAL: THE POINT FORM'S ARGMAX IS ITS TIE-BREAK.** Two dials and no more (CLAIM SET {C_STRICT, C_PROX, C_ALL}, 1197's harvester verbatim; BOUND FORM {B_POINT, B_ONE, B_TWO, B_CP}), every rung published at every cell, nothing selected on. **DATA-FREE FIRST:** a percentile over K draws lives on {0, 1/K, ..., 1}, so at K=10 the point form prints 1.000 where the one-sided 95% Clopper-Pearson statement is **0.741134** — the point **OVERSTATES the K=10 evidence by 0.259 in the statistic's own units**, and 1197 found 0.963-1.000 of U56/B136 cells sitting there. **CENSUS of 32,887 committed text units:** C_STRICT **104** / 26 ceiling-quoted / **10 FLOOR-quoted** / 40 state K / **23 ADJUDICATED AT THE CEILING**; of 31 saturated units only **21 are re-expressible at all** because **0.3226 state no draw count** and a bound form is a function of K — a loss the point form **hid rather than avoided**. **PRICE ARM:** 36 books x 3 panels, 14,400 gross-matched nulls, nested K ladder. The point form separates 12 books into **1.75 (U56) / 1.40 (B136) distinct values at K=10** with **10.95 / 11.60 of 12 tied at its maximum**, against **CH_Z at 12.00 of 12 at every one of 360 cells**; SMALL de-saturates to 0.000 at K=400, so **saturation is a statistic-meets-easy-panel property, not a machinery one**. **RULE 8 (chosen on 2009-2016 only, 2017-2026 read once, 20 seeds x 18 (panel,K) cells): B_POINT 0.8897 mean OOS Sharpe and 0 of 18 4b-clearing picks; B_ONE = B_TWO = B_CP 0.9568 (+0.0671) and 7 of 18.** **THE THREE BOUND FORMS ARE DECISION-IDENTICAL TO SIX DECIMALS AT ALL 54 CELLS — every basis point comes from DECLARING THE TIE, none from how the bound is written.** **MECHANISM: B_POINT lands on N=5/M, the FIRST cell in the sort order, at 6 of 6 U56 and 5 of 6 B136 rungs — a saturated point percentile does not choose, the alphabet does, silently** — and carries OOS MaxDD -28.7% against the bound form's -19.7%.

  **KEEP paths on all 36 books: 4a 0 of 36; 4b full 6, 4b OOS 7, BOTH 6** (U56 5/12, B136 1/12, **SMALL 0/12 on every path**). **NO NEW CANDIDATE AND THE REASON IS THE RECORD'S OWN: U56 N=20/W IS the standing 2026-09-04 incumbent, and the five U56 passers span Sharpe 1.1520 -> 1.2111 over an N ladder from 15 to 40 — 0.059 across a 2.7x change in holding count, with the walk-forward's reach (N=15/W, OOS 17.58% / 1.1795 / -19.69%) ahead of the incumbent by 0.0130 of full Sharpe and 0.0090 of OOS Sharpe. That is idea 1189's degenerate-ladder reading arriving from a fourth direction, a cross-run confirmation and not a discovery; preferring it would be selecting inside a ladder this record has already measured flat. RECORDED AND NOT PROMOTED. No memo.**

  **PROTOCOL CLAUSE PROPOSED NOT ENACTED (rule 6):** *"no saturated percentile as a point" — any committed sentence quoting a null percentile at an attainable extreme (>= 1 - 1/(K+1) or <= 1/(K+1)) SHALL publish it as a BOUND carrying its own draw count, and SHALL name the statistic used to break the resulting tie. A ceiling percentile quoted as a point, or quoted without K, is not an adjudication and may not be cited as one.*

  **SURVIVORSHIP (rule 9).** U56 and B136 are CURRENT-CONSTITUENT lists; SMALL is the current output of a sub-$2B screen less the documented `max_1d_move >= 1.0` exclusion (52 of 715 dropped, 663 names plus SPY as benchmark only). Every LEVEL is optimistic and every 4a/4b count is an UPPER bound. The Arm C **comparison** runs two choosers over the same book population and is far less exposed, but the levels are published beside it.
